Abstract: Path generation, the problem of producing smooth, executable paths from discrete planning outputs, such as waypoint sequences, is a fundamental step in the control of autonomous robots, industrial robots, and CNC machines, as path following and trajectory tracking controllers impose strict differentiability requirements on their reference inputs to guarantee stability and convergence, particularly for nonholonomic systems. Mollification has been recently proposed as a computationally efficient and analytically tractable tool for path generation, offering formal smoothness and curvature guarantees with advantages over spline interpolation and optimization-based methods. However, this mollification is subject to a fundamental geometric constraint: the smoothed path is confined within the convex hull of the original path, precluding exact waypoint interpolation, even when explicitly required by mission specifications or upstream planners. We introduce directional mollification, a novel operator that resolves this limitation while retaining the analytical tractability of classical mollification. The proposed operator generates infinitely differentiable paths that strictly interpolate prescribed waypoints, converge to the original non-differentiable input with arbitrary precision, and satisfy explicit curvature bounds given by a closed-form expression, addressing the core requirements of path generation for controlled autonomous systems. We further establish a parametric family of path generation operators that contains both classical and directional mollification as special cases, providing a unifying theoretical framework for the systematic generation of smooth, feasible paths from non-differentiable planning outputs.

</details>


### [39] [Optimal Solutions for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles via Lazy Branch and Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21880)
*Anoop Bhat,Geordan Gutow,Surya Singh,Zhongqiang Ren,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Lazy BPRC算法解决带障碍物的移动目标车辆路径问题，通过延迟计算和连续性松弛将运行速度提升一个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MT-VRP-O问题的传统方法计算成本高，因为需要在移动目标之间进行无碰撞运动规划，计算每个路径的真实成本非常耗时

Method: 提出Lazy BPRC算法，采用分支定价框架，但延迟计算真实成本：1）使用松弛连续性约束计算成本下界；2）按需懒评估真实成本；3）在凸集图上搜索最短路径；4）用连续性松弛方法加速搜索

Result: Lazy BPRC算法比两种消融实验运行速度快一个数量级，能够高效找到MT-VRP-O问题的最优解

Conclusion: 通过延迟成本计算和连续性松弛，Lazy BPRC算法显著提高了MT-VRP-O问题的求解效率，为复杂动态环境下的多智能体路径规划提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: The Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles (MT-VRP-O) seeks trajectories for several agents that collectively intercept a set of moving targets. Each target has one or more time windows where it must be visited, and the agents must avoid static obstacles and satisfy speed and capacity constraints. We introduce Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC), which finds optimal solutions for the MT-VRP-O. Lazy BPRC applies the branch-and-price framework for VRPs, which alternates between a restricted master problem (RMP) and a pricing problem. The RMP aims to select a sequence of target-time window pairings (called a tour) for each agent to follow, from a limited subset of tours. The pricing problem adds tours to the limited subset. Conventionally, solving the RMP requires computing the cost for an agent to follow each tour in the limited subset. Computing these costs in the MT-VRP-O is computationally intensive, since it requires collision-free motion planning between moving targets. Lazy BPRC defers cost computations by solving the RMP using lower bounds on the costs of each tour, computed via motion planning with relaxed continuity constraints. We lazily evaluate the true costs of tours as-needed. We compute a tour's cost by searching for a shortest path on a Graph of Convex Sets (GCS), and we accelerate this search using our continuity relaxation method. We demonstrate that Lazy BPRC runs up to an order of magnitude faster than two ablations.

</details>


### [40] [Disengagement Analysis and Field Tests of a Prototypical Open-Source Level 4 Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21926)
*Marvin Seegert,Christian Oefinger,Korbinian Moller,Christoph Bank,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于Autoware的开源自动驾驶系统在236公里混合交通中的表现，通过五级关键性框架分析了30次脱离事件，发现空间脱离率为0.127次/公里，主要问题集中在感知和规划模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前专有自动驾驶系统主要通过脱离事件进行评估，但原型开源L4车辆在真实世界中的长距离运行能力尚未得到充分探索。研究旨在填补这一空白，评估开源自动驾驶软件在实际道路环境中的表现。

Method: 使用基于Autoware的软件栈的研究车辆，在混合交通中行驶236公里，通过26次行程收集数据。采用新颖的五级关键性框架对30次脱离事件进行分类分析，评估空间脱离率和干预模式。

Result: 空间脱离率为0.127次/公里，干预主要发生在低速接近静态物体和交通信号灯时。感知故障占40%，规划故障占26.7%，主要原因是物体跟踪丢失和停车车辆导致的运行死锁。安全驾驶员频繁进行不必要的干预，表明对系统缺乏信任。

Conclusion: 开源自动驾驶软件能够支持大规模运行，但脱离事件分析对于揭示标准指标遗漏的鲁棒性问题至关重要。研究强调了通过详细分析脱离事件来改进系统可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: Proprietary Autonomous Driving Systems are typically evaluated through disengagements, unplanned manual interventions to alter vehicle behavior, as annually reported by the California Department of Motor Vehicles. However, the real-world capabilities of prototypical open-source Level 4 vehicles over substantial distances remain largely unexplored. This study evaluates a research vehicle running an Autoware-based software stack across 236 km of mixed traffic. By classifying 30 disengagements across 26 rides with a novel five-level criticality framework, we observed a spatial disengagement rate of 0.127 1/km. Interventions predominantly occurred at lower speeds near static objects and traffic lights. Perception and Planning failures accounted for 40% and 26.7% of disengagements, respectively, largely due to object-tracking losses and operational deadlocks caused by parked vehicles. Frequent, unnecessary interventions highlighted a lack of trust on the part of the safety driver. These results show that while open-source software enables extensive operations, disengagement analysis is vital for uncovering robustness issues missed by standard metrics.

</details>


### [41] [VP-VLA: Visual Prompting as an Interface for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22003)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuxin Chen,Yuqi Liu,Jinhui Ye,Pengguang Chen,Changsheng Lu,Shu Liu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VP-VLA是一个双系统视觉-语言-动作框架，通过结构化视觉提示解耦高层推理和低层执行，提高机器人任务的空间精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型将视觉观察和语言指令直接映射到控制信号，这种"黑箱"映射要求单次前向传播同时处理指令解释、空间定位和低层控制，导致空间精度差且在分布外场景中鲁棒性有限。

Method: 提出VP-VLA双系统框架：1) System 2规划器将复杂指令分解为子任务，识别目标物体和位置；2) 将这些空间锚点作为结构化视觉提示（如十字准线和边界框）叠加到视觉观察上；3) System 1控制器在训练时通过辅助视觉定位目标生成精确的低层执行动作。

Result: 在Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop基准测试和SimplerEnv仿真中，VP-VLA分别将成功率提高了5%和8.3%，超越了QwenOFT和GR00T-N1.6等竞争基线。

Conclusion: 通过结构化视觉提示解耦高层推理和低层执行，VP-VLA显著提高了VLA模型的空间精度和鲁棒性，为机器人任务执行提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically map visual observations and linguistic instructions directly to robotic control signals. This "black-box" mapping forces a single forward pass to simultaneously handle instruction interpretation, spatial grounding, and low-level control, often leading to poor spatial precision and limited robustness in out-of-distribution scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose VP-VLA, a dual-system framework that decouples high-level reasoning and low-level execution via a structured visual prompting interface. Specifically, a "System 2 Planner" decomposes complex instructions into sub-tasks and identifies relevant target objects and goal locations. These spatial anchors are then overlaid directly onto visual observations as structured visual prompts, such as crosshairs and bounding boxes. Guided by these prompts and enhanced by a novel auxiliary visual grounding objective during training, a "System 1 Controller" reliably generates precise low-level execution motions. Experiments on the Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop benchmark and SimplerEnv simulation demonstrate that VP-VLA improves success rates by 5% and 8.3%, surpassing competitive baselines including QwenOFT and GR00T-N1.6.

</details>


### [42] [MEVIUS2: Practical Open-Source Quadruped Robot with Sheet Metal Welding and Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22031)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Shintaro Inoue,Temma Suzuki,Jun Oda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了MEVIUS2开源四足机器人，尺寸与波士顿动力Spot相当，采用钣金焊接和金属加工技术实现大型耐用结构，集成LiDAR和高动态范围相机等传感器，具备环境感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源四足机器人大多基于3D打印设计，结构脆弱且尺寸较小；少数金属结构机器人也尺寸偏小且缺乏多模态感知传感器，实用性不足。需要开发大型、耐用且具备感知能力的开源四足机器人平台。

Method: 通过钣金焊接和金属加工技术构建机器人结构，减少零件数量；集成LiDAR和高动态范围相机等多模态传感器；所有结构部件可通过电商服务订购；硬件、软件和训练环境完全开源。

Result: 成功开发了MEVIUS2机器人，尺寸与波士顿动力Spot相当，结构耐用；实验验证了其在多种崎岖地形上的行走能力，并展示了环境感知功能；所有设计文件、代码和训练环境已开源。

Conclusion: MEVIUS2是一个实用的大型开源四足机器人平台，通过金属结构和多模态传感器集成，解决了现有开源四足机器人在尺寸、耐用性和感知能力方面的局限性，为机器人研究提供了更好的硬件基础。

Abstract: Various quadruped robots have been developed to date, and thanks to reinforcement learning, they are now capable of traversing diverse types of rough terrain. In parallel, there is a growing trend of releasing these robot designs as open-source, enabling researchers to freely build and modify robots themselves. However, most existing open-source quadruped robots have been designed with 3D printing in mind, resulting in structurally fragile systems that do not scale well in size, leading to the construction of relatively small robots. Although a few open-source quadruped robots constructed with metal components exist, they still tend to be small in size and lack multimodal sensors for perception, making them less practical. In this study, we developed MEVIUS2, an open-source quadruped robot with a size comparable to Boston Dynamics' Spot, whose structural components can all be ordered through e-commerce services. By leveraging sheet metal welding and metal machining, we achieved a large, highly durable body structure while reducing the number of individual parts. Furthermore, by integrating sensors such as LiDARs and a high dynamic range camera, the robot is capable of detailed perception of its surroundings, making it more practical than previous open-source quadruped robots. We experimentally validated that MEVIUS2 can traverse various types of rough terrain and demonstrated its environmental perception capabilities. All hardware, software, and training environments can be obtained from Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevius2.

</details>


### [43] [RAFL: Generalizable Sim-to-Real of Soft Robots with Residual Acceleration Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22039)
*Dong Heon Cho,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAFL框架，通过元素级残差加速度场增强基础模拟器，实现跨形状泛化，解决软机器人模拟中几何依赖效应和模拟到现实差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分模拟器在软机器人优化中存在模拟到现实差距问题，尤其在几何本身是设计变量时更严重。系统识别方法常将几何依赖效应误吸收为材料参数，而更复杂的本构模型计算成本过高。

Method: 提出残差加速度场学习框架，在基础模拟器上添加可转移的元素级校正动力学场。模型基于共享局部特征，与全局网格拓扑和离散化无关，通过可微分模拟器使用稀疏标记观测进行端到端训练。

Result: 在模拟到模拟和模拟到现实实验中，该方法在未见形态上实现一致的零样本改进，而系统识别方法常出现负迁移。框架支持持续细化，能在形态优化过程中累积模拟精度。

Conclusion: RAFL框架通过残差加速度场有效解决软机器人模拟中的几何依赖问题，实现跨形状泛化，为形态优化提供更准确的模拟基础。

Abstract: Differentiable simulators enable gradient-based optimization of soft robots over material parameters, control, and morphology, but accurately modeling real systems remains challenging due to the sim-to-real gap. This issue becomes more pronounced when geometry is itself a design variable. System identification reduces discrepancies by fitting global material parameters to data; however, when constitutive models are misspecified or observations are sparse, identified parameters often absorb geometry-dependent effects rather than reflect intrinsic material behavior. More expressive constitutive models can improve accuracy but substantially increase computational cost, limiting practicality.
  We propose a residual acceleration field learning (RAFL) framework that augments a base simulator with a transferable, element-level corrective dynamics field. Operating on shared local features, the model is agnostic to global mesh topology and discretization. Trained end-to-end through a differentiable simulator using sparse marker observations, the learned residual generalizes across shapes. In both sim-to-sim and sim-to-real experiments, our method achieves consistent zero-shot improvements on unseen morphologies, while system identification frequently exhibits negative transfer. The framework also supports continual refinement, enabling simulation accuracy to accumulate during morphology optimization.

</details>


### [44] [Programming Manufacturing Robots with Imperfect AI: LLMs as Tuning Experts for FDM Print Configuration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22118)
*Ekta U. Samani,Christopher G. Atkeson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化闭环方法，将LLM作为调优专家嵌入贝叶斯优化循环中，用于FDM 3D打印配置优化，相比单次AI推荐显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDM 3D打印中，打印配置对输出质量有重要影响，但新手用户通常依赖默认配置、试错法或通用AI模型推荐。这些方法虽然能完成打印，但无法可靠满足特定目标。专家通过迭代调优使用先前打印证据，但这一过程需要专业知识。

Method: 提出模块化闭环方法：将LLM作为调优专业知识源嵌入贝叶斯优化循环中。近似评估器对每个打印配置评分并返回结构化诊断，LLM使用这些诊断提出自然语言调整建议，这些建议被编译成机器可执行的优化指导。

Result: 在100个Thingi10k部件测试中，LLM引导的循环在78%的对象上实现了最佳配置，且0%可能出现故障的情况；而单次AI模型推荐很少达到最佳，且有15%可能出现故障的情况。

Conclusion: LLM作为约束决策模块在证据驱动的优化循环中比作为端到端的打印配置选择预言机提供更多价值。这一结果有望扩展到更广泛的基于LLM的机器人编程领域。

Abstract: We use fused deposition modeling (FDM) 3D printing as a case study of how manufacturing robots can use imperfect AI to acquire process expertise. In FDM, print configuration strongly affects output quality. Yet, novice users typically rely on default configurations, trial-and-error, or recommendations from generic AI models (e.g., ChatGPT). These strategies can produce complete prints, but they do not reliably meet specific objectives. Experts iteratively tune print configurations using evidence from prior prints. We present a modular closed-loop approach that treats an LLM as a source of tuning expertise. We embed this source of expertise within a Bayesian optimization loop. An approximate evaluator scores each print configuration and returns structured diagnostics, which the LLM uses to propose natural-language adjustments that are compiled into machine-actionable guidance for optimization. On 100 Thingi10k parts, our LLM-guided loop achieves the best configuration on 78% objects with 0% likely-to-fail cases, while single-shot AI model recommendations are rarely best and exhibit 15% likely-to-fail cases. These results suggest that LLMs provide more value as constrained decision modules in evidence-driven optimization loops than as end-to-end oracles for print configuration selection. We expect this result to extend to broader LLM-based robot programming.

</details>


### [45] [ROBOGATE: Adaptive Failure Discovery for Safe Robot Policy Deployment via Two-Stage Boundary-Focused Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22126)
*Byungjin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROBOGATE是一个机器人部署风险管理框架，通过两阶段自适应采样策略在物理仿真中高效发现操作参数空间中的故障边界，显著提升故障检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境中部署学习型机器人操作策略需要进行严格的部署前验证，但在高维参数空间中进行详尽测试是不可行的，需要一种高效的方法来发现故障边界。

Method: 结合物理仿真与两阶段自适应采样策略：第一阶段使用拉丁超立方采样在8维参数空间中进行20,000次均匀分布实验；第二阶段在30-70%成功率过渡区域进行10,000次边界聚焦采样，实现精确的故障边界映射。

Result: 逻辑回归风险模型在组合数据集上达到AUC 0.780（vs. 第一阶段单独0.754），识别出封闭形式的故障边界方程，发现影响两个机器人平台的四个通用危险区域；VLA模型评估显示Octo-Small在68个对抗场景中成功率为0.0%，而脚本化基线为100%。

Conclusion: ROBOGATE框架能够高效发现机器人操作策略的故障边界，为工业环境中学习型机器人策略的部署前验证提供了有效的风险管理工具，特别揭示了基础模型在工业部署中面临的挑战。

Abstract: Deploying learned robot manipulation policies in industrial settings requires rigorous pre-deployment validation, yet exhaustive testing across high-dimensional parameter spaces is intractable. We present ROBOGATE, a deployment risk management framework that combines physics-based simulation with a two-stage adaptive sampling strategy to efficiently discover failure boundaries in the operational parameter space. Stage 1 employs Latin Hypercube Sampling (LHS) across an 8-dimensional parameter space to establish a coarse failure landscape from 20,000 uniformly distributed experiments. Stage 2 applies boundary-focused sampling that concentrates 10,000 additional experiments in the 30-70% success rate transition zone, enabling precise failure boundary mapping. Using NVIDIA Isaac Sim with Newton physics, we evaluate a scripted pick-and-place controller on two robot embodiments -- Franka Panda (7-DOF) and UR5e (6-DOF) -- across 30,000 total experiments. Our logistic regression risk model achieves an AUC of 0.780 on the combined dataset (vs. 0.754 for Stage 1 alone), identifies a closed-form failure boundary equation, and reveals four universal danger zones affecting both robot platforms. We further demonstrate the framework on VLA (Vision-Language-Action) model evaluation, where Octo-Small achieves 0.0% success rate on 68 adversarial scenarios versus 100% for the scripted baseline -- a 100-point gap that underscores the challenge of deploying foundation models in industrial settings. ROBOGATE is open-source and runs on a single GPU workstation.

</details>


### [46] [Closed-Loop Verbal Reinforcement Learning for Task-Level Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22169)
*Dmitrii Plotnikov,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Dmitrii Maliukov,Dmitrij Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Artem Trandofilov,Georgii Gazaryan,Kirill Bogatikov,Timofei Kozlov,Igor Duchinskii,Mikhail Konenkov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于移动机器人任务级规划的Verbal Reinforcement Learning框架，通过自然语言反馈迭代改进行为树策略，实现可解释的规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对移动机器人在执行不确定性下的任务规划问题，需要一种可解释、透明且能适应执行失败的规划框架。传统强化学习缺乏透明性，而符号规划难以适应不确定性

Method: 采用闭环架构，结合大型语言模型作为行动者生成可执行行为树，视觉语言模型作为批评者提供结构化自然语言反馈，通过语言反馈迭代改进策略而非梯度优化

Result: 在真实移动机器人多阶段操作和导航任务中验证，框架支持可解释的策略改进、对执行失败的闭环适应，并能可靠部署在物理机器人系统上

Conclusion: VRL框架在符号规划层面实现策略更新，提供透明推理、显式因果反馈和人类可解释的策略演化，为不确定性环境下的机器人任务规划提供了新的可解释方法

Abstract: We propose a new Verbal Reinforcement Learning (VRL) framework for interpretable task-level planning in mobile robotic systems operating under execution uncertainty. The framework follows a closed-loop architecture that enables iterative policy improvement through interaction with the physical environment. In our framework, executable Behavior Trees are repeatedly refined by a Large Language Model actor using structured natural-language feedback produced by a Vision-Language Model critic that observes the physical robot and execution traces. Unlike conventional reinforcement learning, policy updates in VRL occur directly at the symbolic planning level, without gradient-based optimization. This enables transparent reasoning, explicit causal feedback, and human-interpretable policy evolution. We validate the proposed framework on a real mobile robot performing a multi-stage manipulation and navigation task under execution uncertainty. Experimental results show that the framework supports explainable policy improvements, closed-loop adaptation to execution failures, and reliable deployment on physical robotic systems.

</details>


### [47] [Cross-Modal Reinforcement Learning for Navigation with Degraded Depth Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22182)
*Omkar Sawant,Luca Zanatta,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跨模态学习框架，利用深度和灰度图像的互补信息进行鲁棒导航，通过Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder学习共享潜在表示，在深度传感器退化时从灰度图像推断深度特征，结合强化学习策略实现无碰撞导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，深度传感器容易因光照条件差、反射表面等不利因素而退化，导致导航性能下降。需要利用其他模态（如灰度图像）的互补信息来增强系统在深度传感器失效时的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder，通过强制跨模态一致性学习共享潜在表示，使系统能够在深度测量损坏时从灰度观测中推断深度相关特征。将学习到的表示与基于强化学习的策略集成，用于无碰撞导航。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法在深度传感器显著退化情况下仍能保持鲁棒性能，并成功迁移到真实环境。系统能够在深度信息损坏时利用灰度图像信息维持导航能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态学习框架有效利用深度和灰度图像的互补信息，增强了导航系统在深度传感器退化条件下的鲁棒性，实现了从仿真到真实环境的成功迁移。

Abstract: This paper presents a cross-modal learning framework that exploits complementary information from depth and grayscale images for robust navigation. We introduce a Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder that learns shared latent representations by enforcing cross-modal consistency, enabling the system to infer depth-relevant features from grayscale observations when depth measurements are corrupted. The learned representations are integrated with a Reinforcement Learning-based policy for collision-free navigation in unstructured environments when depth sensors experience degradation due to adverse conditions such as poor lighting or reflective surfaces. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance under significant depth degradation and successfully transfers to real environments.

</details>


### [48] [Make Tracking Easy: Neural Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22201)
*Qingrui Zhao,Kaiyue Yang,Xiyu Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Yi Lu,Xinfang Zhang,Wei Yin,Qiu Shen,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NMR神经运动重定向框架，将传统优化重定向问题转化为数据分布学习问题，通过分层数据管道和CNN-Transformer架构消除关节跳跃和自碰撞，显著提升人形机器人运动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要多样化的运动技能以适应复杂环境，但从人类数据到机器人运动的重定向存在运动学和动力学上的"具身鸿沟"。传统基于优化的重定向方法存在非凸性问题，容易陷入局部最优，导致关节跳跃和自穿透等物理伪影。

Method: 提出NMR神经运动重定向框架：1) CEPR分层数据管道：利用基于VAE的运动聚类将异构运动分组为潜在模式，通过大规模并行强化学习专家将噪声人类演示投影到机器人可行运动流形；2) 非自回归CNN-Transformer架构：利用全局时间上下文抑制重建噪声并绕过几何陷阱。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，NMR相比最先进基线方法消除了关节跳跃，显著减少了自碰撞。NMR生成的参考运动加速了下游全身控制策略的收敛，为弥合人机具身鸿沟提供了可扩展路径。

Conclusion: NMR框架通过将运动重定向问题重新定义为数据分布学习而非优化最优解，成功解决了传统方法的非凸性问题。该方法生成的高质量运动数据不仅改善了运动质量，还促进了下游控制策略的学习，为实现人形机器人的复杂动态技能提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require diverse motor skills to integrate into complex environments, but bridging the kinematic and dynamic embodiment gap from human data remains a major bottleneck. We demonstrate through Hessian analysis that traditional optimization-based retargeting is inherently non-convex and prone to local optima, leading to physical artifacts like joint jumps and self-penetration. To address this, we reformulate the targeting problem as learning data distribution rather than optimizing optimal solutions, where we propose NMR, a Neural Motion Retargeting framework that transforms static geometric mapping into a dynamics-aware learned process. We first propose Clustered-Expert Physics Refinement (CEPR), a hierarchical data pipeline that leverages VAE-based motion clustering to group heterogeneous movements into latent motifs. This strategy significantly reduces the computational overhead of massively parallel reinforcement learning experts, which project and repair noisy human demonstrations onto the robot's feasible motion manifold. The resulting high-fidelity data supervises a non-autoregressive CNN-Transformer architecture that reasons over global temporal context to suppress reconstruction noise and bypass geometric traps. Experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid across diverse dynamic tasks (e.g., martial arts, dancing) show that NMR eliminates joint jumps and significantly reduces self-collisions compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, NMR-generated references accelerate the convergence of downstream whole-body control policies, establishing a scalable path for bridging the human-robot embodiment gap.

</details>


### [49] [DexDrummer: In-Hand, Contact-Rich, and Long-Horizon Dexterous Robot Drumming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22263)
*Hung-Chieh Fang,Amber Xie,Jennifer Grannen,Kenneth Llontop,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDrummer：一种用于击鼓的层次化物体中心双手灵巧操作策略，结合轨迹规划和强化学习，解决灵巧操作中的接触丰富、长时程协调等挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 击鼓作为灵巧操作的测试平台，自然整合了三个关键挑战：手内控制（稳定和调整鼓槌）、接触丰富的交互（反复击打鼓面）和长时程协调（切换鼓和维持节奏）

Method: 提出DexDrummer框架，采用层次化物体中心双手击鼓策略，结合轨迹规划和残差强化学习校正以实现快速鼓间转换；灵巧操作策略处理接触丰富的动力学，通过显式建模手指-鼓槌和鼓槌-鼓面交互的奖励函数指导

Result: 在仿真中，策略能演奏两种音乐风格：多鼓双手歌曲和需要更高灵巧性的技术练习；在仿真双手任务中，灵巧反应策略比固定抓握策略在简单歌曲上F1分数提高1.87倍，在困难歌曲上提高1.22倍；在真实世界任务中，能在多鼓设置中演奏训练歌曲及其扩展版本，F1分数达到1.0

Conclusion: DexDrummer成功展示了将灵巧操作、接触丰富交互和长时程协调整合到单一复杂任务中的能力，为灵巧操作研究提供了有效的测试平台和解决方案

Abstract: Performing in-hand, contact-rich, and long-horizon dexterous manipulation remains an unsolved challenge in robotics. Prior hand dexterity works have considered each of these three challenges in isolation, yet do not combine these skills into a single, complex task. To further test the capabilities of dexterity, we propose drumming as a testbed for dexterous manipulation. Drumming naturally integrates all three challenges: it involves in-hand control for stabilizing and adjusting the drumstick with the fingers, contact-rich interaction through repeated striking of the drum surface, and long-horizon coordination when switching between drums and sustaining rhythmic play. We present DexDrummer, a hierarchical object-centric bimanual drumming policy trained in simulation with sim-to-real transfer. The framework reduces the exploration difficulty of pure reinforcement learning by combining trajectory planning with residual RL corrections for fast transitions between drums. A dexterous manipulation policy handles contact-rich dynamics, guided by rewards that explicitly model both finger-stick and stick-drum interactions. In simulation, we show our policy can play two styles of music: multi-drum, bimanual songs and challenging, technical exercises that require increased dexterity. Across simulated bimanual tasks, our dexterous, reactive policy outperforms a fixed grasp policy by 1.87x across easy songs and 1.22x across hard songs F1 scores. In real-world tasks, we show song performance across a multi-drum setup. DexDrummer is able to play our training song and its extended version with an F1 score of 1.0.

</details>


### [50] [UniDex: A Robot Foundation Suite for Universal Dexterous Hand Control from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22264)
*Gu Zhang,Qicheng Xu,Haozhe Zhang,Jianhan Ma,Long He,Yiming Bao,Zeyu Ping,Zhecheng Yuan,Chenhao Lu,Chengbo Yuan,Tianhai Liang,Xiaoyu Tian,Maanping Shao,Feihong Zhang,Mingyu Ding,Yang Gao,Hao Zhao,Hang Zhao,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex是一个机器人基础套件，包含大规模机器人中心数据集、统一的视觉-语言-动作策略和实用的人体数据采集系统，用于通用灵巧手控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作面临三大挑战：真实机器人遥操作数据收集成本高、手部执行器异构性大、控制维度高。需要一种可扩展的方法来实现通用灵巧手控制。

Method: 1) 构建UniDex-Dataset：从人类第一视角视频数据集中提取50K+轨迹，覆盖8种灵巧手；采用人在回路的重定向方法对齐指尖轨迹，保留合理的手-物接触；使用带掩码的3D点云缩小视觉和运动学差距。2) 提出函数-执行器对齐空间(FAAS)：将功能相似的执行器映射到共享坐标，实现跨手迁移。3) 训练UniDex-VLA：基于FAAS的3D VLA策略，在UniDex-Dataset上预训练，任务演示微调。4) 开发UniDex-Cap：便携式采集系统，记录同步RGB-D流和手部姿态，转换为机器人可执行轨迹。

Result: 在两个不同手部执行器的工具使用任务上，UniDex-VLA达到81%的平均任务进度，大幅超越先前VLA基线方法，展现出强大的空间、物体和零样本跨手泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDex-Dataset、UniDex-VLA和UniDex-Cap共同构成了一个可扩展的通用灵巧操作基础套件，通过统一的数据集、动作空间和采集系统解决了灵巧操作的关键挑战。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation remains challenging due to the cost of collecting real-robot teleoperation data, the heterogeneity of hand embodiments, and the high dimensionality of control. We present UniDex, a robot foundation suite that couples a large-scale robot-centric dataset with a unified vision-language-action (VLA) policy and a practical human-data capture setup for universal dexterous hand control. First, we construct UniDex-Dataset, a robot-centric dataset over 50K trajectories across eight dexterous hands (6--24 DoFs), derived from egocentric human video datasets. To transform human data into robot-executable trajectories, we employ a human-in-the-loop retargeting procedure to align fingertip trajectories while preserving plausible hand-object contacts, and we operate on explicit 3D pointclouds with human hands masked to narrow kinematic and visual gaps. Second, we introduce the Function-Actuator-Aligned Space (FAAS), a unified action space that maps functionally similar actuators to shared coordinates, enabling cross-hand transfer. Leveraging FAAS as the action parameterization, we train UniDex-VLA, a 3D VLA policy pretrained on UniDex-Dataset and finetuned with task demonstrations. In addition, we build UniDex-Cap, a simple portable capture setup that records synchronized RGB-D streams and human hand poses and converts them into robot-executable trajectories to enable human-robot data co-training that reduces reliance on costly robot demonstrations. On challenging tool-use tasks across two different hands, UniDex-VLA achieves 81% average task progress and outperforms prior VLA baselines by a large margin, while exhibiting strong spatial, object, and zero-shot cross-hand generalization. Together, UniDex-Dataset, UniDex-VLA, and UniDex-Cap provide a scalable foundation suite for universal dexterous manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [51] [Approximating Convex Hulls via Range Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20943)
*T. Schibler,J. Xue,J. Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在范围搜索预言机设置下的凸包问题，通过查询范围空性预言机来估计未知点集的凸包，分析了查询数量与输出误差之间的紧致权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着各种应用中数据规模的快速增长，研究在仅能通过查询范围搜索预言机访问输入点集的情况下解决几何问题变得重要。先前工作研究了这种预言机设置下的几何问题，本文继续这一研究方向，探讨最基本的几何问题之一——凸包问题。

Method: 研究未知点集P在[0,1]^d中的凸包问题，通过查询范围空性预言机来输出凸多边形C作为凸包CH(P)的估计。误差定义为对称差C⊕CH(P)的体积。分析不同变体问题的查询数量与输出误差之间的权衡关系，包括正交空性查询和半平面空性查询，以及非自适应和自适应查询。

Result: 1. d维空间正交空性查询：确定性算法使用q个查询能达到的最小误差为Θ(q^{-1/d})（非自适应）和Θ(q^{-1/(d-1)})（自适应）。特别地，在2D中，非自适应和自适应查询的界限分别为Θ(1/√q)和Θ(1/q)。2. 2D半平面空性查询：确定性算法使用q个查询能达到的最小误差为Θ(1/√q)（非自适应）和$\widetildeΘ(1/q^2)$（自适应），其中$\widetildeΘ$隐藏对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文在范围搜索预言机设置下对凸包问题进行了系统研究，证明了不同查询类型（正交/半平面）和查询策略（非自适应/自适应）下的紧致或近紧致误差界限。这些结果为理解预言机设置下几何问题的计算复杂性提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: Recently, motivated by the rapid increase of the data size in various applications, Monemizadeh [APPROX'23] and Driemel, Monemizadeh, Oh, Staals, and Woodruff [SoCG'25] studied geometric problems in the setting where the only access to the input point set is via querying a range-search oracle. Algorithms in this setting are evaluated on two criteria: (i) the number of queries to the oracle and (ii) the error of the output. In this paper, we continue this line of research and investigate one of the most fundamental geometric problems in the oracle setting, i.e., the convex hull problem. Let $P$ be an unknown set of points in $[0,1]^d$ equipped with a range-emptiness oracle. Via querying the oracle, the algorithm is supposed to output a convex polygon $C \subseteq [0,1]^d$ as an estimation of the convex hull $CH(P)$ of $P$. The error of the output is defined as the volume of the symmetric difference $C \oplus CH(P) = (C \backslash CH(P)) \cup (CH(P) \backslash C)$. We prove tight and near-tight tradeoffs between the number of queries and the error of the output for different variants of the problem, depending on the type of the range-emptiness queries and whether the queries are non-adaptive or adaptive. - Orthogonal emptiness queries in $d$-dimensional space: We show that the minimum error a deterministic algorithm can achieve with $q$ queries is $Θ(q^{-1/d})$ if the queries are non-adaptive, and $Θ(q^{-1/(d-1)})$ if the queries are adaptive. In particular, in 2D, the bounds are $Θ(1/\sqrt{q})$ and $Θ(1/q)$ for non-adaptive and adaptive queries, respectively. - Halfplane emptiness queries in 2D: We show that the minimum error a deterministic algorithm can achieve with $q$ queries is $Θ(1/\sqrt{q})$ if the queries are non-adaptive, and $\widetildeΘ(1/q^2)$ if the queries are adaptive. Here $\widetildeΘ(\cdot)$ hides logarithmic factors.

</details>

TL;DR: Team Shadoks在CG:SHOP 2026挑战赛中获胜的方法，该挑战涉及平面三角剖分的并行重构，通过结合SAT精确方法、贪心启发式算法以及SAT/MaxSAT优化技术来寻找最优中心三角剖分和最短并行翻转路径。

Motivation: 解决CG:SHOP 2026挑战赛中平面三角剖分并行重构问题，该问题需要从一组输入三角剖分中选择一个中心三角剖分，并找到从每个输入到中心的最短并行翻转路径，同时最小化路径长度总和。

Abstract: We describe the methods used by Team Shadoks to win the CG:SHOP 2026 Challenge on parallel reconfiguration of planar triangulations. An instance is a collection of triangulations of a common point set. We must select a center triangulation and find short parallel-flip paths from each input triangulation to the center, minimizing the sum of path lengths. Our approach combines exact methods based on SAT with several greedy heuristics, and also makes use of SAT and MaxSAT for solution improvement. We present a SAT encoding for bounded-length paths and a global formulation for fixed path-length vectors. We discuss how these components interact in practice and summarize the performance of our solvers on the benchmark instances.

</details>

*Yakov Nekrich,Saladi Rahul*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在I/O模型中构造三维支配范围浅切割的最优成本算法，并应用于离线三维支配报告和近似计数问题。

Result: 算法实现了最优I/O复杂度O(N/B·log_{M/B}(N/B))，这是三维支配范围浅切割构造在I/O模型中的最优成本。作为应用，设计了离线三维支配报告和离线三维近似支配计数的快速算法。

Conclusion: 本文首次提供了三维支配范围浅切割在I/O模型中的最优构造算法，填补了该领域的重要空白。该算法有望在离线三维范围搜索问题和改进三维范围搜索数据结构的构造成本方面找到进一步应用。

Abstract: Shallow cuttings are a fundamental tool in computational geometry and spatial databases for solving offline and online range searching problems. For a set $P$ of $N$ points in 3-D, at SODA'14, Afshani and Tsakalidis designed an optimal $O(N\log_2N)$ time algorithm that constructs shallow cuttings for 3-D dominance ranges in internal memory. Even though shallow cuttings are used in the I/O-model to design space and query efficient range searching data structures, an efficient construction of them is not known till now. In this paper, we design an optimal-cost algorithm to construct shallow cuttings for 3-D dominance ranges. The number of I/Os performed by the algorithm is $O\left(\frac{N}{B}\log_{M/B}\left(\frac{N}{B}\right) \right)$, where $B$ is the block size and $M$ is the memory size.
  As two applications of the optimal-cost construction algorithm, we design fast algorithms for offline 3-D dominance reporting and offline 3-D approximate dominance counting. We believe that our algorithm will find further applications in offline 3-D range searching problems and in improving construction cost of data structures for 3-D range searching problems.

</details>

Abstract: We present an algorithm that computes the girth of the intersection graph of $n$ given line segments in the plane in $O(n^{1.483})$ expected time. This is the first such algorithm with $O(n^{3/2-\varepsilon})$ running time for a positive constant $\varepsilon$, and makes progress towards an open question posed by Chan (SODA 2023). The main techniques include (i)~the usage of recent subcubic algorithms for bounded-difference min-plus matrix multiplication, and (ii)~an interesting variant of the planar graph separator theorem. The result extends to intersection graphs of connected algebraic curves or semialgebraic sets of constant description complexity.

</details>


### [55] [Triangulating a Polygon with Holes in Optimal (Deterministic) Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21617)
*Timothy M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出了计算多边形三角剖分和梯形分解的确定性最优算法，时间复杂度为O(n + h log h)，改进了之前的最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多边形三角剖分和梯形分解是计算几何中的基本问题，已有算法存在时间复杂度的改进空间。Clarkson、Cole和Tarjan（1992）以及Seidel（1991）提出了随机算法，Bar-Yehuda和Chazelle（1994）提出了确定性算法，但都没有达到最优的O(n + h log h)时间复杂度。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用Chazelle的线性时间简单多边形三角剖分算法作为黑盒。对于有h个洞的多边形或h条不相交简单多边形链，设计了新的确定性算法。对于可能相交的非简单多边形链，在交点数不超过n^{1-ε}时，也能达到最优时间复杂度。

Result: 提出了第一个确定性最优算法，时间复杂度为O(n + h log h)，解决了多边形三角剖分和梯形分解问题。对于非简单且可能相交的多边形链，在交点数受限的情况下也达到了最优时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文解决了多边形三角剖分和梯形分解的确定性最优算法问题，填补了该领域长期存在的空白，将时间复杂度从之前的次优结果改进到理论最优的O(n + h log h)。

Abstract: We consider the problem of triangulating a polygon with $n$ vertices and $h$ holes, or relatedly the problem of computing the trapezoidal decomposition of a collection of $h$ disjoint simple polygonal chains with $n$ vertices total. Clarkson, Cole, and Tarjan (1992) and Seidel (1991) gave randomized algorithms running in $O(n\log^*n + h\log h)$ time, while Bar-Yehuda and Chazelle (1994) described deterministic algorithms running in $O(n+h\log^{1+\varepsilon}h)$ or $O((n+h\log h)\log\log h)$ time, for an arbitrarily small positive constant $\varepsilon$. No improvements have been reported since. We describe a new $O(n + h\log h)$-time algorithm, which is optimal and deterministic.
  More generally, when the given polygonal chains are not necessarily simple and may intersect each other, we show how to compute their trapezoidal decomposition (and in particular, compute all intersections) in optimal $O(n + h\log h)$ deterministic time when the number of intersections is at most $n^{1-\varepsilon}$.
  To obtain these results, Chazelle's linear-time algorithm for triangulating a simple polygon is used as a black box.

</details>


### [56] [Charting the Diameter Computation Landscape of Geometric Intersection Graphs in Three Dimensions and Higher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21790)
*Timothy M. Chan,Hsien-Chih Chang,Jie Gao,Sándor Kisfaludi-Bak,Hung Le,Da Wei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维及更高维几何相交图的直径计算问题，给出了首个三维单位立方体直径≤3的亚二次时间算法，建立了单位球与单位立方体在小直径情况下的计算复杂度分离，并提供了多种几何对象的直径计算算法与下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在二维几何相交图直径计算方面取得了显著进展，但在三维及更高维度，即使对于单位球或单位立方体等基本对象，也缺乏已知的亚二次时间算法。Bringmann等人的工作给出了多个亚二次时间下界，暗示了高维直径计算问题的悲观前景。本文旨在扩展三维及更高维对象直径计算的研究版图，提供一些积极的结果。

Method: 1. 针对三维单位立方体直径≤3问题，提出了基于伪直线（pseudolines）新连接的亚二次时间算法；2. 在正交向量（OV）假设下，证明了三维单位球直径≤3问题的亚二次时间下界；3. 为三维单位立方体直径≤2问题设计了近线性时间算法；4. 为任意维度和尺寸的矩形框的直径≤2和直径≤3问题分别提供了亚二次时间算法和下界。

Result: 1. 首次实现了三维单位立方体直径≤3的亚二次时间判定算法；2. 首次在小直径情况下分离了单位球与单位立方体的计算复杂度；3. 实现了三维单位立方体直径≤2的近线性时间算法；4. 为矩形框的直径≤2和直径≤3问题分别建立了亚二次时间算法和下界。

Conclusion: 本文显著扩展了三维及更高维几何相交图直径计算的研究版图，提供了多个积极结果。通过引入伪直线连接等新技术，首次在三维及更高维实现了某些直径问题的亚二次时间算法，并建立了不同几何对象在小直径情况下的计算复杂度分离，为高维几何图直径计算问题带来了新的希望。

Abstract: Recent research on computing the diameter of geometric intersection graphs has made significant strides, primarily focusing on the 2D case where truly subquadratic-time algorithms were given for simple objects such as unit-disks and (axis-aligned) squares. However, in three or higher dimensions, there is no known truly subquadratic-time algorithm for any intersection graph of non-trivial objects, even basic ones such as unit balls or (axis-aligned) unit cubes. This was partially explained by the pioneering work of Bringmann et al. [SoCG '22] which gave several truly subquadratic lower bounds, notably for unit balls or unit cubes in 3D when the graph diameter $Δ$ is at least $Ω(\log n)$, hinting at a pessimistic outlook for the complexity of the diameter problem in higher dimensions. In this paper, we substantially extend the landscape of diameter computation for objects in three and higher dimensions, giving a few positive results. Our highlighted findings include:
  - A truly subquadratic-time algorithm for deciding if the diameter of unit cubes in 3D is at most 3 (Diameter-3 hereafter), the first algorithm of its kind for objects in 3D or higher dimensions. Our algorithm is based on a novel connection to pseudolines, which is of independent interest.
  - A truly subquadratic time lower bound for \Diameter-3 of unit balls in 3D under the Orthogonal Vector (OV) hypothesis, giving the first separation between unit balls and unit cubes in the small diameter regime. Previously, computing the diameter for both objects was known to be truly subquadratic hard when the diameter is $Ω(\log n)$.
  - A near-linear-time algorithm for Diameter-2 of unit cubes in 3D, generalizing the previous result for unit squares in 2D.
  - A truly subquadratic-time algorithm and lower bound for Diameter-2 and Diameter-3 of rectangular boxes (of arbitrary dimension and sizes), respectively.

</details>


### [57] [Online Packing of Orthogonal Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22098)
*Tim Gerlach,Benjamin Hennies,Linda Kleist*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了正交多边形在线装箱问题，发现正交六边形的竞争比下界为Ω(n/logn)，而正交八边形的最优竞争比为n（即每个物体单独装箱）。对于退化多边形（骨架），六骨架有常数竞争比算法，八骨架竞争比下界为n。研究还探讨了其他变体如条带装箱、周长装箱和最小包围盒面积装箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统装箱理论研究主要关注矩形和盒状物体，而复杂形状的在线装箱问题研究较少。已知凸多边形在平移下不存在常数竞争比的在线算法，因此研究正交多边形（特别是复杂度较低的正交多边形）的在线装箱问题具有理论意义。

Method: 研究采用理论分析方法，针对正交多边形在线装箱问题建立下界证明。对于正交六边形，证明了任何在线算法的最小竞争比为Ω(n/logn)；对于正交八边形，证明了最优竞争比为n（即每个物体单独装箱）。同时考虑了对称或小的正交六边形、退化多边形（骨架）以及不同装箱变体（条带装箱、周长装箱、最小包围盒面积装箱）。

Result: 1. 正交六边形在线装箱：任何算法竞争比下界为Ω(n/logn)；对称或小的六边形存在常数竞争比算法
2. 正交八边形在线装箱：最优竞争比为n（即每个物体单独装箱）
3. 退化多边形（骨架）：六骨架存在常数竞争比算法；八骨架竞争比下界为n
4. 其他变体：条带装箱竞争比下界为Ω(n/logn)；周长装箱存在O(1)竞争比算法；最小包围盒面积装箱存在O(√n)竞争比算法；当每个物体都能放入单位箱内部时，临界装箱密度为正

Conclusion: 正交多边形的复杂度对在线装箱算法的性能有显著影响。正交六边形比凸多边形有更好的下界（Ω(n/logn) vs 无常数竞争比），但正交八边形的最优竞争比退化为n，表明复杂度增加会显著恶化算法性能。对于退化多边形，从六骨架到八骨架的复杂度变化导致算法性能从常数竞争比急剧下降到n竞争比。研究为正交多边形在线装箱问题建立了理论界限，并为不同变体提供了算法保证。

Abstract: While rectangular and box-shaped objects dominate the classic discourse of theoretic investigations, a fascinating frontier lies in packing more complex shapes. Given recent insights that convex polygons do not allow for constant competitive online algorithms for diverse variants under translation, we study orthogonal polygons, in particular of small complexity. For translational packings of orthogonal 6-gons, we show that the competitive ratio of any online algorithm that aims to pack the items into a minimal number of unit bins is in $Ω(n / \log n)$, where $n$ denotes the number of objects. In contrast, we show that constant competitive algorithms exist when the orthogonal 6-gons are symmetric or small. For (orthogonally convex) orthogonal 8-gons, we show that the trivial $n$-competitive algorithm, which places each item in its own bin, is best-possible, i.e., every online algorithm has an asymptotic competitive ratio of at least $n$. This implies that for general orthogonal polygons, the trivial algorithm is best possible.
  Interestingly, for packing degenerate orthogonal polygons (with thickness $0$), called skeletons, the change in complexity is even more drastic. While constant competitive algorithms for 6-skeletons exist, no online algorithm for 8-skeletons achieves a competitive ratio better than $n$.
  For other packing variants of orthogonal 6-gons under translation, our insights imply the following consequences. The asymptotic competitive ratio of any online algorithm is in $Ω(n / \log n)$ for strip packing, and there exist online algorithms with competitive ratios in $O(1)$ for perimeter packing, or in $O(\sqrt{n})$ for minimizing the area of the bounding box. Moreover, the critical packing density is positive (if every object individually fits into the interior of a unit bin).

</details>

*Jacob Fox,Jonathan Tidor*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 证明了任意维度d中球体和球面相交图存在最优分隔符，给出了大小为O_d(m^{1/d}n^{1-2/d})的平衡分隔符存在性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维空间中几何对象的相交图结构，特别是寻找有效的分隔符来分析和处理这类图

Method: 使用几何组合方法，分析球体和球面相交图的组合结构，证明存在特定大小的平衡分隔符

Result: 证明了对于d维空间中n个球体的相交图，若包含m条边，则存在大小为O_d(m^{1/d}n^{1-2/d})的平衡分隔符，且该界在参数意义下是最优的

Conclusion: 该结果不仅适用于球体和球面，也适用于胖凸体及其边界，为高维几何相交图的分析提供了重要的分隔符理论工具

Abstract: We prove the existence of optimal separators for intersection graphs of balls and spheres in any dimension $d$. One of our results is that if an intersection graph of $n$ spheres in $\mathbb{R}^d$ has $m$ edges, then it contains a balanced separator of size $O_d(m^{1/d}n^{1-2/d})$. This bound is best possible in terms of the parameters involved. The same result holds if the balls and spheres are replaced by fat convex bodies and their boundaries.

</details>


### [59] [Flip Distance of Non-Crossing Spanning Trees: NP-Hardness and Improved Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22262)
*Håvard Bakke Bjerkevik,Joseph Dorfer,Linda Kleist,Torsten Ueckerdt,Birgit Vogtenhuber*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了平面上点集非交叉生成树的翻转图，证明了计算两树间翻转距离是NP难的（即使点集处于凸位置），并改进了翻转图直径的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非交叉生成树翻转图的直径和计算复杂度，特别是针对凸位置点集。先前研究提出了冲突图作为关键工具，本文旨在探索冲突图的更多应用潜力。

Method: 采用冲突图作为核心分析工具，证明翻转距离计算的NP难性（适用于凸位置点集、兼容翻转和旋转操作）。通过分析堆叠树结构，证明特定情况下的最优性。构造新实例改进直径下界。

Result: 1) 计算非交叉生成树间翻转距离是NP难的，即使点集处于凸位置，且适用于兼容翻转和旋转操作；2) 若一棵树是堆叠树，则至多需要14/9(n-1)次翻转可达任意其他树；3) 凸位置点集翻转图直径下界改进为11/7n - o(n)。

Conclusion: 冲突图是分析非交叉生成树翻转问题的强大工具，不仅有助于直径分析，还能用于证明计算复杂性问题。翻转距离计算本质上是困难的，即使对高度受限的情况也是如此。

Abstract: We consider the problem of reconfiguring non-crossing spanning trees on point sets. For a set $P$ of $n$ points in general position in the plane, the flip graph $F(P)$ has a vertex for each non-crossing spanning tree on $P$ and an edge between any two spanning trees that can be transformed into each other by the exchange of a single edge. This flip graph has been intensively studied, lately with an emphasis on determining its diameter diam$(F(P))$ for sets $P$ of $n$ points in convex position. The current best bounds are $\frac{14}{9}n-O(1) \leq$ diam$(F(P))<\frac{15}{9}n-3$ [Bjerkevik, Kleist, Ueckerdt, and Vogtenhuber; SODA 2025]. The crucial tool for both the upper and lower bound are so-called *conflict graphs*, which the authors stated might be the key ingredient for determining the diameter (up to lower-order terms).
  In this paper, we pick up the concept of conflict graphs and show that this tool is even more versatile than previously hoped. As our first main result, we use conflict graphs to show that computing the flip distance between two non-crossing spanning trees is NP-hard, even for point sets in convex position. Interestingly, the result still holds for more constrained flip operations, concretely, compatible flips (where the removed and the added edge do not cross) and rotations (where the removed and the added edge share an endpoint). Extending the line of research from [BKUV SODA25], we present new insights on the diameter of the flip graph. Their lower bound is based on a constant-size pair of trees, one of which is *stacked*. We show that if one of the trees is stacked, then the lower bound is indeed optimal up to a constant term, that is, there exists a flip sequence of length at most $\frac{14}{9}(n-1)$ to any other tree. Lastly, we improve the lower bound on the diameter of the flip graph $F(P)$ for $n$ points in convex position to $\frac{11}{7}n-o(n)$.

</details>
