### [60] [Fast Solving Complete 2000-Node Optimization Using Stochastic-Computing Simulated Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20197)
*Kota Katsuki,Duckgyu Shin,Naoya Onizawa,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于随机计算的模拟退火算法（SC-SA）用于解决大规模组合优化问题，相比传统SA实现了几个数量级的加速，并在MAX-CUT问题上获得了更好的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟退火算法在解决大规模组合优化问题时收敛速度较慢，需要更高效的算法来加速全局最优解的搜索过程。

Method: 提出基于随机计算的模拟退火算法（SC-SA），利用随机比特流进行计算，实现快速收敛到问题的全局最小能量状态。

Result: SC-SA相比典型SA实现了几个数量级的加速；在K2000（2000节点完全优化问题）上获得了比其他现有方法更好的MAX-CUT分数。

Conclusion: SC-SA是一种高效的大规模组合优化问题求解方法，通过随机计算技术显著提升了模拟退火算法的性能和收敛速度。

Abstract: In this paper, we evaluate stochastic-computing simulated annealing (SC-SA) for solving large-scale combinatorial optimization problems. SC-SA is designed using stochastic computing, where the computatoin is reazlied using random bitstream, resulting in fast converging to the global minimum energy of the problems. The proposed SC-SA is compared with a typical SA and existing simulated-annealing (SA) processors on the maximum cut (MAX-CUT) problems, such as Gset that is a benchmark for SA. The simulation results show that SC-SA realizes a few orders of magnitude faster than a typical SA. In addition, SC-SA achieves better MAX-CUT scores than other existing methods on K2000 that is a complete 2000-node optimization problem.

</details>


### [61] [The Coercive Projection Theorem for Canonical Reciprocal Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20205)
*Jonathan Washburn,Amir Rahnamai Barghi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种有限数据框架，用于在可分离互易成本下认证正向量的零缺陷（中性）配置，建立了基于识别组合律和局部二次校准的规范决策程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发一个有限数据框架来认证正向量在可分离互易成本下的零缺陷配置，确保在有限观测条件下能够可靠识别中性状态。

Method: 基于识别组合律和局部二次校准的公理化方法，构建了规范决策程序Φ*=A∘B∘P，其中投影/强制核心由规范成本公理决定，聚合/重构步骤在非退化可识别性轨迹上指定。

Result: 证明了该标量成本在非恒定连续成本中由识别组合律和平衡点的局部二次校准唯一表征，互易对称性和中性点归一化可从组合律推导。在守恒约束和短窗口观测下，构造的规范决策程序在所有可靠程序中是局部最大化的。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限数据条件下的零缺陷配置认证提供了理论基础和实用方法，规范决策程序在可识别性轨迹上具有最优性，为理性信号类的中性状态识别提供了系统解决方案。

Abstract: We develop a finite-data framework for certifying \emph{zero-defect} (neutral) configurations of positive vectors under the canonical separable reciprocal cost. We show that this scalar cost is characterized among non-constant continuous costs by the Recognition Composition Law together with a local quadratic calibration at balance; in particular, reciprocity symmetry and the normalization at the neutral point follow from the composition law. Under a conservation constraint and short-window observations of a rational (finite--state) signal class, we construct a canonical decision procedure that is \emph{locally maximal on the identifiability locus} among all sound procedures: any sound rule that resolves a datum must agree with the canonical output, and cannot resolve strictly more cases.
  The method is organized as $Φ^\ast=A\circ B\circ P$: the projection/coercivity core is forced by the canonical-cost axioms, while the aggregation/reconstruction step is specified on a non-degenerate identifiability locus (e.g.\ a Hankel invertibility condition).

</details>


### [62] [Dynamic Menu-Based Pricing for Electric Vehicle Charging with Vehicle-to-Grid Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20226)
*Mozhdeh Hematiboroujeni,Pierre Le Bodic,Adel N. Toosi,Markus Wagner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于菜单的动态定价机制，用于停车场运营商管理电动汽车充电和V2G放电，通过双层优化建模实现运营商利润提升30%、用户支付减少17%，V2G贡献增加87-235%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车快速增长带来环境效益，但无序充电给电网带来压力，而V2G技术虽有潜力但现有定价机制（如分时电价）缺乏放电激励。需要设计有效的激励机制来促进V2G参与，平衡电网供需并支持可再生能源

Method: 提出菜单式定价机制：电动汽车声明能量需求和停车时长，运营商根据实时电价提供放电水平与价格的选项菜单。将运营商与电动汽车的互动建模为双层优化问题，并转化为单层模型求解

Result: 相比无V2G基准，运营商利润增加30%，电动汽车支付减少17%；相比广泛使用的电价基准，运营商利润提高22-29%，电动汽车支付降低9-18%，V2G贡献增加87-235%。机制具有实用性、计算效率和经济效益优势

Conclusion: 提出的动态菜单式定价框架为实时电动汽车充电和V2G集成提供了实用、计算高效且经济有利的方法，能有效激励V2G参与，平衡电网供需，支持可再生能源整合

Abstract: The number of electric vehicles is rapidly increasing worldwide. This growth brings significant environmental benefits but also introduces new challenges: uncoordinated charging can place stress on the grid, particularly during peak hours. Beyond these challenges lies the opportunity for electric vehicles to feed energy back to the grid (V2G), which helps balance supply and demand and supports renewable energy. However, current pricing schemes such as time-of-use tariffs provide little incentive for discharging. To study incentive design in a realistic context, we focus on a parking lot operator who manages multiple EV chargers. We propose a menu-based pricing mechanism in which each EV declares its energy requirement and parking duration; given the retail real-time electricity prices, the operator offers a menu of options that trade off the allowed level of discharging and the associated price. We formulate this interaction as a bilevel optimization problem and reformulate it into a single-level model. Results show that, relative to a no-V2G baseline, the proposed mechanism increases operator profit by 30% and reduces EV payments by 17%. Compared to widely used tariff baselines, it improves operator profit by 22-29 percent, lowers EV payments by 9-18 percent, and increases V2G contribution by 87-235 percent. Overall, the results show that the proposed dynamic menu-based pricing framework provides a practical, computationally efficient, and economically advantageous approach for real-time EV charging and V2G integration.

</details>


### [63] [Compact Lifted Relaxations for Low-Rank Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20228)
*Ryan Cory-Wright,Jean Pauphilet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出针对秩约束二次优化问题的可处理凸松弛方法，通过提升半定规划技术消除对谱结构的依赖，获得紧凑且可扩展的松弛形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秩约束二次优化问题在缺乏谱结构时通常难以获得可处理的凸松弛，现有方法主要适用于具有置换不变性的目标或约束。本文旨在开发不依赖谱结构的通用可处理松弛方法。

Method: 采用提升半定规划技术，首先构建包含n²+nm+1维大矩阵的直接提升松弛，然后证明矩矩阵的许多块是冗余的，最终得到仅包含nm+1维和n+m维两个半定约束的紧凑等价松弛。针对矩阵补全、基追踪和降秩回归等具体问题，利用额外结构获得至多2max(n,m)维的更紧凑公式。

Result: 获得了秩约束二次优化问题的可扩展半定边界，松弛规模显著减小：从原始n²+nm+1维降低到两个较小半定约束(nm+1维和n+m维)，针对特定问题进一步降至2max(n,m)维。

Conclusion: 开发了不依赖谱结构的秩约束二次优化问题的可处理凸松弛框架，通过消除冗余获得紧凑公式，为广泛的低秩二次问题提供了可扩展的半定边界计算方法。

Abstract: We develop tractable convex relaxations for rank-constrained quadratic optimization problems over $n \times m$ matrices, a setting for which tractable relaxations are typically only available when the objective or constraints admit spectral (permutation-invariant) structure. We derive lifted semidefinite relaxations that do not require such spectral terms. Although a direct lifting introduces a large semidefinite constraint in dimension $n^2 + nm + 1$, we prove that many blocks of moment matrix are redundant and derive an equivalent compact relaxation that only involves two semidefinite constraints of dimension $nm + 1$ and $n+m$ respectively. For matrix completion, basis pursuit, and reduced-rank regression problems, we exploit additional structure to obtain even more compact formulations involving semidefinite matrices of dimension at most $2\max(n,m)$. Overall, we obtain scalable semidefinite bounds for a broad class of low-rank quadratic problems.

</details>


### [64] [Time-Reversed BSDEs for Accurate Gradient Estimation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20455)
*Yuhang Mei,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反向随机微分方程(BSDE)的梯度估计方法，用于改进扩散模型的微调过程，相比现有的伴随匹配方法，该方法能产生更稳定、方差更低的梯度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机最优控制(SOC)的扩散模型微调方法中，伴随匹配方法使用的伴随过程不适用于前向扩散过滤，这可能导致梯度估计不稳定或方差过高。需要一种更稳定的梯度估计方法来改进扩散模型的微调过程。

Method: 通过反向随机微分方程(BSDE)的视角重新审视扩散模型中的梯度估计，提出基于时间反转BSDE公式的替代估计器，该估计器产生适应于底层过滤的伴随过程，从而获得更稳定的梯度估计。

Result: 数值实验表明，在玩具扩散模型的微调任务中，所提出的方法表现出改进的梯度稳定性和竞争性性能。理论分析验证了所提估计器的准确性，并与伴随匹配方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 基于BSDE的梯度估计方法为扩散模型的微调提供了更稳定的梯度估计，通过适应过滤结构降低了方差，在扩散模型优化中具有潜在优势。

Abstract: There is a growing literature adopting a stochastic optimal control (SOC) perspective to fine-tune diffusion models and related generative policies. A prominent class of methods, known as iterative diffusion optimization, solves the SOC problem by simulating the diffusion process, evaluating a loss function, and applying stochastic optimization algorithms, with adjoint matching emerging as a state-of-the-art approach. However, the adjoint process used in these methods is not adapted to the forward diffusion filtration, which can lead to unstable or high-variance gradient estimates. In this paper, we revisit gradient estimation in diffusion models through the lens of backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs). We propose an alternative estimator based on a time-reversed BSDE formulation introduced in our prior work, which produces an adjoint process adapted to the underlying filtration. This adapted structure leads to more stable gradient estimates with potentially lower variance. We analyze the accuracy of the proposed estimator and compare it with adjoint matching. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning toy diffusion models demonstrate improved gradient stability and competitive performance.

</details>


### [65] [Perturbation Duality for Robust and Distributionally Robust Optimization: Short and General Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20503)
*Louis L. Chen,Jake Roth,Johannes O. Royset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将扰动对偶作为鲁棒优化和分布鲁棒优化的统一框架，相比传统方法更具一般性和概念清晰度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒优化和分布鲁棒优化的对偶方法主要依赖鞍点论证、拉格朗日技术和锥对偶，缺乏统一的理论框架。本文旨在展示Fenchel-Rockafellar凸分析中的扰动对偶作为一种更通用、统一的方法论的有效性。

Method: 应用Fenchel-Rockafellar凸分析中的扰动对偶理论：1) 将其应用于统一phi-散度和Wasserstein模糊集的最优传输框架；2) 建立无需紧致性假设的对偶表示；3) 开发基于双函数的表示方法，统一现有鲁棒对偶概念。

Result: 1) 为统一phi-散度和Wasserstein模糊集的DRO框架建立了对偶表示，无需先前认为必要的紧致性假设；2) 提供了鲁棒对偶（"原始最坏等于对偶最好"）的统一透明表征；3) 开发的双函数表示简化了近期结果，提供了更简洁的证明。

Conclusion: 扰动对偶是RO和DRO中一个多功能且未充分利用的框架，为广泛模型提供了概念统一和技术通用性，简化了现有结果并扩展了理论边界。

Abstract: Duality is a foundational tool in robust and distributionally robust optimization (RO and DRO), underpinning both analytical insights and tractable reformulations. The prevailing approaches in the literature primarily rely on saddle-point arguments, Lagrangian techniques, and conic duality. In contrast, this paper applies perturbation duality in the sense of Fenchel--Rockafellar convex analysis and demonstrates its effectiveness as a general and unifying methodology for deriving dual formulations in RO and DRO. We first apply perturbation duality to a recently proposed DRO framework that unifies phi-divergence and Wasserstein ambiguity sets through optimal transport with conditional moment constraints. We establish the associated dual representation without imposing compactness assumptions previously conjectured to be necessary, instead introducing alternative conditions motivated by perturbation analysis and leveraging the Interchangeability Principle. We then revisit the concept of robust duality -- commonly described as ``primal-worst equals dual-best'' -- and show that perturbation-based formulations provide a unified and transparent characterization of this principle. In particular, we develop a bifunction-based representation that encompasses existing formulations in the literature and yields concise and general proofs, substantially simplifying recent results. This work positions perturbation duality as a versatile and underutilized framework for RO and DRO, offering both conceptual unification and technical generality across a broad class of models.

</details>


### [66] [Optimality in Decentralized Optimization under Bandwidth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20735)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带宽受限的分布式非凸随机优化问题，推导了并行和异步设置下的最优时间复杂性（对数因子内），提出了Grace SGD和Leon SGD方法，并通过最小割/最大流等图论工具获得更紧的复杂性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在带宽受限的实际场景中缺乏理论保证，特别是对于非凸随机优化问题，需要建立更精确的复杂性分析框架，考虑通信约束对收敛速度的影响。

Method: 提出Grace SGD（针对同构设置）和Leon SGD（针对异构设置）两种分布式优化算法，利用最小割/最大流、Gomory-Hu树和Steiner树打包等图论工具分析通信拓扑对收敛性的影响，推导最优时间复杂性。

Result: 获得了带宽受限分布式非凸随机优化的最优时间复杂性界限（对数因子内），这些界限基于最小割/最大流等图论量，比现有工作更紧且更实用，为实际部署提供了理论指导。

Conclusion: 通过图论工具重新刻画分布式优化的通信复杂性，为带宽受限场景提供了理论最优的算法设计和分析框架，Grace SGD和Leon SGD在理论和实践上都具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider a realistic decentralized setup with bandwidth-constrained communication and derive optimal time complexities for non-convex stochastic parallel and asynchronous optimization (up to logarithmic factors). We develop the corresponding methods, Grace SGD and Leon SGD, for both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings. Unlike previous work, our optimal bounds are characterized in terms of min-cut/max-flow quantities and rely on tools from Gomory-Hu trees and Steiner Tree Packing problems, providing tighter and more practical complexities.

</details>


### [67] [Local Convergence Analysis of ADMM for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20751)
*Xiyuan Xie,Lihua Yang,Qia li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了ADMM算法在一类非凸复合优化问题中的局部收敛性，这类问题来源于现代成像和机器学习模型。在适当假设下，证明了ADMM的局部收敛性和线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于现代成像和机器学习模型中出现的非凸复合优化问题。这类问题具有凸约束集，目标函数包含可微（可能非凸）光滑项和通过线性映射组合的多面体凸非光滑项。研究受到Rockafellar近期工作的启发，旨在建立ADMM在非凸设置下的局部收敛理论。

Method: 方法包括：1）对多面体凸函数的Moreau包络的局部强凸性给出基本证明；2）在适当假设下证明强变分充分性条件成立；3）推导ADMM迭代的下降不等式，形式类似于凸问题的经典下降分析；4）通过选择合适的惩罚参数，建立ADMM到原始-对偶解的局部收敛性；5）当约束集为多面体凸时，证明局部线性收敛速率；6）提供三个分析示例说明结果适用性和局部假设的必要性。

Result: 主要结果：1）证明了多面体凸函数的Moreau包络具有局部强凸性；2）在适当假设下，所考虑问题满足强变分充分性条件；3）建立了ADMM迭代的下降不等式；4）对于合适的惩罚参数，证明了ADMM到原始-对偶解的局部收敛性；5）当约束集为多面体凸时，获得了局部线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: 结论：该研究为ADMM在一类非凸复合优化问题中的局部收敛性提供了理论保证。通过建立强变分充分性条件和推导下降不等式，证明了在适当假设下ADMM能够局部收敛到原始-对偶解，并在多面体凸约束下达到线性收敛速率。三个分析示例验证了理论结果的适用性和局部假设的必要性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the local convergence of the standard ADMM scheme for a class of nonconvex composite problems arising from modern imaging and machine learning models. This problem is constrained by a closed convex set, while its objective is the sum of a continuously differentiable (possibly nonconvex) smooth term and a polyhedral convex nonsmooth term composed with a linear mapping. Our analysis is mainly motivated by the recent works of Rockafellar [29,30]. We begin with an elementary proof of a key local strong convexity property of the Moreau envelope of polyhedral convex functions. Building on this property, we show that the strong variational sufficiency condition holds for the considered problem under appropriate assumptions. Using the strong variational sufficiency condition, we further derive a descent inequality for the ADMM iterates, in a form analogous to the classical descent analysis of ADMM for convex problems. As a consequence, for a suitable choice of the penalty parameter, we establish local convergence of the ADMM scheme to a primal-dual solution, and a local linear convergence rate for the case where the constraint set is polyhedral convex. Finally, we present three analytic examples to illustrate the applicability of our local convergence result and the necessity of the local assumptions.

</details>


### [68] [Derivative-Free Bilevel Optimization with Inexact Lower-Level Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20759)
*Edoardo Cesaroni,Giampaolo Liuzzi,Stefano Lucidi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种无导数双层优化框架，处理带约束的上层和下层问题，采用渐进精度策略求解下层问题，证明收敛性并通过数值实验验证自适应精度策略优于固定精度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统双层优化方法依赖一阶信息的局限性，处理上层问题无法获得或难以获得梯度信息的情况，同时应对变量边界约束和一般非线性约束。

Method: 采用无导数优化框架，上层问题仅使用函数值信息；下层问题采用渐进精度策略，在优化过程中逐步提高求解精度；通过精确罚函数方法处理复杂约束。

Result: 理论证明：无约束情况下收敛到Clarke-Jahn稳定点；有限精度下收敛到Goldstein近似稳定点；约束扩展下收敛到稳定点。数值实验：在BOLIB库160个问题上验证自适应精度策略优于固定精度方法，且精度要求越严格优势越明显。

Conclusion: 提出的无导数双层优化框架有效解决了上层问题缺乏一阶信息的情况，自适应精度策略在计算效率和求解质量上优于传统固定精度方法，为约束双层优化问题提供了实用的无导数解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose derivative-free framework for bilevel optimization. We consider both the upper and lower-level problems with bound constraints on the variables, as well as general nonlinear constraints, assuming that first-order information (in the upper-level) is not available or it is impractical to obtain. The lower-level problem is solved with an accuracy that is progressively refined throughout the optimization process. We first analyze the case in which the upper-level problem is subject only to bound constraints, establishing convergence to Clarke-Jahn stationary points when the refinement process is allowed to reach its maximum precision. When a limitation is imposed on this refinement process, we prove convergence to approximate stationary points using an extended notion of Goldstein stationarity. Finally, we extend the proposed framework to handle more complex constraints via an exact penalty function approach, proving convergence to stationary points under suitable assumptions. A comprehensive numerical study on 160 problems from the BOLIB collection shows that the adaptive accuracy strategy consistently yields better results than fixed-precision solves, with its benefits becoming more pronounced as the required lower-level accuracy becomes more stringent.

</details>


### [69] [A reliability-aware randomized simheuristic for the team orienteering problem with stochastic travel times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20766)
*Michele Circelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种面向团队定向问题（TOP）随机变体的可靠性感知模拟启发式方法，该方法在不确定旅行时间和全有或全无奖励策略下，平衡期望奖励与路径可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有不确定旅行时间和全有或全无奖励策略的团队定向问题随机变体。在这种策略下，如果路径旅行时间超过可用预算，则奖励完全丧失。这使得期望奖励与路径可靠性之间的权衡成为解决方案设计的核心问题。

Method: 提出一种可靠性感知模拟启发式方法，结合了基于节约的构造启发式、受控随机化、局部搜索和蒙特卡洛模拟。该方法直接在不确定性下评估候选解，并使用估计的期望奖励和可靠性标准来选择解，而不是依赖确定性优化后进行事后随机评估。

Result: 在从TOP文献改编的基准实例上的计算实验表明，所提出的方法相对于在不确定性下评估的确定性基线，显著提高了随机性能。在大多数实例中，模拟启发式方法同时提高了期望奖励和可靠性，在最宽松的制度下，可靠性可接近0.99，同时保持适中的计算时间。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知模拟启发式方法能有效解决具有不确定旅行时间和全有或全无奖励策略的团队定向问题，在期望奖励和路径可靠性之间实现良好平衡，显著优于传统的确定性优化方法。

Abstract: We study a stochastic variant of the Team Orienteering Problem (TOP) with uncertain travel times and an all-or-nothing reward policy, under which the reward of a route is lost if its travel time exceeds the available budget. This setting makes the trade-off between expected reward and route reliability a central issue in solution design.
  To address this problem, we propose a reliability-aware simheuristic that combines a savings-based constructive heuristic, controlled randomization, local search, and Monte Carlo simulation. The method evaluates candidate solutions directly under uncertainty and selects them using both estimated expected reward and a reliability criterion, rather than relying on deterministic optimization followed by ex-post stochastic evaluation.
  Computational experiments on benchmark instances adapted from the TOP literature show that the proposed approach substantially improves stochastic performance with respect to a deterministic baseline evaluated under uncertainty. In most instances, the simheuristic increases both expected reward and reliability, and in the loosest regimes reliability can approach 0.99 while keeping computation times moderate.

</details>


### [70] [On hyperbolic PDEs, filtered feedback control laws, and fractal-like stability crossing curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20877)
*Wim Michiels,Federico Bribiesca-Argomedo,Jean Auriol*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究一类双曲型偏微分方程的边界控制问题，通过积分-差分方程等价表示，采用低通滤波器解决直接补偿反射项导致的闭环系统脆弱性问题，分析了滤波器截止频率对稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲型PDE边界控制中，直接补偿反射项会导致闭环系统缺乏强稳定性的脆弱性问题，需要设计鲁棒的控制策略来平衡高频鲁棒性和低频性能。

Method: 采用积分-差分方程等价表示双曲型PDE，在控制律中加入低通滤波器，理论分析滤波器参数T（截止频率的倒数）的最大稳定区间，考虑PDE参数失配情况并推导稳定性充分条件，通过特殊案例验证稳定性图表的精确分析。

Result: 确定了参数T的最大稳定区间，推导了允许失配和截止频率的稳定性充分条件（在T=0附近呈圆锥形区域），特殊案例的精确稳定性图表显示稳定性穿越曲线具有分形结构，可用离散时滞极限系统解释。

Conclusion: 低通滤波器能有效解决双曲型PDE边界控制的稳定性问题，滤波器截止频率的选择需要在鲁棒性和性能间权衡，稳定性条件在参数空间中呈现特定几何结构，失配分析揭示了系统对参数不确定性的容忍度。

Abstract: The paper addresses the boundary control of a class of hyperbolic PDEs, based on an equivalent representation in terms of an integral-difference equation. The situation is considered where direct compensation of reflection terms induces a fragile closed-loop system, in the sense of lack of strong stability. This is theoretically resolved by adding a low-pass filter to the control law, but the choice of its cut-off frequency is crucial in balancing robustness at high frequencies and performance at low frequencies. First, the maximum stability interval in parameter $T$ is determined, with $T$ the inverse of the filter's cutoff frequency. Next, model mismatch on the PDE parameters is considered and a sufficient stability condition is derived in terms of allowable mismatch and cut-off frequency, satisfied in a region in the combined parameter space with a conic shape around $T=0$. Finally, this qualitative behavior is confirmed by exact stability charts for a special case where all model mismatch is contained into one parameter. It is highlighted that the set of stability crossing curves exhibits a fractal-like structure, which is explained using a limit system with discrete delays.

</details>


### [71] [Unfolding with a Wasserstein Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20903)
*Katy Craig,Benjamin Faktor,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用Wasserstein损失替代传统KL散度的数据展开方法，解决了Richardson-Lucy去卷积中的分箱误差问题，在粒子物理等领域表现出更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Richardson-Lucy(RL)去卷积使用KL散度作为损失函数，要求测量数据与噪声模型输出的支撑集重叠，通常通过分箱来强制满足这一假设，但分箱会引入数值误差。需要一种能避免分箱误差的替代方法。

Method: 提出使用Wasserstein损失替代KL散度的最优传输方法。建立了优化器存在性和唯一性的严格条件，回答了Li等人关于传输映射噪声模型存在性和唯一性必要条件的开放问题。开发了可证明收敛的广义Sinkhorn算法来计算近似优化器，该算法仅需噪声模型和测量数据的经验观测，计算复杂度与数据规模而非环境维度相关。

Result: 在受粒子物理中喷注质量展开启发的一维和二维问题上进行数值实验，结果表明最优传输方法相比经典RL去卷积提供了更稳健、准确的性能，特别是在分箱伪影显著的情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 基于Wasserstein损失的最优传输方法为数据展开问题提供了有前景的替代方案，避免了传统RL方法的分箱误差问题，在存在显著分箱伪影的应用场景中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Data unfolding -- the removal of noise or artifacts from measurements -- is a fundamental task across the experimental sciences. Of particular interest are applications in physics, where the dominant approach is Richardson-Lucy (RL) deconvolution. The classical RL approach aims to find denoised data that, once passed through the noise model, is as close as possible to the measured data in terms of Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. This requires that the support of the measured data overlaps with the output of the noise model, a hypothesis typically enforced by binning, which introduces numerical error.
  As a counterpoint, the present work studies an alternative formulation using a Wasserstein loss. We establish sharp conditions for existence and uniqueness of optimizers, answering open questions of Li, et al., regarding necessary conditions for existence and uniqueness in the case of transport map noise models. We then develop a provably convergent generalized Sinkhorn algorithm to compute approximate optimizers. Our algorithm requires only empirical observations of the noise model and measured data and scales with the size of the data, rather than the ambient dimension. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional problems inspired by jet mass unfolding in particle physics demonstrate that the optimal transport approach offers robust, accurate performance compared to classical RL deconvolution, particularly when binning artifacts are significant.

</details>


### [72] [Mirror-Free Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20918)
*Abhijeet Vyas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出无镜像映射的镜像近端算法，扩展经典方法，在相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz条件下分析收敛性，并展示其在二阶和三阶光滑极小极大优化中的应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典镜像近端算法需要显式的镜像映射，这在实际应用中可能受限。本文旨在消除这一限制，提出更通用的无镜像映射算法，使其能处理更广泛的优化问题，特别是高阶光滑极小极大优化问题。

Method: 提出无镜像映射镜像近端算法，通过引入更一般的势算子来松弛标准Bregman散度，在相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz条件下分析收敛性。还提出了强单调变体来解决正则化泰勒展开子问题。

Result: 成功扩展了经典镜像近端算法，使其无需显式镜像映射。建立了在相对光滑性和相对Lipschitz条件下的收敛性理论。展示了算法在二阶和三阶光滑极小极大优化中解决正则化泰勒展开子问题的能力。

Conclusion: 无镜像映射镜像近端算法为经典方法提供了更灵活的扩展，能够处理更广泛的优化问题，特别是在高阶光滑极小极大优化中具有应用潜力，为相关优化算法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a \emph{mirror-free} mirror prox (MFMP) algorithm, which extends the classic approach of Nemirovski (2004) to allow for proximal-like updates without the explicit need for a mirror map. We further analyze the convergence of our method under suitable notions of relative smoothness and relative Lipschitzness, for which we introduce a relaxation of the standard Bregman divergence in terms of more general potential operators. Finally, we show how a strongly monotone variant of our method allows us to solve regularized Taylor-expansion subproblems that appear in both second- and third-order smooth min-max optimization.

</details>


### [73] [Data-Driven Modeling of a Controlled Orthotropic Plate Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20931)
*Yongho Kim,Alexander Zuyev,Francesco Pellicano,Antonio Zippo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验数据构建振动板控制系统的替代模型，比较了线性回归、多层感知机和门控循环单元三种方法，发现多层感知机最能准确捕捉系统的非线性输入输出行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复合结构振动板控制系统的输入输出映射学习问题。由于材料的正交各向异性和未知阻尼特性，传统的解析建模方法面临挑战，因此需要从实验数据中构建替代模型。

Method: 从实验采样数据构建三种替代模型：线性回归、多层感知机（MLP）和门控循环单元（GRU）。通过比较分析评估这些模型对振动板控制系统动态行为的近似能力。

Result: 比较分析表明，多层感知机模型能够提供对该动态系统的可接受近似，能够捕捉其输入输出行为中潜在的非线性现象。

Conclusion: 对于具有正交各向异性材料和未知阻尼特性的振动板控制系统，基于实验数据的多层感知机模型是有效的替代建模方法，能够准确描述系统的非线性动态行为。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the input-output map of a controlled vibrating plate with a composite structure from experimental measurements. Analytical modeling of this control system faces challenges due to the essential orthotropy and unknown damping characteristics of the material. Surrogate models based on linear regression, multilayer perceptrons, and gated recurrent units are constructed from the available sampled data. Through comparative analysis, we show that the multilayer perceptron model provides an acceptable approximation of this dynamical system, capturing the potentially nonlinear phenomena in its input-output behavior.

</details>


### [74] [Periodic solutions of nonlinear control systems with switching: a Lie-algebraic and contraction approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20935)
*Alexander Zuyev,Peter Benner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 分析具有bang-bang控制的非线性仿射控制系统的周期解，应用于化学反应性能优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有约束输入的周期最优控制问题，特别是在化学反应性能优化中的重要应用

Method: 将周期解构造问题转化为指数映射复合的不动点问题，使用Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff-Dynkin公式处理，建立原始控制系统与涉及迭代李括号的自治系统周期解的等价性

Result: 在平面情况下，应用Bendixson-Dulac定理将问题进一步简化为寻找自治系统的平衡点

Conclusion: 所发展的理论成功应用于具有约束控制的非线性化学反应模型

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the analysis of periodic solutions of nonlinear control-affine systems with bang-bang controls. Such problems naturally arise in periodic optimal control with constrained inputs, which have, in particular, important applications in the performance optimization of chemical reactions. We reduce the problem of constructing a periodic solution to that of finding a fixed point of a composition of exponential maps. The latter problem is then addressed using the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff-Dynkin (BCHD) formula. We establish the equivalence between periodic solutions of the original control system and those of an associated autonomous system involving iterated Lie brackets. In the planar case, applying the Bendixson-Dulac theorem allows us to further simplify the problem to finding the equilibria of this autonomous system. The developed theory is then applied to nonlinear chemical reaction models with constrained controls.

</details>


### [75] [Mean field control with stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21204)
*Pierre Cardaliaguet,Joe Jackson,Panagiotis E. Souganidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究高维随机优化问题，结合控制和停止机制，通过均值场极限得到具有跳跃动态的控制问题，建立拟变分不等式理论框架并证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时包含控制和停止机制的高维随机优化问题，其中中心规划者可以引导大量粒子并在任意时刻通过支付惩罚移除粒子。这类问题在金融、资源分配和风险管理中有广泛应用，需要建立理论框架处理不连续动态和粒子交互。

Method: 采用均值场极限方法，将N粒子问题转化为具有跳跃动态的均值场控制问题。建立非线性障碍问题层次结构描述N粒子值函数，引入拟变分不等式的粘性解概念，获得比较原理和正则性估计。

Result: 证明了均值场值函数是拟变分不等式的唯一粘性解，建立了N粒子值函数向均值场值函数的收敛性，获得了比较原理和正则性估计等理论结果。

Conclusion: 成功建立了同时包含控制和停止机制的高维随机优化问题的均值场理论框架，证明了收敛性结果，为处理具有不连续动态的粒子系统优化问题提供了数学工具。

Abstract: We study a high-dimensional stochastic optimization problem which features both control and stopping. In particular, a central planner steers a large population of particles, and can also remove particles at any time by paying a penalty. In the limit, we obtain a mean field control problem with discontinuous dynamics, in the sense that the controlled Fokker-Planck equation can have jumps. The value of the N-particle problem is characterized by a hierarchy of non-linear obstacle problems. The value of the limiting problem, meanwhile, solves an infinite-dimensional quasi-variational inequality (QVI). We introduce a notion of viscosity solution for this QVI, and obtain a comparison principle. Together with various regularity estimates, this comparison principle allows us to characterize the mean field value function as the unique viscosity solution of the QVI, and to establish the convergence of the N-particle value functions to the mean field value function.

</details>


### [76] [Two-stage robust bilevel optimization model for facility location considering operational service level under disruption risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21225)
*Haitao Hu,Jing Li,Jiafu Tang,Bo Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层设施选址问题提出了一种新的两阶段鲁棒优化模型，开发了增强的列与约束生成算法，解决了决策依赖不确定性问题，相比集中式模型更关注需求满足和服务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层设施选址问题是许多分散工业系统设计的核心优化问题，如供应链系统中供应商建设设施服务零售商，零售商独立处理客户需求。传统基于场景的随机方法在数据不足或场景数量指数增长时变得不切实际，因此需要采用鲁棒优化方法。

Method: 采用鲁棒优化方法，提出新颖的两阶段鲁棒双层设施选址模型。推导了多个结构性质以改进理论理解和求解效率。基于此，开发了增强的列与约束生成算法，用于处理决策依赖不确定性的鲁棒双层优化问题。

Result: 数值结果表明，相比集中式两阶段鲁棒优化模型，新模型更关注需求满足，通常带来更高的服务效率和更好的供应能力利用率。在小规模中断下，新模型提供更好的服务性能；但在大规模中断下，集中式模型表现更有效。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两阶段鲁棒双层设施选址模型和增强算法有效解决了决策依赖不确定性问题，在服务效率方面优于传统方法，但不同中断规模下需要权衡选择集中式或分散式模型。

Abstract: The bilevel facility location problem (BO-FLP) is one of the core optimization problems behind the design of many decentralized industrial systems, e.g., supply chain systems where a supplier constructs some critical facilities and then uses them to serve retailers in a cost-effective fashion, while retailers directly handle customers aiming to minimize the total unmet demand in a rather independent fashion. When uncertainty is considered, scenario-based stochastic approaches are commonly used, but they often become impractical due to insufficient data or an exponential number of scenarios. To address this issue, this paper adopts robust optimization and proposes a novel two-stage robust bilevel facility location model. Several structural properties are derived to improve both theoretical understanding and solution efficiency. Based on this, an enhanced column-and-constraint generation algorithm is developed for robust bilevel optimization with decision-dependent uncertainty, significantly improving exact solution capability over the standard method. Numerical results show that, compared to the centralized two-stage RO model, our model pays more attention to demand fulfillment, typically resulting in higher service efficiency and better utilization of supply capacity. Under a small-scale disruption, this new model delivers better service performance. However, under a large-scale disruption, the centralized model performs more effectively.

</details>


### [77] [A Modular Approach to Stochastic Optimisation for Inverse Problems Using the Core Imaging Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21230)
*Evangelos Papoutsellis,Margaret A. G. Duff,Jakob S. Jørgensen,Sam Porter,Claire Delplancke,Gemma Fardell,Edoardo Pasca,Kris Thielemans*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CIL扩展了随机优化模块，为成像逆问题提供模块化随机优化框架，支持灵活组合算法组件，适用于大规模CT、PET等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIL框架缺乏专门的随机优化支持，而随机方法在大规模成像逆问题中具有显著计算优势，需要模块化、可扩展的框架来支持研究社区开发和测试随机算法。

Method: 开发了CIL的随机优化扩展框架，采用模块化设计提供可组合的构建块（如采样策略、步长策略、优化算法组件），而非固定的黑盒实现。支持灵活配置算法组件，适应不同问题结构，实现多种随机优化算法实例。

Result: 成功实现了模块化随机优化框架，能够支持多种现有随机优化算法，并允许用户通过组合模块构建新算法。在真实世界X射线CT和PET重建数据集上验证了框架的实用性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 该扩展为研究社区提供了强大、可扩展的随机优化套件，支持开发、测试和基准测试逆问题的随机方法，特别适用于大规模成像应用，填补了CIL框架在随机优化方面的空白。

Abstract: The Core Imaging Library (CIL) is an open-source versatile Python framework for solving inverse problems with special emphasis on imaging applications such as computed tomography (CT), using a plug-in architecture for data and operators, interfacing to toolboxes such as ASTRA, TIGRE and SIRF. A key component of CIL is its optimisation module enabling users to flexibly combine mathematical operators and functionals to form smooth and non-smooth optimisation problems and solve these with a range of first-order algorithms. The present work introduces an expansion of CIL with a new modular framework for stochastic optimisation, allowing researchers to easily use a variety of existing stochastic optimisation algorithms as well form new ones by combining modular building blocks. Users can flexibly configure algorithmic components, adapt to diverse problem structures, and experiment with various sampling and step size strategies. Rather than individual black-box implementations of each fixed algorithm with significant redundancies, our design is modular providing building blocks that can be flexibly combined to realise a wealth of algorithm instances. The framework is particularly well-suited for large-scale applications, where stochastic methods offer notable computational advantages over deterministic approaches. To demonstrate its versatility and practical utility, we present experiments on real-world datasets from imaging inverse problems, such as X-Ray CT and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) reconstruction. In summary, the presented software expansion aims to support the research community with a robust, extensible optimisation suite for developing, testing, and benchmarking stochastic methods for inverse problems.

</details>


### [78] [Theorem of Alternative for Extended Homogeneous Linear System and its Application in Conic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21500)
*Yurii Nesterov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Gordan择一定理构造锥优化不可行启动原始-对偶方法的新框架，通过最小化带线性等式约束的障碍函数来获得指定精度ε的近似解，并分析了阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法的多项式时间复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为锥优化问题开发一种新的不可行启动原始-对偶方法框架，能够从任意初始点开始，在保证数值精度的同时获得多项式时间复杂性，并能利用热启动机会提高效率。

Method: 基于Gordan择一定理，将锥优化问题转化为最小化带线性等式约束的障碍函数的辅助凸问题。给定目标对偶间隙上界ε作为唯一输入参数，通过求解该辅助问题获得原始和对偶问题的近似解。分析了两种优化方案：阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法，均能保证线性等式约束残差达到机器精度水平。

Result: 证明了两种方法（阻尼牛顿法和短步路径跟踪法）都具有多项式时间复杂性，能够从任意初始点开始求解，且能保证线性等式约束残差达到机器精度，与输入参数ε无关。方法还能从热启动中获益。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Gordan择一定理的锥优化不可行启动原始-对偶方法新框架，通过构造辅助凸优化问题，实现了多项式时间复杂性和高数值精度，为锥优化问题提供了有效的求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a new framework for constructing infeasible-start primal-dual methods for Conic Optimization. Our approach can be seen as an unambiguous consequence of Gordan Theorem of Alternative. Given by the target upper bound $ε> 0$ for the duality gap as the only input parameter, we form an auxiliary convex problem of minimizing barrier function with linear equality constraints. Its solution can be easily transformed to the requested output. This function can be minimized by different schemes of Unconstrained Optimization, with possible quadratic convergence in the end of the process. In our paper, we analyze the Damped Newton Method and a short-step path-following scheme. For both of them, we prove polynomial-time complexity results. Our methods are able to benefit from the hot-start opportunities. We can ensure the residual of the linear equality constraints in the primal and dual problems to be on the level of machine accuracy, independently on the input parameter $ε$.

</details>


### [79] [Optimal local linear convergence of Nesterov's accelerated gradient method for $C^2$ functions under the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21516)
*Zixu Feng,Hao Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Nesterov加速梯度法在满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式的C²函数上能达到最优局部线性收敛率，且分析仅需C²光滑性而非更高阶条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对Nesterov加速梯度法局部收敛性的分析通常需要目标函数具有高于C²的光滑性，或要求局部极小值点集具有额外的几何正则性。本文旨在消除这些限制，在更弱的条件下建立最优收敛率。

Method: 采用两阶段论证框架：首先建立粗糙但有效的局部线性收敛率，然后基于这一先验收敛保证，对线性化迭代算子进行精细刻画，从而得到最优收敛率。分析仅需C²光滑性，无需更高阶条件。

Result: 证明了Nesterov加速梯度法在满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式的C²函数上能达到最优局部线性收敛率ρ = (√(3L+μ)-2√μ)/(√(3L+μ)) + ε，其中ε为任意小常数。同时恢复了连续时间Heavy Ball动力学在相同条件下的最优局部指数收敛率√μ。

Conclusion: 该工作显著降低了Nesterov加速梯度法局部收敛性分析所需的条件，仅需C²光滑性而非更高阶光滑性，且无需局部极小值点集的额外几何假设。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In this work, we establish that Nesterov's accelerated gradient method, applied to $C^2$ functions satisfying the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality around local minimizers, achieves the optimal local linear convergence rate $ρ=\frac{\sqrt{3L+μ}-2\sqrtμ}{\sqrt{3L+μ}}+\varepsilon$, where $\varepsilon$ is an arbitrarily small constant. Our analysis requires neither higher-order smoothness beyond $C^2$ of the objective function nor any additional geometric regularity of the submanifold of local minimizers. The key novelty lies in a two-stage argument: we first establish a coarse yet valid local linear convergence rate and then, building upon this a priori convergence guarantee, obtain a refined characterization of the linearized iteration operator, which yields the optimal rate. As a result, we only need to slightly strengthen the standard $C^{1,1}$ assumption, which is commonly required in theoretical analyses of linear convergence for first-order methods, to $C^2$ smoothness. Moreover, the same analytical framework allows us to recover, under identical conditions, the optimal local exponential convergence rate $\sqrtμ$ for the continuous-time Heavy Ball dynamics. Finally, a representative numerical experiment corroborates our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [80] [Sinkhorn algorithms for entropic vector quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21554)
*Kengo Kato,Boyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种用于向量分位数回归的Sinkhorn型算法，建立了线性收敛性，并推导了显式定量界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量分位数回归是经典线性分位数回归的向量值响应变量扩展，基于最优传输框架。现有方法需要求解隐式函数方程，计算上不实用，需要开发更高效的计算算法。

Method: 提出了两种Sinkhorn型算法：1) 基于求解完整薛定谔型系统的经典Sinkhorn迭代的直接适应；2) 新的改进方案，用投影梯度步替换隐式更新。两种算法都基于熵正则化，适用于紧支撑边际分布。

Result: 对于两种算法，在一般紧支撑边际分布下，建立了对偶目标值和迭代的线性收敛性。关键创新在于推导了对偶势和Sinkhorn迭代的显式定量界。

Conclusion: 本文为向量分位数回归提供了两种实用的Sinkhorn型算法，并建立了严格的收敛性理论，特别是第二种算法在计算上更实用，为最优传输框架下的统计推断提供了有效的计算工具。

Abstract: Vector quantile regression (VQR) is an optimal transport (OT)-based framework that extends linear quantile regression to vector-valued response variables and can be formulated as an OT problem with a mean-independence constraint. In this paper, we study two Sinkhorn-type algorithms for VQR with entropic regularization, building on our previous work on its duality theory. The first is a direct adaptation of the classical Sinkhorn iteration based on solving the full Schrödinger-type system characterizing the dual potentials, which requires solving an implicit functional equation at each iteration. The second algorithm, which is new in the literature, replaces the implicit update with a projected gradient step, resulting in a modified scheme that is computationally more practical. For both algorithms, and for general compactly supported marginals, we establish linear convergence in both the dual objective value and the iterates. A key innovation in our analysis is the derivation of explicit quantitative bounds on the dual potentials and Sinkhorn iterates.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化中通信成本高且存在隐私泄露风险，需要设计同时支持压缩通信和差分隐私保护的算法，在保证收敛性能的同时降低通信开销并保护数据隐私

Method: 提出压缩分布式随机梯度下降算法，支持包含相对和绝对压缩误差的通用压缩算子，算法在非凸函数下找到一阶稳定点，在满足P-Ł条件时收敛到最优解，并能实现(0,δ)-差分隐私

Result: 算法在平滑非凸函数下实现线性加速收敛率O(1/√(nT))，在满足P-Ł条件时收敛率为O(1/(nT^θ))，当已知P-Ł常数时可达O(1/(nT))，且能实现差分隐私而不牺牲收敛精度

Conclusion: 所提算法能有效解决分布式随机非凸优化问题，在压缩通信和差分隐私约束下仍保持良好收敛性能，为实际分布式学习系统提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: This paper studies distributed stochastic nonconvex optimization problems with compressed communication and differential privacy, in which each agent aims to minimize the sum of all agents' cost functions by using local compressed information exchange. To this end, we propose a compressed distributed stochastic gradient descent algorithm, which is robust under a general class of compression operators that allow both relative and absolute compression errors. We then show that the proposed algorithm finds the first-order stationary point for smooth nonconvex functions with the linear speedup convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{nT})$ and converges to the optimum if the global cost function additionally satisfies the Polyak--Łojasiewicz (P--Ł) condition with the convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/(nT^θ)),θ\in(0,1)$, where $T$ is the total number of iterations and $n$ is the number of agents. Furthermore, if the P--Ł~constant is known in advance, we show that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(1/(nT))$. Finally, we show that the proposed algorithm is able to achieve $(0,δ)$-differential privacy without sacrificing convergence accuracy. Numerical experiments are carried out to

</details>


### [82] [Nonlinear Control Synchronization Method for Fractional-order Time Derivatives Chaotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21747)
*Vivek Mishra,S. K. Agrawal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数阶时间导数系统的非线性控制同步技术，使用Adams-Basford-Moulton方法求解分数阶系统，并验证了两个混沌系统同步的可靠性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步现象在物理、工程、生物、经济等多个领域广泛存在，并在神经科学和通信系统中具有重要应用价值。研究分数阶时间导数系统的同步控制技术对于理解和应用这些复杂系统的同步行为具有重要意义。

Method: 采用非线性控制同步技术处理分数阶时间导数系统，使用Adams-Basford-Moulton数值方法求解分数阶系统方程，通过数值模拟验证方法的可靠性。

Result: 成功实现了两个混沌系统的同步，同时保持系统处于混沌状态。研究发现同步时间和导数重排对同步过程具有显著影响，数值模拟证明了该方法的可靠性和易用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分数阶时间导数系统的非线性控制同步方法是有效的，能够实现混沌系统的同步控制，同步时间和导数重排是影响同步过程的关键因素，该方法在实际应用中具有可行性。

Abstract: "Synchronization of two dynamical systems" is the term used to describe the phenomenon when two or more systems gradually change their states or behaviors to become similar or identical. This can happen in a lot of fields, such as physics, engineering, biology, and economics. Synchronization finds applications in neurology and communication systems. It is present in both man-made and organic systems. The nonlinear control synchronization technique for fractional-order time derivative systems is described in this article, where the Adams Basford Moulton method is used for solving the fractional-order system. The reliability and ease of applicability for two chaotic systems are demonstrated by the numerical simulation. Furthermore, in this article, both systems were kept in a chaotic condition while being synchronized with each other. The effects of synchronizing time and rearranging the derivatives are the most significant sections of this article.

</details>


### [83] [Safe and wind-aware synchronous path planning for a fleet of fixed-wing constant speed aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21789)
*Maël Feurgard,Gautier Hattenberger,Nicolas Durand,Simon Lacroix*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Dubins路径枚举和整数线性规划的集中式方法，用于解决多固定翼无人机编队飞行模式转换的路径规划问题，确保同步到达、最小间隔和巡航速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机路径规划本身就很困难，特别是对于固定翼飞机编队。编队飞行模式转换需要同步到达、确保最小间隔并保持巡航速度，这增加了问题的复杂性。

Method: 基于Dubins路径枚举的集中式方法：给定编队飞行时间，为每架飞机构建可能的路径集合；并行检查每对路径之间的间隔；将结果转化为整数线性规划问题的系数，判断是否存在全编队无冲突解；通过递增分辨率采样不同飞行时间重复此过程，直到达到用户定义的精度。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟对多达20架飞机同时进行基准测试，平均计算时间8秒内达到95%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决多固定翼无人机编队飞行模式转换的路径规划问题，在合理计算时间内实现高成功率。

Abstract: Path planning for multiple unmanned aerial vehicles is a difficult task, and even more for a fleet of fixed-wing aircraft. One specific case is the transition to, or between, formation flight patterns, which requires synchronous arrivals while ensuring minimal separation, and ideally maintaining cruise speed. We present a centralized method to solve this problem based on enumerating different Dubins paths. Given a travel time for the fleet, it builds a set of possible paths for each aircraft. Then, it checks in parallel separation between each path pair. This yields coefficients for an Integer Linear Programming problem determining if a fleet-wide conflict-free solution exists. This process is repeated for different travel times sampled with increasing resolution until the user-defined accuracy is met. The method is benchmarked with Monte-Carlo simulations considering up to 20 aircraft simultaneously, achieving an 95% success rate for an average 8 seconds computation time.

</details>


### [84] [A Moser-Type Construction for the Liouville Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21850)
*Alfio Borzì,Marco Caponigro,Arianna Vicari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将Moser体积形式引理推广到动力学刘维尔方程，建立了相空间密度在单位时间内通过刘维尔方程连接的条件和构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个光滑正相空间密度能否通过刘维尔方程在单位时间内相互转换的问题，扩展经典的Moser体积形式引理到动力学系统。

Method: 通过速度边缘分布的自然相容性条件，构造了一族显式的力场，该构造基于速度变量的加权椭圆问题。

Result: 证明了两个相空间密度可通过刘维尔方程在单位时间内连接的充要条件是速度边缘分布满足相容性条件，并给出了数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 成功将Moser引理推广到动力学刘维尔方程，为相空间密度插值提供了理论框架和构造性方法。

Abstract: We present a novel extension of Moser's volume form lemma to the kinetic Liouville equation. In particular, we show that two smooth, positive phase-space densities $f$ and $g$ can be connected in unit time by the Liouville equation if and only if a natural compatibility condition on velocity marginals is satisfied. Under this condition, an explicit family of force fields is constructed via a weighted elliptic problem in the velocity variable. Results of numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical framework.

</details>


### [85] [Towards sparse optimization over convex loops: Equivalence of Square Root Velocity distance and Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22009)
*Giacomo Cristinelli,José A. Iglesias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明了在凸绝对连续平面曲线情形下，SRVT诱导的形状距离等价于WFR距离，并研究了WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao距离在S²上与经典弹性形状距离一致，但在S¹上这种对应关系不成立，特别是对于凸曲线，现有二维论证无法推导。本文旨在建立凸绝对连续平面曲线的SRVT形状距离与WFR距离之间的等价关系，并探索WFR正则化形状优化中的稀疏性。

Method: 利用圆环通用覆盖上的最优传输单调性原理，证明存在达到SRVT下确界的最优重参数化，从而将问题转化为一维非平衡最优运输问题。对于WFR正则化形状优化，研究相应球的线性优化，证明当参考测度离散时的有限性结果，并提出适用于条件梯度算法的凸正齐次正则化器。

Result: 证明了对于平面上的凸绝对连续S¹浸入，SRVT诱导的形状距离等价于作用于其关联长度测度的WFR距离。对于WFR正则化形状优化，当参考测度离散时，线性优化具有有限性，并提出了有效的正则化器。

Conclusion: 建立了凸平面曲线的SRVT形状距离与WFR距离的等价性，为形状分析提供了新的理论联系。同时，WFR距离在形状优化中表现出良好的稀疏性特性，为相关算法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) distance on $S^{2}$ has recently been shown to coincide with a classical elastic distance between $S^{2}$-immersions in the theory of Riemannian shape analysis. While this correspondence holds in dimension $2$, the analogous statement fails in general on $S^{1}$ and, in the case of convex curves, it cannot be derived from existing two-dimensional arguments. In this paper, we establish that for convex absolutely continuous immersions of $S^{1}$ in the plane, the shape distance induced by the square root velocity transformation (SRVT) is indeed equivalent to the WFR distance acting on their associated length measures. The proof exploits a monotonicity principle for optimal transport on the universal cover of the circle, which in turn guarantees the existence of an optimal reparametrization achieving the SRVT infimum and enables a one-dimensional unbalanced optimal transport reformulation. Motivated by this equivalence, we further investigate the role of sparsity in shape optimization problems formulated in terms of length measures and regularized by the WFR distance. We study linear optimization over the corresponding balls, for which we prove a finiteness result when the reference measure is discrete, and propose a convex, positively one-homogeneous regularizer suitable for conditional gradient algorithms.

</details>


### [86] [Here, there and everywhere: state-dependent time-inconsistent stochastic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22022)
*Dylan Possamaï,Mateo Rodriguez Polo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续时间框架下的时间不一致随机控制问题，通过建立BSDE系统来表征均衡值函数，并以时间不一致线性二次调节器为例进行应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续时间框架下时间不一致随机控制问题的理论挑战，特别是当目标函数依赖于当前状态导致时间不一致性时，需要建立合适的数学框架来表征均衡解。

Method: 采用概率论方法，建立了一个后向随机微分方程(BSDE)系统，该系统能够表征时间不一致控制问题的均衡值函数。

Result: 成功推导出BSDE系统作为时间不一致随机控制问题均衡值函数的概率表示，并将理论应用于时间不一致线性二次调节器问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为连续时间框架下的时间不一致随机控制问题提供了有效的概率表示方法，通过BSDE系统成功表征了均衡值函数，并在经典控制问题中得到验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of time-inconsistent stochastic control within a continuous-time framework. Its primary focus lies in uncovering a probabilistic representation, specifically in the shape of a system of backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs). These equations encapsulate the equilibrium value function essential for resolving cases where the present state affecting the target functional triggers the inconsistency. Additionally, the paper offers an application exemplifying this theory through the time-inconsistent linear--quadratic regulator.

</details>


### [87] [Distributionally robust optimization for recommendation selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22090)
*Tomoya Yanagi,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Ken Kobayashi,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的推荐系统框架，用于在保持准确性的同时提高推荐多样性，通过惩罚交替方向法（PADM）高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统虽然能达到高准确性，但往往忽视推荐多样性对用户满意度的影响。基于金融投资组合理论的方法对评分统计的估计误差敏感，需要更稳健的解决方案。

Method: 建立了基于矩模糊集的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架，提出惩罚交替方向法（PADM）高效求解带基数约束的DRO模型，并证明了算法的收敛性。

Result: 在三个公开评分数据集上的实验表明，DRO模型在保持相同准确性的前提下，比现有模型产生更多样化的推荐，且每个用户的推荐计算仅需几秒钟。

Conclusion: 该研究建立的DRO框架能够有效平衡推荐准确性和多样性，为各种协同过滤方法提升推荐质量提供了潜力。

Abstract: Recommender systems play an essential role in online services by providing personalized item lists to support users' decision-making processes. While collaborative filtering methods can achieve high accuracy, it is crucial to consider not only accuracy but also the diversity of recommended items to improve user satisfaction. Although financial portfolio theory has been applied to balance these factors, existing models are often sensitive to estimation errors in rating statistics. To overcome these challenges, we establish a computational framework of distributionally robust optimization (DRO) for recommendation selection. We first formulate a cardinality-constrained DRO model based on moment-based ambiguity sets to select a specified number of items for each user. We then design a penalty alternating direction method (PADM) to efficiently compute high-quality solutions and prove its convergence properties. Computational experiments using three publicly available rating datasets demonstrate that our DRO model generates more diverse recommendations than existing models while maintaining the same level of accuracy. Additionally, our solution method computes these recommendations for each user in just a few seconds, proving its practical effectiveness. This study establishes a DRO framework that has the potential to enhance the recommendation quality of various collaborative filtering methods.

</details>

### [88] [Degeneracy in Accretion Disk Spectra from Naked Singularities and Kerr Black Holes: Application to the AGN MCG-06-30-15](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20282)
*Vishva Patel,Sayantan Bhattacharya,Sudip Bhattacharyya,Pankaj S. Joshi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了利用吸积盘光谱区分黑洞和裸奇点的可行性，发现克尔黑洞与JMN-1裸奇点在光谱拟合上存在简并性，可能导致自旋测量错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力坍缩可能形成黑洞或裸奇点，但缺乏区分两者的观测特征。研究旨在探索相对论性吸积盘光谱是否能提供这种区分测试。

Method: 构建了JMN-1裸奇点几何结构（匹配施瓦西外部）的薄吸积盘加法表模型，使用NuSTAR X射线数据拟合MCG-06-30-15活动星系核，并与标准克尔和施瓦西黑洞模型比较，同时包含相对论反射光谱成分relxill。

Result: 自旋的克尔黑洞与非自旋的JMN-1裸奇点提供相似的光谱拟合结果，且都显著优于施瓦西黑洞的拟合。这种简并性可能导致基于吸积盘光谱的坍缩天体自旋测量错误。

Conclusion: 吸积盘光谱在区分克尔黑洞和裸奇点方面存在简并性，需要独立的自旋测量来打破这种简并并识别裸奇点。这一发现对引力波源和X射线双星中测量的坍缩天体自旋分布有重要意义。

Abstract: Theoretical studies suggest that gravitational collapse can form either a black hole or a visible (naked) singularity. Identifying observational signatures that distinguish these two types of collapsed objects is a holy grail of physics. Here, we examine whether relativistic accretion disk spectra can provide such a test. We construct an additive table model for a thin accretion disk in the Joshi-Malafarina-Narayan (JMN-1) naked singularity geometry matched to a Schwarzschild exterior and fit it to NuSTAR X-ray data from the AGN MCG-06-30-15. Our results are compared with standard Kerr and Schwarzschild black hole models. We also include the relativistic reflection spectral component relxill. Despite their different underlying geometries, the spinning (Kerr) black hole and the non-spinning JMN-1 naked singularity provide similar spectral fits, which are significantly better fits than the Schwarzschild black hole. This degeneracy between the naked singularity and the Kerr black hole could lead to incorrect spin measurements of collapsed objects using disk spectra. The degeneracy could be broken with an independent spin measurement, which could also help identify a naked singularity. Our results could also have a role in different spin distributions of collapsed objects measured from gravitational-wave sources and X-ray binaries.

Method: 使用TDECat光学TDE目录，采用基于TDE瞬态特性的时空关联算法，并模拟了两组与中微子具有不同关联程度的TDE样本来进一步研究统计相关性。

Conclusion: 光学TDE与高能中微子无统计关联，但未来LSST巡天和下一代中微子观测站（KM3NET、IceCube-Gen2）将扩大样本量，有助于进一步澄清两者的潜在关联。

Abstract: High-energy (HE) neutrinos have been observed by the IceCube (IC) Neutrino observatory for over a decade. Nevertheless, the astrophysical origin and the responsible mechanisms producing these HE neutrinos are still a mystery, with many astrophysical phenomena as potential emitters. A plethora of previous studies have attempted to study the correlation between HE neutrinos and active galactic nuclei, finding inconclusive results. Tidal disruption events (TDEs) have been proposed as candidate HE neutrino emitters, yet there is only one prior statistical study for the correlation of the two due to the limited number of observed TDEs. For this reason we used TDECat, an optical TDE repository, to investigate the potential association of TDEs with IceCube HE neutrino events. We implemented a spatio-temporal algorithm, where the temporal constraint is based on the transient nature of TDEs. We also simulated two sets of TDEs, correlated differently with neutrinos, to further study their statistical correlation. Despite the individual cases of TDE AT2019dsg and AT2021lo, we find no statistical association between optical TDEs and HE neutrinos. We find jetted TDE Sw J2058+05 to be spatio-temporally associated with a neutrino event. However, a $γ$-ray-flaring, flat-spectrum radio quasar is also within the neutrino's sky error region. Although our findings indicate no statistical correlation between optical TDEs and HE neutrinos, this correlation should be further studied in the future. Upcoming surveys such as the Legacy Survey of Space and Time, coupled with next-generation neutrino observatories, such as KM3NET and IceCube-Gen2, will expand both TDE and HE neutrino populations, clarifying their potential correlation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前暗物质间接探测模型通常只考虑伽马射线在传播过程中的衰减（主要通过河外背景光上的电子对产生），但忽略了由此产生的电子和正电子后续的逆康普顿散射效应，这种效应可以再生高能伽马射线。这种简化可能导致对观测伽马射线通量的预测不准确，进而影响暗物质限制的可靠性。

Method: 重新审视WIMP湮灭的伽马射线通量预测，采用更详细的传播效应处理方法。特别关注电子和正电子在传播过程中的逆康普顿散射效应，这种效应可以再生高能伽马射线。研究考虑了不同距离的源、不同WIMP质量以及不同湮灭通道（如τ⁺τ⁻通道）。

Result: 对于遥远源和特定湮灭通道（如τ⁺τ⁻），完整的传播效应处理可以显著改变观测到的伽马射线通量，对于重WIMP，改变幅度可达三个数量级。这表明不考虑传播效应的现有暗物质限制需要重新评估，具体影响取决于所考虑的WIMP质量和湮灭通道。

Conclusion: 详细的传播效应处理对于暗物质间接探测至关重要。为了获得准确、更可靠的暗物质信号预测和排除限制，必须考虑包括逆康普顿散射再生效应在内的完整传播过程。当前基于简化传播模型的暗物质限制需要重新评估。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate dark matter (DM) detection in the context of weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs). Upon annihilation, WIMPs generate cascades of secondary particles through various channels, many of which culminate in the production of gamma rays. As these gamma rays travel toward Earth, their spectra are reshaped by interactions with the intervening medium. While current models typically account for attenuation via pair production on the extragalactic background light, they often neglect the fate of the resulting electrons and positrons, specifically subsequent inverse Compton scattering of these secondary particles, which can regenerate high-energy gamma rays. Here, we revisit the predicted gamma-ray fluxes from WIMP annihilation by performing a more detailed treatment of propagation effects. We show that for distant sources and annihilation channels such as $τ^+τ^-$, the full treatments can significantly alter the observed gamma-ray flux, by up to a factor of three orders of magnitude for heavy WIMPs. This has an impact on current dark matter limits derived without taking into account propagation effects, depending on the considered WIMP mass and annihilation channel. Our study demonstrates the importance of a detailed propagation treatment for indirect dark matter searches, and the need to account for such effects in order to obtain accurate, more reliable dark matter signal predictions and exclusion limits.

</details>


### [91] [Accurate and efficient simulation-based inference for massive black-hole binaries with LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20431)

Method: 在DINGO代码中实现模拟推理框架，使用对齐自旋高阶模波形模型和探测器响应的低频近似训练归一化流模型，通过重要性采样获得真实后验分布。

Conclusion: DINGO成为LISA波段大质量黑洞双星快速全参数估计的有前景的神经推理框架，其无似然特性便于扩展到时变探测器响应、非平稳噪声等复杂情况。

Abstract: We develop an accurate simulation-based inference framework for high-mass ($\gtrsim\!10^7 \rm{M_\odot}$) black-hole binaries observable by LISA. The method is implemented within the DINGO gravitational-wave parameter-estimation code, extending its application from ground-based detectors to the LISA band. We train a normalizing-flow model using aligned-spin higher-mode waveform models and a low-frequency approximation of the detector response. After sampling, we importance-sample to the true posterior. We validate performance on simulated signals spanning the signal-to-noise regimes relevant for LISA observations and benchmark our new DINGO implementation against standard methods. We report robust agreement in the inferred posterior distributions up to signal-to-noise ratios of $\sim\!500$. At higher signal-to-noise ratios of $\sim\!1000$, we observe a reduction in sampling efficiency, while still yielding unbiased and tightly localized posteriors that can be used as a starting point for follow-up with traditional methods.The trained flow can generate 20 thousand posterior samples in less than a minute, establishing DINGO as a promising neural inference framework for rapid full-parameter estimation of massive black-hole binaries in the LISA band. The likelihood-free nature of this approach allows for straightforward generalizations, including a time-dependent detector response, non-stationary noise artifacts such as gaps and glitches, and low-latency parameter estimations.

</details>

<details>

Abstract: Context. Gravitational waves (GW) provide a unique probe of the explosion mechanism of massive stars and the evolution of nascent proto-neutron stars (PNS). Magnetorotational explosions are one of the promising non-canonical core-collapse supernova scenarios, possibly linked to magnetar formation and energetic supernova explosions. However, the GW signatures of such events remain incompletely understood presently. Aims. This study investigates the origin and nature of gravitational-wave polarization arising from a magnetorotational core-collapse model and examines its potential detectability by current gravitational-wave observatories. Methods. We perform a three-dimensional simulation of general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics of a rapidly rotating, strongly magnetized 20 M$_\odot$ progenitor, including multi-energy neutrino transport. The polarization states of the GW signals are analyzed with Stokes parameters. Results. We find that strong circular polarization emerges along the rotation axis during the early post-bounce phase (<230 ms after core bounce). The characteristic GW spectrum peaks at ~90 Hz, consistent with the emission at twice the local angular velocity (~45 Hz) around the PNS surface at cylindrical radii of ~50 km. These features are attributed to the low-T/|W| instabilities and non-axisymmetric motions near the PNS, rather than to the magnetohydrodynamic jets themselves. The polarization signals lie within the sensitivity bands of current GW detectors. Conclusions. Our study demonstrates that models launching magnetorotationally driven jets can produce circularly polarized GW signals originating from the inner PNS region. This provides an observational signature that complements previous findings from non-magnetized rotating models. Thus, our novel findings establish that the GW polarization is a promising diagnostic of non-canonical core-collapse supernovae.

TL;DR: 对FRB 20240114A的偏振测量显示其法拉第旋转量随时间演化，而色散量保持稳定，表明该重复快速射电暴源处于动态演化的磁离子环境中。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: FRB 20240114A处于动态演化的磁离子环境中，其发射机制基本保持不变，但发射几何结构复杂。该偏振目录为研究重复快速射电暴的前身星及其环境提供了基础。

Abstract: Polarization measurements of fast radio bursts (FRBs) probe the magnetized plasma surrounding their central engines. FRB~20240114A is an exceptionally active repeating source, with 17,356 bursts detected between 2024 January 28 and 2025 May 30 by FAST, enabling time-resolved polarimetric studies. In this work, we present a polarimetric catalog of 6,131 bright bursts (with a signal-to-noise ratio S/N $\geq$ 20, 35.3% of the total sample), including arrival time (MJD$_{\text{topo}}$), dispersion measure (DM), burst width (W$_{\text{eff}}$), bandwidth, Faraday rotation measure (RM), linear and circular polarization degrees (DOL, DOC), and intrinsic polarization angle (PA$_0$). We detect a clear temporal evolution of RM: after an initial stable phase, it decreases linearly by $\sim$200 $\rm rad\ m^{-2}$ over 200 days, forming a bimodal distribution, whereas DM remains stable at 528.9 $\rm pc\ cm^{-3}$. The linear polarization fraction is generally high, with the 3$σ$ lower bound around 76%, while circular polarization is low, with 1,157 of 17,356 bursts (6.67%) having DOC $\geq$10%. We perform a power-law fit between $|\textrm{V}|$/I and $|\textrm{RM}|$, which yields an index of $-2.98 \pm 0.80$. It is found that the combined 2D distribution of L/I versus V/I remains stable, implying that the emission mechanism is largely invariant. Our PA$_0$ measurements show a broad, non-uniform distribution, implying a complex emission geometry. These results suggest that FRB~20240114A resides in a dynamically evolving magneto-ionic environment. This catalog provides a foundation for studies of repeating FRB progenitors and their environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子望远镜为研究高能宇宙提供了独特的观测窗口，能够探索传统高能信使无法触及的宇宙加速器和极端环境。尽管与粒子物理中的中微子实验共享切伦科夫辐射探测等核心原理，但其科学目标和操作约束存在显著差异。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统回顾了天体物理中微子探测的动机，概述了在不同介质和部署环境中的关键设计策略，并分析了中微子望远镜在多信使天文学背景下的作用。

Result: 论文强调了中微子望远镜在揭示宇宙射线起源和探测宇宙中最能量现象驱动机制方面的潜力，确立了其作为多信使天文学关键组成部分的地位。

Conclusion: 中微子望远镜是研究高能宇宙不可或缺的工具，通过与其他信使（如光子、引力波）的协同观测，能够提供对宇宙极端物理过程的全面理解，特别是在探索宇宙射线起源和极高能天体物理现象方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes provide a unique observational gateway to the high-energy universe, enabling the study of cosmic accelerators and extreme environments that remain inaccessible to the other high-energy messengers. Although they share core detection principles with neutrino experiments in particle physics, such as the observation of Cherenkov radiation, their scientific objectives and operational constraints diverge markedly. This paper reviews the motivations behind astrophysical neutrino detection, outlines key design strategies across various media and deployment environments, and highlights the critical role of neutrino telescopes in the context of multimessenger astronomy. In particular, we emphasize their potential to illuminate the origins of cosmic rays and to probe the mechanisms driving the most energetic phenomena in the universe.

</details>

*E. Churazov,I. I. Khabibullin,A. M. Bykov,N. N. Chugai,R. A. Sunyaev,V. P. Utrobin,I. I. Zinchenko*

Abstract: Unlike the complicated X-ray and radio structure observed in the North Polar Spur area, the South-Eastern part of the eROSITA bubbles can be reasonably well described as a propagating forward shock, plausibly created by the transient energy release at the Galactic Center. In this model, the physical radius of the bubble is $R_{\rm b}\sim 7-8\,{\rm kpc}$ and the age of the outburst is $t_{\rm age}\sim 5-8\,{\rm Myr}$. The visible segment of the shock front (located at a distance of $\sim 10-12\,{\rm kpc}$ above the Galactic Disk and at a similar distance from the Sun) is currently expanding with the velocity $\sim 700\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$ through the gas with density $n_e\sim 3\times 10^{-4}\,{\rm cm^{-3}}$, and the abundance of heavy elements in this gas is $Z\lesssim 0.1 \times Z_\odot$. Unlike constraints derived from the line-of-sight-integrated quantities, these are effectively in situ measurements of the circumgalactic medium (CGM) properties. Given the simplifying assumptions used in deriving the density, we assign a factor of 2 systematic uncertainty to the final estimate. An eventual decisive test for the shock properties can be provided by the velocity measurements of the X-ray-emitting gas with soft X-ray bolometers. The extended forward shock propagating through low-metallicity gas is a favorable site to accelerate very high-energy cosmic rays, which might contribute to the observed proton-rich galactic cosmic ray component at PeV energies.

</details>


### [96] [Measurement of the Orbital Parameters, Spin and Spectral Evolution During the Main High State of Her X-1 with Insight-HXMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20768)
*Wen Yang,Wei Wang,Qianhan Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于慧眼卫星观测，对Her X-1在2020年2月的一次完整主高态进行了详细计时分析和光谱演化研究，精确测定了中子星自转参数、轨道参数及其变化率，并分析了X射线光谱参数在主高态期间的演化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Her X-1是一个重要的X射线双星系统，对其计时特性和光谱演化的精确测量有助于理解中子星吸积物理、轨道演化机制以及吸积盘进动等天体物理过程。

Method: 利用慧眼卫星观测数据，通过五个食的Rømer延迟测量确定精确的本地历元，结合文献中的测量值精化轨道历元，分析自转周期、轨道周期及其变化率，并研究主高态期间X射线光谱参数（吸收柱密度、光子指数、回旋吸收线）的演化。

Result: 测得中子星自转周期为1.23765212±0.00000026秒，自转周期变化率为-(1.18±0.04)×10⁻¹³秒/秒；精化轨道历元得到轨道周期为1.7001674990±0.0000000105天，轨道周期变化率为-(1.957±0.335)×10⁻¹¹天/天；主高态期间氢吸收柱密度单调增加，光子指数基本保持不变，回旋吸收线中心能量约38keV且不随光度显著变化。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了Her X-1系统精确的计时参数和光谱演化特征，支持吸积盘进动模型解释超轨道相位的光谱变化，为理解中子星双星系统的吸积物理和轨道演化机制提供了重要观测约束。

Abstract: Based on Insight-HXMT observations, we present a detailed timing analysis and spectral evolution of a complete Main High state for Her X-1 in February 2020. We determine an accurate local ephemeris using the Rømer delay measured from five eclipses. We report the spin period of the neutron star at $P_{\rm spin}=1.23765212 \pm 0.00000026$ s with a spin period derivative of $\dot P_{\rm spin}=-(1.18\pm 0.04)\times 10^{-13}$ s\,s$^{-1}$. By combining the newly measured local values $T_{ecl}$ with those reported in the literature, we refine the orbital ephemeris of Her X-1, obtaining $T_{ecl} = 46359.871956 \pm 0.000010$ MJD and $P_{orb}=1.7001674990 \pm 0.0000000105$ day, then detect a continuous decrease in the orbital period with a rate of $\dot{P}_{\rm orb} = -(1.957 \pm 0.335)\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{d\,d^{-1}}$. We also investigate the evolution of X-ray spectral parameters during the Main High state. The hydrogen absorption column density $N_{\rm H}$ increased monotonously during the phase, and the photon index kept nearly constant. The cyclotron absorption line was detected with a centroid energy around 38 keV, showing no significant evolution with luminosity. The spectral variations with the superorbital phase are discussed within the accretion disk precession scenario.

</details>


### [97] [X-Ray Polarization Study of Pulsar Wind Nebulae with eXTP: Simulation Results and Scientific Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20779)
*Kuan Liu,Fei Xie,Ming-Yu Ge,Wei Deng,En-Wei Liang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 评估eXTP任务对脉冲星风云的偏振观测能力，相比IXPE有约2倍的偏振探测灵敏度提升，能显著扩大可观测的脉冲星风云样本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IXPE的有限有效面积只能观测最亮的脉冲星风云，许多较暗的星云无法研究。需要评估下一代X射线偏振任务eXTP的能力，以扩大偏振观测的脉冲星风云样本

Method: 开发并验证eXTP偏振聚焦阵列的模拟框架，使用修改的IXPEOBSSIM工具，包含适当的响应函数和仪器背景模型。对SNRcat目录中12个不同演化阶段和亮度水平的脉冲星风云进行综合模拟

Result: eXTP相比IXPE在99%置信水平的最小可探测偏振度有约2倍改进。对最亮目标(N157B, G54.1+0.3, Mouse)，1Ms观测能达到4-5%的MDP99。对扩展源如Vela脉冲星风云，显著偏振探测区域几乎是IXPE的两倍

Conclusion: eXTP增强的偏振能力将显著扩大具有可靠X射线偏振测量的脉冲星风云样本，使系统研究不同演化阶段的磁场结构、粒子加速机制和脉冲星风云-环境相互作用成为可能

Abstract: X-ray polarization observations of pulsar wind nebulae (PWNe) provide crucial insights into magnetic field structures and particle acceleration mechanisms. While the Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE) has made significant contributions to PWN studies, its limited effective area restricts observations to only the brightest sources, leaving many fainter nebulae unexplored. We evaluate the polarization capabilities of the enhanced X-ray Timing and Polarimetry mission (eXTP) for studying PWNe and establish a methodology for simulating eXTP Polarimetry Focusing Array (PFA) observations using modified IXPEOBSSIM. We develop and validate a simulation framework with appropriate response functions and instrumental background models, conducting comprehensive simulations of twelve PWNe selected from the SNRcat catalogue across various evolutionary stages and brightness levels. Our simulations demonstrate that eXTP provides approximately a factor of 2 improvement in minimum detectable polarization at the 99\% confidence level (MDP$_{99}$) compared to IXPE. For the brightest targets (N157B, G54.1+0.3, and Mouse), 1 Ms observations achieve MDP$_{99}$ values of 4-5\%. The area with significant polarization detection for extended sources like Vela PWN is nearly twice as large as achievable with IXPE. These enhanced capabilities will significantly expand the sample of PWNe with robust X-ray polarization measurements, enabling systematic studies of magnetic field structures, particle acceleration mechanisms, and PWN-environment interactions across different evolutionary phases.

</details>


### [98] [Modeling surface radiation of rotating neutron stars with Monk-NS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20870)
*Wenda Zhang,Wenfei Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 开发了Monk-NS代码用于模拟旋转中子星的辐射传输，特别关注表面发射被散射介质再处理的情况，并通过X射线偏振观测区分不同物理模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星是研究超密核物质的独特实验室，其物态方程可通过质量和半径有效约束。旋转中子星的强相对论效应会在电磁辐射中留下印记，需要更现实的模型来模拟表面发射被散射介质再处理的情况

Method: 开发了Monk-NS，这是广义相对论蒙特卡洛辐射传输代码Monk的定制版本。通过一系列基准测试验证代码，包括计算旋转中子星的能量谱、脉冲轮廓和偏振，并与X射线计时领域的成熟代码进行比较

Result: 验证测试显示与现有代码结果一致。应用Monk-NS研究解释中子星低质量X射线双星低脉动振幅的各种模型，发现X射线偏振度对观测者倾角的依赖可作为区分这些模型的关键因素，复杂热点形态产生的偏振特性与圆形热点不同

Conclusion: Monk-NS为模拟旋转中子星辐射传输提供了有效工具，特别适用于表面发射被散射介质再处理的复杂情况。X射线偏振观测成为区分不同物理模型的重要诊断工具，复杂热点形态对偏振特性有显著影响

Abstract: Neutron stars serve as unique laboratories for studying ultra-dense nuclear matter. The equation of state of neutron star matter can be effectively constrained by their masses and radii. Particular attention has been paid to rapidly rotating neutron stars, where strong relativistic effects leave imprints on their electromagnetic emission. To model the emission of rotating neutron stars in more realistic situations, especially when their surface emission is further re-processed by a scattering medium, we develop Monk-NS, a customized version of the general relativistic Monte-Carlo radiative transfer code Monk. We validate the code through a series of benchmarking tests, including computing the energy spectrum, pulse profile, and polarisation of rotating neutron stars, and comparing the results with those of the established codes in the X-ray timing community, yielding consistent outcomes. As an example to demonstrate Monk-NS's capabilities, we apply it to investigate various models proposed to explain the low pulsation amplitude of neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries. Our findings indicate that the dependence of the X-ray polarisation degree on the observer's inclination can serve as a key factor in distinguishing these models. We also find that complex hotspot morphologies yield polarisation properties different from those of circular hotspots.

</details>


### [99] [Distributed accelerators in the jet of Centaurus A: the origin of the spectral hardening of very high energy gamma-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21063)
*Y. S. Honda,M. Honda*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 论文提出同步自康普顿（SSC）模型结合丝状喷流结构，解释半人马座A的甚高能γ射线辐射，通过非均匀磁场和分布式激波加速机制实现电子能量超过10^8，并解释H.E.S.S.观测到的能谱硬化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释半人马座A（Cen A）中观测到的甚高能γ射线辐射的起源，特别是H.E.S.S.探测到的能谱硬化现象，以及Fermi望远镜观测到的γ射线通量。

Method: 采用同步自康普顿（SSC）模型结合丝状喷流结构，假设非均匀磁场与不同横向尺寸的电流丝相关。考虑千秒差距尺度喷流中分布式激波加速产生高能电子，电子在弱磁场细丝中被俘获从而抑制同步辐射损失。

Result: 电子最大洛伦兹因子稳定超过10^8，喷流内部非均匀SSC主导贡献于H.E.S.S.观测到的γ射线通量显著硬化。外喷流弥散区域的谱贡献可能达到Fermi观测通量水平。

Conclusion: 丝状喷流模型中的SSC机制能够成功解释半人马座A的甚高能γ射线辐射特征，包括能谱硬化和不同望远镜观测通量，为活动星系核喷流高能辐射机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We propose the synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) scenario coupled with filamentary jet model, to reproduce the very high energy $γ$-ray emissions from Cen A. With reference to self-similarity of knot-like features in the jet, we assume nonuniform magnetic field associated with current filaments having various transverse sizes. For energetic electron production, the diffusive shock acceleration at sites distributed over the kiloparsec-scale jet is considered. We show that maximum Lorentz factor of the electron steadily exceeds $10^{8}$ due to suppression of synchrotron loss of the electrons trapped in weak magnetic field of the thin filaments, and inhomogeneous SSC in the inner jet can dominantly contribute to establishment of the pronounced hardening of $γ$-ray flux detected by the H.E.S.S. It is also suggested that the spectral contribution from diffuse regions of the outer jet potentially amounts to the observed Fermi fluxes.

</details>


### [100] [On the origin of the strong internal magnetic fields of central compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21103)
*Kazım Yavuz Ekşi,İrem Bakır*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出中央致密天体（CCOs）的弱偶极磁场（~10¹⁰ G）来自前身星核心的磁通守恒，其缓慢自转阻止了α效应增强极向场，但Ω效应可在原中子星阶段产生强环向场（~10¹³ G）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释中央致密天体（CCOs）观测到的弱偶极磁场与理论预期的强内部磁场之间的矛盾，并阐明这些天体缓慢自转的成因。

Method: 提出简化模型：1）CCOs的偶极磁场通过磁通守恒继承自前身星核心；2）缓慢自转抑制α效应，无法增强极向场；3）Ω效应在原中子星阶段从种子极向场产生强环向场；4）推测缓慢自转源于未通过回落物质获得角动量。

Result: 模型解释了CCOs观测到的弱偶极磁场（~10¹⁰ G）与内部强环向场（~10¹³ G）并存的现象，并为其缓慢自转提供了物理机制。

Conclusion: CCOs的弱偶极磁场反映了前身星核心的原始磁场，缓慢自转限制了α效应，但Ω效应仍能产生强环向场；这些天体未通过回落物质获得角动量是导致其缓慢自转的关键因素。

Abstract: Central compact objects are radio-quite young neutron stars associated with supernova remnants. They have relatively small dipole fields, $B_{\rm p} \sim 10^{10}\,{\rm G}$ as inferred from their spin parameters. X-ray observations and theoretical arguments imply the presence of stronger internal magnetic fields. We argue that the dipole fields of these objects are very close to what they had inherited from the \textit{core} of the progenitor by flux conservation and their small initial rotation frequency does not allow for the $α$-process to enhance their poloidal fields. Although a full-fledged dynamo process can not proceed, relatively strong toroidal magnetic fields, $B_φ\sim 10^{13}\,{\rm G}$, can be generated from the seed poloidal fields via the $Ω$-effect in the proto-neutron star stage. We present a simplistic model for these processes and further speculate that the reason why these objects are born relatively slow-rotating is that they were not spun-up by acquiring angular momentum from the fallback matter.

</details>


### [101] [The First GeV Gamma-Ray Flares from the CSO-like Source 4C 76.03](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21110)
*Xiong Jiang,Hai Lei,Hao-Yi Huang,Wei Zhang,Yang-Ji Li,Da-Ming Wei*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次在类CSO源4C 76.03中检测到GeV伽马射线耀发活动，发现两个约30天和20天的短时标耀发，表明核区存在局部瞬态能量耗散，可能代表CSO演化中的罕见过渡案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧凑对称天体（CSO）的伽马射线发射特性，特别是探索CSO是否具有短时标变异性，以了解其核区活动和早期射电喷流演化过程。

Method: 基于17年的Fermi-LAT观测数据，进行时间分辨分析，采用100天的时间分箱，检测伽马射线耀发活动，并与4FGL-DR4星表进行长期平均特性对比。

Result: 发现两个显著的伽马射线耀发，时标分别为约30天和20天，相隔约2.5年，具有几乎相同的流量、检验统计量值和光子指数；长期平均特性与4FGL-DR4一致。

Conclusion: 4C 76.03的短时标变异性表明核区存在局部瞬态能量耗散，可能与新注入的喷流成分相关；该源可能代表CSO演化中的罕见过渡案例，重复能量注入使其超过典型CSO的500 pc尺度，为射电喷流早期演化提供关键见解。

Abstract: We report the first detection of GeV gamma-ray flaring activity from the compact symmetric object (CSO)-like source 4C 76.03, based on 17 years of Fermi-LAT observations. Its long-term, time-averaged gamma-ray properties are consistent with the 4FGL-DR4 catalog. However, a time-resolved analysis with 100-day binning reveals two prominent flares occurring on timescales of approximately 30 days and 20 days, separated by about 2.5 years, with nearly identical fluxes, test statistic (TS) values, and photon indices. The short-timescale variability indicates localized and transient energy dissipation in the nuclear region, likely associated with newly injected jet components. Although the gamma-ray emission does not directly trace the long-term jet power responsible for building the observed radio structure, it demonstrates that the central engine remains active. In the context of CSO evolution, 4C 76.03 may represent a rare transitional case, where repeated energy injections allow the source to exceed the canonical 500 pc scale of most CSOs, providing key insight into the early stages of radio jet evolution.

</details>

*Baikal-GVD Collaboration,:,V. A. Allakhverdyan,A. D. Avrorin,A. V. Avrorin,V. M. Aynutdinov,I. A. Belolaptikov,Z. Beňušová,E. A. Bondarev,I. V. Borina,N. M. Budnev,V. A. Chadymov,A. S. Chepurnov,V.
