Ryabov,G. B. Safronov,B. A. Shaybonov,A. S. Sheshukov,V. Y. Shishkin,E. V. Shirokov,F. Šimkovic,A. E. Sirenko,A. V. Skurikhin,A. G. Solovjev,M. N. Sorokovikov,I. Štekl,A. P. Stromakov,O. V. Suvorova,V. A. Tabolenko,V. I. Tretyak,G. V. Trubnikov,B. B. Ulzutuev,Z. Wang,Y. V. Yablokova,D. N. Zaborov,S. I. Zavyalov,D. Y. Zvezdov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 贝加尔湖GVD中微子望远镜使用2019-2024年数据对92个天体物理源进行中微子通量搜索，未发现显著超出，但报告了与ANTARES和KM3NeT竞争的上限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用贝加尔湖GVD中微子望远镜（目前北半球最大的中微子望远镜）的高灵敏度和亚度角分辨率，搜索来自92个感兴趣天体物理源的μ子中微子通量，为全面科学利用GVD数据奠定基础

Method: 使用基于χ²的径迹重建方法和基于截断的分析，仅利用向上运动的μ子（覆盖赤纬-90°至+38°），对2019年4月至2024年3月期间部分完成探测器收集的径迹状事件进行分析

Result: 未发现显著超出，报告了上限值；获得的极限与ANTARES和KM3NeT设定的极限具有竞争力；简要评论了Westerlund 1方向可能的低显著性超出迹象

Conclusion: 这项工作为全面科学利用贝加尔湖GVD数据设立了重要里程碑，展示了该探测器在宇宙中微子源搜索方面的能力

Abstract: Baikal-GVD is a new-generation neutrino telescope currently under construction in Lake Baikal, Russia. With an instrumented volume already at 0.7 km$^3$, Baikal-GVD is currently the largest neutrino telescope in the Northern hemisphere. A sub-degree angular resolution, made possible thanks to high purity of Baikal water, further enhances Baikal-GVD sensitivity to cosmic neutrino sources. In this work, we employ track-like events collected from the partially completed detector between April 2019 and March 2024 to search for muon neutrino fluxes from 92 astrophysical objects of interest. For this, a $χ^2$-based track reconstruction method is used along with a cut-based analysis. The analysis uses upward-going muons only, providing coverage for declinations between -90$^\circ$ and +38$^\circ$. No significant excess has been found, so upper limits are reported. The obtained limits are competitive with those set by ANTARES and KM3NeT. We briefly comment on a possible low-significance indication of an excess from the direction of Westerlund 1. This work sets a major milestone on the way to full-scale scientific exploitation of Baikal-GVD data.

</details>


### [103] [Analytical Framework for Expanding Bubbles in a Hot Circumgalactic Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21306)
*Jiang-Tao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个分析框架，用于描述在热环星系介质中由反馈驱动的气泡演化，考虑了环境压力、声速和非辐射冷却条件，将演化分为四个阶段并推导了相关尺度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解在热环星系介质中反馈驱动气泡的演化动力学，特别是在环境压力和声速不可忽略、辐射冷却效率低的情况下，建立理想化气泡理论与实际观测特征之间的桥梁。

Method: 方法包括：1）建立分析框架将气泡演化分为四个阶段（自由膨胀、Sedov-Taylor膨胀、压力修正/跨声速过渡、跨声速后弛豫）；2）推导各阶段特征半径和时间尺度的自洽标度关系；3）提供数值轨迹对比压力修正模型与经典Sedov-Taylor基准；4）估计可观测参数如气泡尺寸、寿命、X射线光度、高电离离子柱密度。

Result: 主要结果：1）在热晕中，强激波演化的结束通常由压力约束和跨声速性决定，而非灾难性冷却的开始；2）气泡仅轻微超过压力平衡/跨声速点；3）建立了气泡动力学与可观测特征的联系；4）对比了热环星系介质条件下的压力修正模型与经典Sedov-Taylor基准的差异。

Conclusion: 该框架为理想化气泡理论与热星系晕中反馈特征之间提供了透明桥梁，强调了活动星系核反馈相比超新星具有更高的比能量，并指出多相相互作用、质量加载、各向异性耗散等因素可在不改变固有反馈能量分配的情况下提高X射线辐射效率。

Abstract: We develop an analytic framework for the evolution of feedback-driven bubbles expanding into a hot, volume-filling circumgalactic medium (CGM), where the ambient pressure and sound speed are non-negligible and radiative cooling is often inefficient. The evolution is organized into four stages -- free expansion, Sedov--Taylor expansion, pressure-modified/transonic transition, and post-transonic relaxation -- and we derive self-consistent scalings for the characteristic radii and timescales that delimit these stages. A central result is that, in hot halos, the end of the strong-shock evolution is frequently set by pressure confinement and transonicity rather than by the onset of catastrophic cooling, implying only a modest late-time overshoot beyond the pressure-balance/transonic point. We connect the dynamics to observable outcomes by estimating bubble sizes and lifetimes, order-of-magnitude band-limited X-ray luminosities, and high-ionization ion column densities, and we provide stitched numerical trajectories that contrast our pressure-modified model appropriate for hot CGM conditions with a classical Sedov--Taylor benchmark. We then discuss physically motivated extensions beyond the single-event baseline, including continuous or episodic energy injection relevant for AGN-driven bubbles and nuclear outflows, highlighting the much higher specific energy of AGN feedback compared to supernovae and the resulting dynamical differences in how bubbles are driven. We further outline how multiphase interaction, mass loading, anisotropic dissipation, intermittency, confinement, and non-thermal channels can increase the emergent X-ray radiation efficiency without requiring changes to the intrinsic feedback energy partition at the launching site. This framework provides a transparent bridge between idealized bubble theory and feedback signatures in hot galactic halos.

</details>


### [104] [The precessing jet axis of the supernova remnant 3C 397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21307)
*Aleksei Klimov,Dmitry Shishkin,Noam Soker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在超新星遗迹3C 397中发现S形形态特征，归因于爆炸过程中进动喷流的塑造，增加了其与W49B的相似性，提出两种可能的爆炸机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神秘的超新星遗迹3C 397的形态特征，特别是S形结构的成因，探讨其与类似遗迹W49B的相似性，理解喷流在超新星爆炸中的关键作用。

Method: 识别3C 397中的S形形态特征，将其归因于爆炸过程中进动喷流的塑造，通过形态学分析比较与W49B的相似性，提出两种可能的爆炸机制来解释观测特征。

Result: 在3C 397中发现由南北两个气泡组成的S形微弱区域，位于两个X射线明亮侧之间，识别出主要喷流轴，增加了与W49B的相似性，支持喷流在爆炸中的主导作用。

Conclusion: 3C 397的S形结构由爆炸过程中的进动喷流塑造，喷流在超新星爆炸中起主要作用，提出了热核共同包层喷流超新星和塌缩诱导热核喷流驱动爆炸两种可能机制。

Abstract: We identify an S-shaped morphological feature in the enigmatic supernova remnant (SNR) 3C 397, which we attribute to the shaping by a precessing pair of jets during the explosion. We identify an S-shaped, faint region composed of two bubbles, located to the north and south of the center, between two X-ray-bright sides. We attribute the S-shape to a pair of precessing jets that were part of the explosion process. The identification of a main jet axis in SNR 3C 397 increases its similarity to the enigmatic SNR W49B. We discuss two possible scenarios for SNR 3C 397 and W49B. (1) The thermonuclear common-envelope-jet supernova scenario, which was suggested before for W49B, where a neutron star destroys a white dwarf and accretes part of the white dwarf's material via an accretion disk that undergoes a thermonuclear outburst and launches the jets. (2) The collapse-induced thermonuclear jet-driven explosion, which is a core-collapse supernova driven by jets, as in the majority of, or even all, core-collapse supernovae, and in addition, there is a thermonuclear outburst of a rare helium-oxygen mixed layer in the core, which is triggered by the core collapse. Our study emphasizes the primary role of jets even in the enigmatic SNR 3C 397.

</details>


### [105] [Transient narrowband radio bursts from 1E 1547.0-5408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21450)
*Marcus E. Lower,Paul Scholz,Fernando Camilo,David M. Palmer,John E. Reynolds,John M. Sarkissian,Lawrence J. Toomey,George Younes*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在磁星1E 1547.0-5408爆发后一个月观测到84个窄带射电暴，这些暴发与硬X射线发射相位对齐，表明它们可能起源于闭合磁场线的对级联过程，为重复快速射电暴机制提供了低能量类似物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁星中窄带射电暴的罕见现象，探索其与快速射电暴（FRB）的潜在联系，特别是重复FRB的起源机制。

Method: 使用Murriyang望远镜在磁星1E 1547.0-5408 2009年爆发后一个月进行观测，结合Rossi X-ray Timing Explorer的硬X射线数据，分析窄带射电暴的时间特性和相位关系。

Result: 检测到84个窄带射电暴，其中78个在毫秒时间尺度上未分辨；这些暴发集中在2009年2月23-25日的瞬态成分中，与磁视线磁场几何结构的剧烈变化和硬X射线脉冲成分的出现同步；硬X射线发射的前沿与窄带成分相位对齐。

Conclusion: 窄带射电暴可能起源于闭合磁场线的对级联过程，这种闭合场发射可能解释了重复FRB中缺乏秒级周期性的现象；这些暴发代表了重复FRB机制的低能量类似物，进一步将FRB前身星与年轻、高度磁化的中子星联系起来。

Abstract: Radio-loud magnetars are well known for exhibiting rare and unusual radiative properties that are seldom seen in the wider pulsar population. Yet one form of emissive behavior that remains elusive among pulsars and magnetars is narrowband bursts of radio waves. Such emission is a hallmark of repeating sources of fast radio bursts (FRBs), intense radio flashes that originate from distant galaxies. Here, we report the detection of 84 narrowband radio bursts during observations of the magnetar 1E 1547.0-5408 by the Murriyang telescope one month after its 2009 outburst. All but six bursts appear temporally unresolved at millisecond timescales. They were confined to a transient profile component that appeared between 2009 February 23 to 25. This coincided with both dramatic changes in the magnetar line-of-sight magnetic-field geometry, and an emergent pulsed hard X-ray component detected by the Rossi X-ray Timing Explorer. The leading edge of the hard X-ray emission was phase-aligned with the narrowband component, indicating the bursts likely originated from pair cascades along closed magnetic field lines. Such closed-field emission could contribute to the lack of second-scale periodicity in repeating FRBs. Our characterization of the bursts suggests they may represent a low-energy analogue of the repeating FRB mechanism, further linking FRB progenitors to young, highly magnetized neutron stars.

</details>


### [106] [Detection of a Molecular Cloud toward the Heartbeating Gamma-ray Source near the Microquasar SS 433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21550)
*Tomoharu Oka,Ryo Ariyama,Tatsuya Kotani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在SS 433/W50系统北边界发现分子云CO+40.05-2.40，与"心跳"GeV源Fermi J1913+0515位置重合，但云体物理性质正常，无激波加热或增强激发迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SS 433/W50系统北边界发现的"心跳"GeV源Fermi J1913+0515与位置重合的分子云CO+40.05-2.40之间的关联，解释该高能辐射源的物理机制。

Method: 使用Nobeyama 45米望远镜和James Clerk Maxwell Telescope进行毫米波和亚毫米波光谱观测，分析分子云的物理性质；评估电子轫致辐射和强子相互作用等传统高能辐射机制；提出Bondi型吸积驱动的致密天体模型。

Result: 分子云具有银河系盘区宁静暗云的典型物理性质，无激波加热或增强激发证据；传统机制无法在合理能量假设下解释观测到的GeV光度；提出致密天体通过Bondi吸积产生伽马射线的替代模型。

Conclusion: Fermi J1913+0515的伽马射线可能来自嵌入分子云内的致密天体，通过Bondi型吸积产生；其"心跳"样变异性可能反映SS 433进动赤道流出诱导的密度波对吸积流的周期性调制。

Abstract: We report the detection of a molecular cloud, CO+40.05-2.40, positionally coincident with the "heartbeating" GeV source Fermi J1913+0515 at the northern boundary of the SS 433/W50 system. Millimeter and submillimeter spectroscopy with the Nobeyama 45 m telescope and the James Clerk Maxwell Telescope shows that the cloud has physical properties typical of quiescent dark clouds in the Galactic disk, with no evidence of shock heating or enhanced excitation. We examine possible high-energy emission mechanisms and find that the observed GeV luminosity cannot be accounted for by electron bremsstrahlung or hadronic interactions driven by relativistic particles originating from SS 433 under reasonable energetic assumptions. As an alternative, we propose that the gamma-rays may arise from a compact object embedded within the cloud and powered by Bondi-type accretion. In this framework, the reported heartbeat-like variability may reflect periodic modulation of the accretion flow by density waves induced by the precessing equatorial outflow of SS 433.

</details>


### [107] [Multimessenger Concordance for the Cygnus Region as the Source of the Cosmic-Ray Knee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21665)
*Luis E. Espinosa Castro,Kotha Murase,Carlo Rizza,Francesco L. Villante,Vittoria Vecchiotti,Giulia Pagliaroli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究提出银河系内天鹅座区域可能是一个近邻PeVatron（PeV粒子加速器），能够解释宇宙线膝的起源，并通过多信使观测数据（宇宙线、伽马射线、中微子）提供了一致的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线膝的起源是粒子天体物理学的核心未解问题。LHAASO观测到质子谱在3-4 PeV处的明显特征，但100 TeV以上的弥散伽马射线谱却没有相应的断裂，这与标准解释（局部宇宙线分布代表银河系宇宙线海）存在矛盾。

Method: 结合地球上的宇宙线测量数据与LHAASO和Tibet-ASγ实验的甚高能伽马射线观测，识别银河系内部额外的硬伽马射线成分，分析其与天鹅座区域源的相关性。

Result: 在银河系内部发现与天鹅座区域源一致的额外硬伽马射线成分。该结果与LHAASO在天鹅座气泡中探测到的PeVatron候选体以及IceCube中微子天文台观测到的银河系中微子通量性质兼容。

Conclusion: 宇宙线膝可能起源于天鹅座区域的近邻PeVatron，而非传统认为的银河系宇宙线海。多信使观测数据（宇宙线、伽马射线、中微子）提供了相互印证的一致图像，支持这一新解释。

Abstract: The origin of the cosmic-ray (CR) knee remains one of the central open questions in particle astrophysics. Recent measurements by the Large High Altitude Air Shower Observatory revealed a pronounced feature in the proton spectrum at $\sim3-4$~PeV, while observations of diffuse gamma rays above $100$~TeV do not exhibit a corresponding spectral break. This apparent discrepancy challenges the standard interpretation, in which the local CR distribution is representative of the Galactic CR sea. Here, we investigate whether the CR knee can instead originate from the Cygnus region as a nearby PeVatron. By combining CR measurements at Earth with very-high-energy gamma-ray observations from LHAASO and the Tibet-AS$γ$ experiment, we identify an additional hard gamma-ray component in the inner Galaxy consistent with a source located in the Cygnus region. We show that our results provide a concordance multimessenger picture. The required properties are compatible with the PeVatron candidate detected by LHAASO in the Cygnus bubble and with the Galactic neutrino flux observed by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory.

</details>


### [108] [The second H.E.S.S. gamma-ray burst catalogue: 15 years of observations with the H.E.S.S. telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21741)

Burger-Scheidlin,S. Casanova,J. Celic,M. Cerruti,S. Chandra,A. Chen,M. Chernyakova,J. O. Chibueze,O. Chibueze,T. Collins,B. Cornejo,G. Cotter,J. Damascene Mbarubucyeye,I. D. Davids,J. de Assis Scarpin,M. de Bony de Lavergne,M. de Naurois,E. de Oña Wilhelmi,A. G. Delgado Giler,J. Devin,A. Djannati-Ataï,J. Djuvsland,A. Dmytriiev,K. Egberts,K. Egg,J. -P. Ernenwein,C. Shapopi,W. Si Said,H. Sol,S. Spencer,Ł. Stawarz,S. Steinmassl,T. Tanaka,A. M. Taylor,G. L. Taylor,R. Terrier,M. Tsirou,T. Unbehaun,C. van Eldik,M. Vecchi,C. Venter,J. Vink,T. Wach,S. J. Wagner,A. Wierzcholska,M. Zacharias,A. A. Zdziarski,W. Zhong,S. J. Zhu,A. Zech*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: H.E.S.S.望远镜15年伽马射线暴观测总结：除两个探测外无显著甚高能信号，获得最大甚高能GRB上限数据集，发现甚高能探测GRB并非独立群体而是具有明亮X射线辐射和有利观测条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用成像大气切伦科夫望远镜观测甚高能伽马射线暴信号，通过H.E.S.S.长期观测数据研究GRB的甚高能辐射特性，为下一代望远镜观测提供基础。

Method: 分析2004-2019年H.E.S.S.对GRB的快速跟观测数据（触发后数十秒内），进行群体比较研究，选取条件有利的暴进行X射线分析和辐射建模。

Result: 除GRB 180720B和GRB 190829A两个探测外，未发现显著甚高能信号，获得最大甚高能GRB上限数据集；甚高能探测GRB倾向于具有明亮X射线辐射、有利红移和观测条件，而非独立群体。

Conclusion: 下一代切伦科夫望远镜阵列（如CTAO）凭借更低能量阈值将增强对更暗、更远GRB的探测能力，推动甚高能GRB研究进展。

Abstract: Recent observational efforts using imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes (IACTs) have led to firm detections of very-high-energy (VHE) signals from bright gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), often at moderate redshifts. This work presents 15 years of H.E.S.S. GRB observations and examines their implications through population comparisons and selected modelling cases. GRBs are a key science target of the High Energy Stereoscopic System (H.E.S.S.). With a low-energy threshold ($\lesssim$100 GeV) and rapid repointing capabilities, H.E.S.S. can begin follow-up observations within tens of seconds after a GRB trigger, covering the late prompt or early afterglow phases. We report GRB follow-up observations with H.E.S.S. from 2004 to 2019, which resulted in no significant VHE signals (aside from the detections of GRB~180720B and GRB~190829A). The resulting upper limits comprise the largest set available for GRBs at VHE. A subset of bursts with favourable conditions were selected for X-ray analysis and emission modelling. Population studies were performed to compare detected and non-detected GRBs. The results indicate that VHE-detected GRBs are not a distinct population, but tend to feature luminous X-ray emission and favourable redshift and observing conditions. This highlights the potential of next-generation IACTs such as the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), whose lower energy threshold will enhance the detection of fainter and more distant GRBs.

</details>


### [109] [X-ray transients in the Chandra archive: Introducing the cumulative distribution discriminator (CuDiDi)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21791)
*I. Saathoff,J. Larsson*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 系统搜索钱德拉X射线天文台观测数据中的亚观测时标瞬变源，使用新型检测流程识别出765个高置信度瞬变事件，涵盖多种天体物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚观测时标X射线瞬变源（如恒星耀斑、磁星爆发、河外快速瞬变源、超新星激波突破）是一类多样且未充分探索的天体物理现象，需要系统性的搜索方法来发现这些罕见事件。

Method: 开发了新的检测流程，结合源识别、光变曲线分析、星表交叉匹配和新型统计分类器（累积分布判别器CuDiDi），对20,212个钱德拉ACIS观测数据进行了系统性搜索。

Result: 从1420个初始候选体中识别出765个高置信度瞬变源，时间尺度、流量和光谱形态范围广泛，中位持续时间为10 ks。部分持续时间<30秒的快速事件显示出非常软的光谱，可能源于耀斑矮星，但河外现象的可能性不能完全排除。

Conclusion: 提供了全面的亚观测时标钱德拉X射线瞬变源星表，建立了利用档案数据集发现罕见短时标高能现象的通用方法，CuDiDi方法能识别大多数已知瞬变源并扩展了样本范围。

Abstract: X-ray transients on sub-observation timescales represent a diverse and underexplored class of astrophysical phenomena, from stellar flares and magnetar bursts to extragalactic fast transients and supernova shock breakouts. We present a systematic search for such events across 20,212 Chandra ACIS observations using a new detection pipeline that combines source identification, light-curve analysis, catalogue cross-matching, and a novel statistical classifier, the cumulative distribution discriminator (CuDiDi). From 1420 initial candidates, we identified a high-confidence golden sample of 765 transients spanning a broad range of timescales, fluxes, and spectral shapes. The candidates are distributed across the whole sky and show a wide range of durations with a median of 10 ks. A subset of fast events lasting < 30 s displays very soft spectra and is likely due to flaring dwarf stars, although extragalactic phenomena cannot be ruled out for all of them. The comparison with previously published samples showed that CuDiDi identifies most known transients while imposing somewhat stricter variability criteria, and it also extends the total sample of Chandra transients to include shorter events. We deliver a comprehensive catalogue of sub-observation Chandra X-ray transients and establish a general method for exploiting archival datasets to uncover rare short-lived high-energy phenomena.

</details>


### [110] [A joint MeerKAT and Parkes view of Omega Centauri: New TRAPUM Searches and Pulsar Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21845)
*Miquel Colom i Bernadich,Shi Dai,Federico Abbate,Matthew Kerr,Matteo Bachetti,Yash Bhargava,Sarah Buchner,Simon Johnston,Marta Burgay,Andrea Possenti,Rouhin Nag,Alessandro Ridolfi,Amodio Carleo,Alessandro Corongiu,Paulo C. C. Freire,Fernando Camilo,Weiwei Chen,Mario Cadelano,Dhanraj Risbud,Prajwal V. Padmanabh,David J. Champion,Michael Kramer,Benjamin Stappers,Maciej Serylak,Vishnu Balakrishnan,Matthew Bailes,Arunima Dutta,Laila Vleeschower Calas,Vivek Venkatraman Krishnan,Yunpeng Men*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对Omega Centauri球状星团中毫秒脉冲星的研究，通过MeerKAT和Parkes望远镜观测发现了新的孤立脉冲星，更新了已知双星系统参数，测量了8颗脉冲星的周期导数，排除了中等质量黑洞的存在，并揭示了该星团复杂的演化历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫秒脉冲星是研究球状星团恒星演化、动力学和引力势的有力探针。Omega Centauri作为最大的银河系球状星团，其脉冲星种群特性可能反映了星团的复杂演化历史，特别是与仅基于相遇率预测的脉冲星种群模型存在差异。

Method: 使用MeerKAT望远镜进行傅里叶域加速度和加加速度搜索，结合MeerKAT和Parkes Murriyang 2021-2025年的数据进行脉冲星计时。利用更新的射电星历折叠Fermi LAT和NICER的高能光子数据以搜索脉冲辐射。

Result: 发现新的孤立毫秒脉冲星PSR J1326-4728S（周期4.538 ms，DM=96.24 cm³pc）。更新了所有已知双星系统的轨道参数，其中I、N、Q的参数与先前估计有显著差异。B、G、H、K、L显示黑寡妇特性，N和Q有>0.2 M⊙伴星，N具有显著轨道偏心率(e=0.093)。测量了8颗脉冲星的周期导数，未检测到单个脉冲星的高能脉冲辐射。加速度测量与King模型引力势一致，排除了10³-10⁴ M⊙中等质量黑洞，上限<10⁵ M⊙。

Conclusion: Omega Centauri中孤立毫秒脉冲星和黑寡妇系统的高比例，以及N的偏心率，难以仅用相遇率解释的脉冲星种群预测来调和。这些特性反映了星团的复杂演化历史，部分脉冲星可能形成于比现今观测环境更致密的环境中。

Abstract: Millisecond pulsars (MSPs) are powerful probes of globular clusters (GCs), tracing stellar evolution, cluster dynamics, and the local gravitational potential. We investigate the MSP population in GC Omega Centauri. We perform Fourier-domain acceleration and jerk searches on MeerKAT observations, and carry out pulsar timing using MeerKAT and Parkes Murriyang data spanning 2021-2025. We fold Fermi LAT and NICER photons using updated radio ephemerides to search for high-energy pulsations. We discover a new isolated MSP, PSR J1326-4728S (hereafter S), with a spin period of 4.538 ms and a dispersion measure of 96.24 cm$^3$pc. We update the orbital parameters of all known binary systems, with those of I, N, and Q differing significantly from previous estimates, and obtain new timing solutions for G, H, and K. Pulsars B, G, H, K, and L exhibit black widow-like properties, I, N and Q are found in wider binaries, with N and Q having >0.2 M$_\odot$ companions, and N showing a significant orbital eccentricity (e=0.093). Significant spin period derivatives are measured for eight pulsars and interpreted as arising from the cluster gravitational potential. No pulsed high-energy emission is detected from individual pulsars. The inferred line-of-sight accelerations are consistent with a King-model gravitational potential. While our measurements are insensitive to an intermediate-mass black hole with mass 10$^3$-10$^4$ M$_\odot$, they place an upper limit of <10$^5$ M$_\odot$ at 90% confidence. The high fraction of isolated MSPs and black widows systems, and possibly the eccentricity of N, are difficult to reconcile with MSP population predictions based solely on encounter rates. Instead, these properties likely reflect the complex evolutionary history of Omega Centauri, with part of its MSP population having formed in denser environments than the one observed today.

</details>

Abstract: Context: Composite galaxies that contain both Seyfert and starburst components may produce very high-energy (VHE; >100 GeV) gamma-ray emission at a wide range of spatial scales, from a few Schwarzschild radii of a supermassive black hole to dimensions of kiloparsec-size jet-driven outflows. In addition to supernova remnants, various sources have been suggested to explain data collected on composite galaxies, including multi-messenger neutrino and ultra-high-energy cosmic-ray data. Aims: The closest composite Seyfert-starburst galaxies (NGC 1068, the Circinus galaxy, and NGC 4945) are observed with the High Energy Stereoscopic System (H.E.S.S.) to provide constraints on cosmic-ray populations in these systems. Methods: Data obtained in H.E.S.S. observations have been analyzed to search for VHE gamma-ray counterparts to the GeV gamma-ray signals detected with Fermi-LAT and for potential spectral components in the VHE range. Results: No significant signals have been found in these H.E.S.S. data. Upper limits on the VHE gamma-ray fluxes were applied to constrain theoretical models involving different spectral components.

### [112] [Millihertz quasi-periodic oscillations in accreting X-ray pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22060)
*Wen Yang,Wei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对吸积中子星中观测到的毫赫兹准周期振荡（mHz QPOs）的观测特征、理论解释及未来研究方向的综述


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积中子星表现出复杂的多波段、多时间尺度变异性，这些变异性可能由吸积流不稳定性、不均匀运动或中子星表面热核爆发等多种物理过程驱动。毫赫兹QPOs作为其中一种重要现象，其物理起源和性质尚不完全清楚，需要系统梳理观测特征和理论解释以推动该领域发展。

Method: 基于NICER、Insight-HXMT和NuSTAR等近期X射线任务的数据，对低质量X射线双星和高质量X射线双星中观测到的毫赫兹QPOs（频率范围~1-1000 mHz）进行系统性综述分析，总结观测特征并梳理现有理论解释。

Result: 系统总结了毫赫兹QPOs在X射线脉冲星光变曲线中的观测特征，包括频率范围、出现条件、与源状态的关系等。整理了当前对该现象的理论解释框架，识别了现有理论面临的挑战和未解决的问题。

Conclusion: 毫赫兹QPOs是理解吸积中子星物理过程的重要探针，当前研究已积累了大量观测数据并提出了多种理论模型，但仍存在诸多挑战。未来需要通过更高精度观测、多波段联合分析以及理论模型改进来深入揭示其物理起源和性质。

Abstract: Accreting neutron stars exhibit pulsed X-rays and complex temporal variability across multi-wavelengths and different timescales. This variability could be driven by various physical processes including instability or inhomogeneous motions within the accretion flow, thermonuclear bursts on the neutron star surface. In this review, we present a concise overview of the observational features for millihertz (mHz) quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) at a frequency range of $\sim 1- 1000$ mHz observed in light curves of X-ray pulsars for both low-mass X-ray binaries and high-mass X-ray binaries, based on recent X-ray missions, e.g., NICER, Insight-HXMT and NuSTAR. We further summarize current theoretical interpretations, discuss remaining challenges and propose potential directions for future studies to advance the understanding of the nature and physical origin of these QPOs.

</details>


### [113] [The full evolution of the type-C QPO in MAXI J1348-630 revealed by Insight-HXMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22100)
*X. -L. Wang,Z. Yan,F. -G. Xie,J. -F. Wang,Y. -X. Li,Z. -Y. Liu,R. -Y. Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究MAXI J1348-630黑洞X射线双星中C型准周期振荡的演化特征，发现QPO频率与光谱硬度存在紧密的反相关关系，主爆发和迷你爆发的频率-流量关系呈现相反的滞后环


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C型准周期振荡是探测黑洞吸积几何结构的灵敏探针，研究其在爆发过程中的演化有助于理解吸积物理机制，特别是不同爆发类型（主爆发与迷你爆发）之间的差异

Method: 利用Insight-HXMT对MAXI J1348-630的观测数据，进行时序分析和光谱分析，追踪QPO频率、分数均方根、能量依赖性及其与流量和光谱形状的相关性

Result: QPO频率从0.24 Hz增加到7.28 Hz，在硬态到硬中间态转变后rms谱变硬；频率-流量关系在主爆发和迷你爆发中呈现相反的滞后环；频率与光谱硬度存在紧密的单轨反相关

Conclusion: 结果支持康普顿区域可在特征尺度上重新形成的观点；QPO频率主要受光谱形状驱动而非光度；反向滞后环表明主爆发和迷你爆发之间的差异与初始条件有关

Abstract: Type-C quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in black hole X-ray binaries are sensitive probes of accretion geometry. Using Insight-HXMT observations of MAXI J1348-630, we study the evolution of type-C QPOs during its 2019 main outburst and later mini outbursts. We carry out timing and spectral analysis, tracking QPO frequency, fractional rms, energy dependence and their correlations with flux and spectral shape. The QPO frequency increased from 0.24 Hz to 7.28 Hz during the rise and remained near 7 Hz when reappearing in different states. The rms spectrum hardened after the transition from hard to hard-intermediate state. The frequency-flux relation shows strong hysteresis between rise and decay, with the loop reversed between main and mini outbursts. In contrast, frequency and hardness follow a tight single-track anti-correlation. Our results support that the Compton region can reform at a characteristic scale. The QPO frequency is mainly driven by spectral shape rather than luminosity. The reversed hysteresis suggests differences between main and mini outbursts related to initial conditions.

</details>

*The VERITAS Collaboration,A. Archer,P. Bangale,J. T. Bartkoske,W. Benbow,N. R. Bond,Y. Chen,J. L. Christiansen,A. J. Chromey,A. Duerr,M. Errando,M. E. Godoy,J. E. Pedrosa,S. Feldman,Q. Feng,S. Filbert,A. Furniss,W. Hanlon,O. Hervet,C. E. Hinrichs,J. Holder,T. B. Humensky,M. Iskakova,W. Jin,M. N. Johnson,E. Joshi,P. Kaaret,M. Kertzman,M. Kherlakian,T. K. Kleiner,N. Korzoun,F. Krennrich,S. Kumar,S. Kundu,M. J. Lang,M. Lundy,G. Maier,P. Moriarty,R. Mukherjee,M. Ohishi,R. A. Ong,A. Pandey,M. Pohl,E. Pueschel,P. L. Rabinowitz,K. Ragan,P. T. Reynolds,D. Ribeiro,E. Roache,I. Sadeh,L. Saha,H. Salzmann,M. Santander,G. H. Sembroski,S. Tandon,J. V. Tucci,D. A. Williams,S. L. Wong,J. Woo,T. Yoshikoshi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: VERITAS对SS 433进行深度观测，在东西喷流瓣探测到延展的TeV伽马射线辐射，支持轻子起源模型，为微类星体贡献银河宇宙射线提供约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微类星体如SS 433被认为是宇宙射线能谱膝区（~4 PeV）的潜在贡献源。研究SS 433喷流驱动的粒子加速过程，有助于理解微类星体如何贡献银河宇宙射线

Method: 使用VERITAS望远镜进行超过150小时的深度观测，能量敏感范围>100 GeV，角分辨率优于0.1°，对SS 433进行形态学和光谱学研究，包括相位分辨分析

Result: 在东西喷流瓣探测到延展的TeV伽马射线辐射，辐射沿喷流轴延伸，与喷流-超新星遗迹相互作用区重合；中心双星无TeV辐射；辐射无轨道或进动相位变化；光谱特征支持轻子起源

Conclusion: SS 433喷流瓣的延展TeV辐射支持轻子起源模型，任何强子加速过程都处于次要地位；结果为微类星体贡献银河宇宙射线提供了重要约束

Abstract: Microquasars such as SS 433 are considered potential contributors to cosmic rays up to the knee of the cosmic ray energy spectrum ($\sim4\,\mathrm{PeV}$), where a transition in the dominant acceleration processes is expected. The SS 433 system, located within the W50 supernova remnant, is a Galactic microquasar with relativistic jets interacting with the surrounding medium over parsec scales, providing an example for studying jet-driven particle acceleration. A deep morphological and spectral study of SS 433 is performed using more than 150 hours of observations with VERITAS, sensitive to $γ$-ray energies $>100\,\mathrm{GeV}$. With an angular resolution better than $0.1^°$, extended TeV $γ$-ray emission is resolved from both the eastern and western jet lobes, located tens of parsecs from the central binary. The emission appears elongated along the jet axis and coincides with regions where the jets interact with the surrounding supernova remnant. No TeV emission is detected from the central binary, nor is significant emission observed between the central binary and the jet lobes. Phase-resolved analyses show no evidence for variability with orbital or precessional phase, supporting a steady emission scenario. The observed morphology and spectra are consistent with scenarios where particles are accelerated in the lobes of the jets, possibly through shocks or alternative processes such as magnetic reconnection. The extended TeV emission from the jet lobes of SS 433 favors a leptonic origin in the VERITAS energy range, suggesting any hadronic acceleration is subdominant. The results offer valuable constraints on how microquasar jets may contribute to the Galactic cosmic-ray population toward the knee.

</details>


### [115] [Characterizing Short-Timescale Optical Variability in Non-blazar Active Galactic Nucleus PKS~0521$-$36 Using TESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22197)
*Sikandar Akbar,Zahir Shah*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用TESS卫星对非耀变体活动星系核PKS 0521-36进行高时间分辨率光学测光分析，发现2.838天的准周期振荡信号，解释为相对论性喷流中的磁流体动力学扭折不稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非耀变体活动星系核PKS 0521-36的光学光变特性，特别是探索其中可能存在的准周期振荡现象，以理解相对论性喷流的物理机制和结构特征

Method: 使用TESS卫星在三个观测扇区（Sector 5、6和32）获取高时间分辨率光学光变曲线（30分钟和10分钟采样），进行功率谱密度分析、流量分布建模、Lomb-Scargle周期图分析、WWZ时频分析和DRW模型拟合

Result: 发现中等程度光变（F_var≈0.69-1.19%），功率谱密度符合弯曲幂律模型，流量分布需双对数正态分布描述，在Sector 5中检测到显著的2.838天准周期振荡信号（置信度>99.99%），该信号为瞬态特征，弯曲频率与QPO周期一致

Conclusion: 观测到的光学QPO可解释为相对论性喷流中磁流体动力学扭折不稳定性，与观测到的螺旋结构一致，中等多普勒因子（δ≈5-10）可解释天尺度周期性，这是首次在具有直接成像螺旋喷流的非耀变体AGN中发现光学QPO

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of high-cadence optical light curves of the non-blazar AGN PKS~0521$-$36 obtained with \textit{TESS} across three sectors: Sectors~5 and~6 (Cycle~1, 30~min cadence) and Sector~32 (Cycle~3, 10~min cadence). The source exhibits moderate variability with $F_\mathrm{var} \approx 0.69$--$1.19\%$, consistent with a mildly beamed jet. The power spectral density (PSD) in all sectors is better described by a bending power-law than a simple power law, with high-frequency slopes $α_1 \approx 2.1$--2.9, indicating red-noise dominated variability. Flux distributions require two-component models, with a double log-normal providing the best description, suggesting the presence of two distinct optical flux states associated with quiescent jet emission and episodic flaring activity. A significant QPO at $P = 2.838 \pm 0.078$~d is detected in Sector~5 at $>99.99\%$ confidence in the Lomb--Scargle periodogram, independently confirmed by WWZ ($2.839 \pm 0.110$~d) and supported at the $3σ$ level by DRW analysis. The signal spans $\sim$9 cycles within the 26.1-day baseline in Sector~5 and is absent in Sectors~6 and~32, indicating a transient feature. The PSD bending frequency ($ν_b \approx 0.308$~d$^{-1}$; $\sim$3.2~d) is consistent with the QPO period, suggesting a common origin. We interpret the oscillation as magnetohydrodynamic kink instabilities in the relativistic jet, consistent with the observed helicoidal structure. A moderate Doppler factor ($δ\approx 5$--10) naturally explains the day-scale periodicity. Together with previously reported $γ$-ray QPOs on longer timescales, this suggests a hierarchical variability structure, and, to the best of our knowledge, provides the first evidence for an optical QPO in a non-blazar AGN with a directly imaged helical jet.

</details>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Interleaving distances from height-difference functions on posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20269)
*Toshitaka Aoki*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高度差函数ρ的广义交错距离d_ρ，用于任意偏序集上的函子范畴，扩展了经典交错距离的适用范围


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典交错距离依赖于平移结构，但在一般偏序集上平移结构稀缺，限制了该框架的应用范围，需要一种更通用的交错型距离

Method: 通过高度差函数ρ构造ℝ≥0-索引的伴随自函子族作为广义平移，在范畴论框架下定义高度交错距离d_ρ，研究其三角不等式性质、稳定性及侵蚀型构造

Result: d_ρ在特定条件下满足三角不等式（至多加性缺陷c(ρ)），当c(ρ)=0时为扩展伪距离；对ρ的扰动具有稳定性；在ℝ^d上ρ=ρ_diag时恢复经典交错距离

Conclusion: 高度交错距离为任意偏序集上的持久性模块比较提供了通用框架，扩展了交错距离的应用范围，建立了系统的理论性质

Abstract: Interleaving distances provide a fundamental tool for comparing persistence modules and have been widely used in topological data analysis. Their definitions are typically based on translation structures (shift operations) on the indexing poset, but on general posets such structures can be scarce, making this framework restrictive. In this paper, we introduce a new interleaving-type distance for functor categories over arbitrary posets, induced by a height-difference function $ρ$. The key idea is to associate to $ρ$ an $\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}$-indexed family of adjoint endofunctors on $\mathrm{Fun}(P,\mathcal{C})$, which play the role of generalized translations and allow us to formulate interleavings in a purely categorical manner and define the distance $d_ρ$, called the height-interleaving distance. In particular, any height function (i.e., a real-valued order-preserving map) canonically induces such a height-difference function, so our framework remains useful on finite posets. Moreover, when $P=\mathbb{R}^d$ and $ρ=ρ_{\mathrm{diag}}$, the resulting distance coincides with the classical interleaving distance for multiparameter persistence modules. However, in general, $d_ρ$ need not satisfy the triangle inequality. Under suitable hypotheses (e.g. CIP for $(P,ρ)$; this condition is automatic when $P$ is a tree poset), we prove a triangle inequality up to an additive defect bounded by a constant $c(ρ)$; in particular, when $c(ρ)=0$ this yields an extended pseudo-distance. We also establish a stability property with respect to perturbations of height-difference functions: small changes in $ρ$ induce small changes in the associated height-interleaving distance. Finally, we study an analogue of the erosion-type constructions from classical interleavings within our framework.

</details>


### [117] [Universal Coefficients and Mayer-Vietoris for Moore Homology of Ample Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20861)
*Luciano Melodia*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了群胚Moore同调的两个结构定理：通用系数定理和Mayer-Vietoris长正合序列，为计算Moore同调提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究充足群胚的Moore同调结构，旨在建立有效的计算工具，特别是通用系数定理和Mayer-Vietoris序列，以简化同调群的计算。

Method: 1. 对每个充足群胚和离散阿贝尔系数群，证明通用系数定理，建立同调群与整数Moore同调的自然短正合序列关系。2. 对单位空间的分解，在链水平上证明Mayer-Vietoris长正合序列，基于相应限制群胚的Moore链复形的短正合序列。

Result: 1. 建立了通用系数定理：0 → H_n(𝒢;ℤ)⊗A → H_n(𝒢;A) → Tor₁(H_{n-1}(𝒢;ℤ),A) → 0。2. 建立了Mayer-Vietoris长正合序列。3. 解释了系数群离散性对通用系数定理的必要性。

Conclusion: 论文为充足群胚的Moore同调计算提供了两个核心结构定理，通用系数定理和Mayer-Vietoris序列，这些工具显著简化了同调群的计算，并阐明了系数群离散性的重要性。

Abstract: We establish two structural results for Moore homology of ample groupoids. First, for every ample groupoid $\mathcal{G}$ and every discrete abelian coefficient group $A$, we prove a universal coefficient theorem relating the homology groups $H_n(\mathcal{G};A)$ to the integral Moore homology of $\mathcal{G}$. More precisely, we obtain a natural short exact sequence $$ 0 \longrightarrow H_n(\mathcal{G};\mathbb{Z})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}} A \xrightarrow{κ_n^{\mathcal{G}}} H_n(\mathcal{G};A) \xrightarrow{ι_n^{\mathcal{G}}} \operatorname{Tor}_1^{\mathbb{Z}}\bigl(H_{n-1}(\mathcal{G};\mathbb{Z}),A\bigr) \longrightarrow 0. $$ Second, for a decomposition of the unit space into clopen saturated subsets, we prove a Mayer-Vietoris long exact sequence in Moore homology. The proof is carried out at the chain level and is based on a short exact sequence of Moore chain complexes associated to the corresponding restricted groupoids. These results provide effective tools for the computation of Moore homology. We also explain why the discreteness of the coefficient group is essential for the universal coefficient theorem.

</details>


### [118] [Buchstaber, Ochanine, Krichever, and Witten genera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21118)
*Mikhail Kornev*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文引入了一类新的形式群律，其模平方构造产生了Buchstaber多项式族，与Krichever亏格相关但不重合，计算了相应Hirzebruch亏格在theta除子和复射影空间上的值，并描述了与Ochanine、Krichever和Witten亏格的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形式群律与亏格理论之间的联系，特别是探索Buchstaber多项式族与现有亏格理论（如Krichever亏格、椭圆亏格）的关系，寻找超出Hirzebruch椭圆亏格的新构造。

Method: 引入一类新的形式群律，通过模平方构造得到Buchstaber多项式族；计算相应Hirzebruch亏格在特定几何对象（theta除子、复射影空间）上的值；建立与Ochanine、Krichever和Witten亏格的关系；证明这些构造提供了不来自Hirzebruch椭圆亏格的新例子。

Result: 成功构造了一类新的形式群律，其模平方产生Buchstaber多项式族；计算了相应亏格在theta除子和复射影空间上的具体值；明确了该亏格与Ochanine、Krichever和Witten亏格的关系；证明了这些构造确实提供了不来自Hirzebruch椭圆亏格的新例子。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了形式群律与亏格理论之间的联系，提供了超出传统椭圆亏格框架的新构造，深化了对Buchstaber多项式族与亏格理论关系的理解，为相关几何和拓扑研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of formal group laws whose modulus square construction yields Buchstaber's family of polynomials. This class is related to, but does not coincide with, the family of formal group laws associated with the Krichever genus. We compute the values of the corresponding Hirzebruch genus on theta divisors and complex projective spaces, describe its relation to the Ochanine, Krichever, and Witten genera, and show how this construction gives examples not arising from Hirzebruch's elliptic genera of level $n$.

</details>


### [119] [Cocycles and positive functionals in higher cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21431)
*Antonio López Neumann,Piotr W. Nowak*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了正泛函与高阶酉上同调中上闭链的对应关系，将GNS构造推广到高阶情形，并研究了代数谱隙与上同调消失的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正泛函与高阶酉上同调中上闭链之间的对应关系，将经典的GNS构造推广到高阶情形，探索代数谱隙与上同调消失之间的深层联系。

Method: 建立正泛函与高阶酉上同调中上闭链的对应理论，将Gelfand-Naimark-Segal构造推广到高阶度，分析代数谱隙在温和条件下如何刻画上同调消失而非约化酉上同调。

Result: 证明了正泛函与高阶酉上同调中上闭链的对应关系，建立了高阶GNS构造，并证明了在温和条件下代数谱隙能够刻画上同调消失而非约化酉上同调。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了正泛函与高阶酉上同调中上闭链的对应理论框架，将经典GNS构造推广到高阶情形，揭示了代数谱隙与上同调消失之间的本质联系，为高阶上同调理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We establish and explore the correspondence between positive functionals and cocycles in higher unitary cohomology. We generalize the classical cocycle version of the Gelfand-Naimark-Segal construction to higher degrees and apply it to characterize vanishing of higher unitary cohomology as an extension property for positive functionals. We also prove that under mild conditions the algebraic spectral gap for the one sided Laplacian characterizes cohomological vanishing instead of reducedness of unitary cohomology

</details>

### [120] [Collective Spin Excitations in Correlated Moiré Chern Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20370)
*Ming Xie,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了过渡金属二硫化物莫尔超晶格中磁有序的稳定性，发现磁振子拓扑性质对磁相变温度有显著影响，在量子反常霍尔相中Tc比拓扑平庸相提高一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格中的窄电子带支持多种关联量子相，其中磁有序是最普遍存在的母态。由于电子-电子关联作用，磁有序的稳定性受到低能集体自旋涨落（磁振子激发）的严重影响。研究磁振子激发的性质及其对磁态稳定性和相变温度的影响具有重要意义。

Method: 研究在整数填充因子ν=-1条件下，通过调节层间位移场来研究磁振子谱的性质。分析磁振子谱的低能带拓扑特性，探讨磁振子能隙与磁基态拓扑性质的关系，并计算磁相变温度Tc。

Result: 发现磁振子谱存在孤立的低能带，其拓扑特性随层间位移场的调节而发生转变。磁振子能隙对磁基态的拓扑性质非常敏感，导致量子反常霍尔相的相变温度Tc比拓扑平庸的关联绝缘体相提高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电子拓扑与磁振子拓扑之间的相互作用，为通过莫尔工程控制磁性和拓扑性质提供了新途径。磁振子拓扑对磁有序稳定性的显著影响为设计高温拓扑磁相提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Moiré-induced narrow electronic bands in transition metal dichalcogenide superlattices support many correlated quantum phases characterized by novel charge, flavor, and topological orders. Among these, magnetic ordering emerges as the most ubiquitous, often serving as the parent state for other correlated phases, including quantum anomalous Hall states, as well as chiral superconducting state. Because of electron-electron correlation, the stability of magnetic order is critically influenced by low-energy collective spin fluctuations, or magnon excitations. We investigate the nature of magnon excitations and their impact on the stability and transition temperature of the magnetic state at integer filling factor $ν= -1$. We find that the magnon spectrum exhibits isolated low-energy bands whose topological character undergoes a transition upon tuning the interlayer displacement field. The magnon gap is found to depend sensitively on the topology of the magnetic ground state, resulting in an order-of-magnitude enhancement of the transition temperature $T_c$ in the quantum anomalous Hall phase compared to the topologically trivial correlated insulator. Our findings provide insight into the interplay between electron and magnon topology and suggest new routes for controlling magnetism and topology via moiré engineering.

</details>


### [121] [Spin-reorientation as a switch for electronic topology in van der Waals ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20457)
*Satyabrata Bera,Sudipta Chatterjee,Suman Kalyan Pradhan,Subhadip Pradhan,Arnab Bera,Sk Kalimuddin,Ashis K. Nandy,Mintu Mondal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了二维范德华铁磁体Fe₄GeTe₂中自旋重取向转变对拓扑电子结构和输运性质的影响，发现自旋重取向可作为调控拓扑输运状态的内禀控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华铁磁体中的自旋重取向转变（SRT）对电子拓扑结构驱动的热力学和输运性质的影响尚未得到充分探索。研究旨在理解磁各向异性如何通过自旋重取向调控电荷输运，特别是在室温二维铁磁体Fe₄GeTe₂中。

Method: 采用温度依赖的磁化、比热、磁输运和热电测量技术研究Fe₄GeTe₂。通过磁化测量确定磁易轴重取向转变温度（T_SRT~100K）和居里温度（T_C~270K），结合塞贝克系数、各向异性磁阻、霍尔效应等输运测量分析费米面重构和拓扑性质变化。

Result: 在自旋重取向转变温度附近观察到塞贝克系数和各向异性磁阻的明显异常，表明费米面重构。磁阻呈现两步场依赖：低场增强与倾斜自旋和畴结构散射相关，高场负响应源于自旋涨落抑制。普通霍尔系数R₀符号改变和反常霍尔电阻ρ^{A}_{yx}尖锐异常表明温度驱动的能带拓扑结构变化。反常霍尔电导σ^{A}_{xy}分析显示Berry曲率驱动的反常霍尔响应在SRT前后发生显著改变。

Conclusion: 自旋重取向可作为二维范德华铁磁体内部控制参数，用于在不同拓扑输运状态之间切换。这为通过对称性控制工程化自旋极化电子态和畴结构驱动功能提供了新途径，揭示了磁各向异性与拓扑电子结构之间的深刻关联。

Abstract: The interplay between spin reorientation and topological electronic structure in two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) ferromagnets is central to understanding how magnetic anisotropy shapes charge transport. Although spin-reorientation transitions (SRTs) are common in 2D metallic ferromagnets, their impact on electronic-topology-driven thermodynamic and transport properties remains largely unexplored. Here we investigate this issue in Fe$_4$GeTe$_2$ (F4GT), a room-temperature quasi-2D vdW ferromagnet, using temperature-dependent magnetization, specific heat, magnetotransport, and thermoelectric measurements. Magnetization and specific heat establish a reorientation of the magnetic easy axis near $T_{\mathrm{SRT}} \sim 100$~K, in addition to ferromagnetic ordering at $T_C \sim 270$~K. Across the SRT, the Seebeck coefficient and anisotropic magnetoresistance show clear anomalies, indicating Fermi-surface reconstruction. The magnetoresistance exhibits a two-step field dependence: a low-field enhancement near the SRT associated with scattering from canted spins and evolving domains, followed by a higher-field negative response as spin fluctuations are suppressed. The simultaneous sign change of the ordinary Hall coefficient $R_0$ and the sharp anomaly in the anomalous Hall resistivity $ρ^{A}_{yx}$ further point to a temperature-driven modification of the underlying band topology. Analysis of the anomalous Hall conductivity $σ^{A}_{xy}$ and the scaling of $ρ^{A}_{yx}$ shows that the Berry-curvature-driven anomalous Hall response below $T_{\mathrm{SRT}}$ is strongly modified above the transition. Our results identify spin reorientation as an internal control parameter for switching between distinct topological transport regimes in a 2D vdW ferromagnet, providing a symmetry-controlled route to engineer spin-polarized electronic states and domain-texture-driven functionalities.

</details>


### [122] [Magnetic and electric properties of the metallic kagome antiferromagnet CrRhAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20558)
*Franziska Breitner,Bin Shen,Anton Jesche,Alexander A. Tsirlin,Philipp Gegenwart*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: CrRhAs是一种反铁磁Kagome金属，在150K发生反铁磁转变，霍尔系数随磁场线性变化但无非线性霍尔贡献，其符号随电流和磁场配置变化，且在TN以下显著增强，表明费米面重构或磁子散射效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究CrRhAs这种预测具有非平庸自旋织构和矢量自旋手性的反铁磁Kagome金属，探索其异常电输运现象，特别是霍尔效应的独特行为。

Method: 合成CrRhAs单晶样品，通过电输运测量、热容测量和磁化测量进行基本表征，重点分析不同电流-磁场配置下的霍尔电阻率行为。

Result: 发现反铁磁转变温度TN=150K；霍尔电阻率随磁场线性变化，无非线性霍尔贡献；霍尔系数在j∥ab,H⊥ab和j∥c,H⊥c配置下符号相反；对于j∥ab配置，霍尔系数在TN以下显著连续增强。

Conclusion: CrRhAs表现出独特的霍尔效应行为，可能与费米面的特殊拓扑结构相关，或源于费米面重构和磁子散射，为探索奇异磁性系统中的异常电输运现象提供了指导。

Abstract: CrRhAs is an antiferromagnetic kagome metal predicted to host a nontrivial spin texture with vector spin chirality [Huang \textit{et al.}, \textit{npj Quantum Mater.} \textbf{8}, 32 (2023)]. We report the synthesis and basic characterization of CrRhAs single crystals, which exhibit an antiferromagnetic transition with $T_{\rm N}$ = 150~K, evidenced by electrical transport, heat capacity, and magnetization measurements. Hall resistivity varies linearly with magnetic field, i.e., there is no nonlinear Hall contribution. Intriguingly, the Hall coefficient changes sign between the configurations of $j \parallel ab, H \perp ab$ and $j \parallel c, H \perp c$, which is likely connected to a peculiar topology of the Fermi surface. Furthermore, for $j \parallel ab$, the Hall coefficient shows a pronounced and continuous enhancement below $T_{\rm N}$, signaling a significant reconstruction of the Fermi surface or an extra scattering from the magnons. Our results offer guidance for exploring anomalous electric transport phenomena in exotic magnetic systems.

</details>


### [123] [Unifying Variational and Dynamical Quantum Embedding: From Ghost Gutzwiller Approximation to Dynamical Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20559)
*Samuele Giuli,Tsung-Han Lee,Yong-Xin Yao,Gabriel Kotliar,Andrei E. Ruckenstein,Olivier Gingras,Nicola Lanatà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文证明了ghost-Gutzwiller近似（ghost-GA）在无限多辅助浴模式极限下严格等价于动力学平均场理论（DMFT），统一了变分和动力学两种量子嵌入框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，动力学框架（如DMFT）和变分框架（如GA）被视为不同的范式。DMFT通过频率依赖的自能捕捉非微扰动力学关联，而GA基于变分优化的基态波函数。本文旨在桥接这两种视角，建立它们之间的严格等价关系。

Method: 通过证明ghost-Gutzwiller近似（ghost-GA）及其密度矩阵匹配的量子嵌入公式ghost-DMET，在无限多辅助浴模式极限下严格等价于DMFT。这种等价性提供了ghost-GA的严格有限温度扩展，并表明物理格林函数可以从嵌入哈密顿量的静态期望值确定。

Result: 建立了ghost-GA、ghost-DMET和DMFT之间的严格等价关系。这种统一具有直接应用：提供了ghost-GA的严格有限温度扩展；展示了无需传统杂质求解器计算动力学谱即可获得物理格林函数的方法；为强关联物质中竞争相的计算研究提供了DMFT级精度的新途径。

Conclusion: 变分ghost-GA、密度矩阵匹配的ghost-DMET公式和动力学DMFT描述不是独立的构造，而是同一量子嵌入结构的互补表述。这为未来超越DMFT的可控扩展提供了具体的理论基础，统一了量子嵌入框架中的变分和动力学视角。

Abstract: Dynamical and variational frameworks have long been viewed as distinct paradigms. In particular, in quantum embedding (QE) frameworks, dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) captures nonperturbative dynamical correlations through a frequency-dependent self-energy, while the Gutzwiller approximation (GA) is formulated in terms of a variationally optimized ground-state wavefunction. Here we bridge these perspectives, proving that the ghost-Gutzwiller approximation (ghost-GA), which also admits a density-matrix-matching QE formulation known as ghost density matrix embedding theory (ghost-DMET), becomes strictly equivalent to DMFT in the limit of infinitely many auxiliary bath modes. This formal unification has immediate consequences. In particular, it yields a rigorous finite-temperature extension of ghost-GA and shows that the physical Green's function can be determined from static expectation values of the embedding Hamiltonians, providing a route to computational studies of competing phases in strongly correlated matter with DMFT-level accuracy, while bypassing the need to calculate dynamical spectra with conventional impurity solvers. More broadly, it shows that the variational ghost-GA, the density-matrix-matching ghost-DMET formulation, and the dynamical DMFT description are not separate constructions, but complementary formulations of the same QE structure, thereby providing a concrete formal basis for future controlled extensions beyond DMFT.

</details>


### [124] [Drinfeld Center as Quantum State Monodromy over Bloch Hamiltonians around Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22029)
*Hisham Sati,Urs Schreiber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文展示了Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$的融合规则可以描述分数拓扑绝缘体材料中点缺陷附近的拓扑序，建立了晶格模型任意子与Bloch哈密顿量参数空间拓扑序之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$用于描述晶格模型中的任意子。本文旨在探索该范畴的融合规则是否也能描述分数拓扑绝缘体材料中点缺陷附近的拓扑序，建立不同物理系统之间的数学联系。

Method: 通过证明$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$在布里渊区穿孔圆盘上局部反映了Bloch哈密顿量参数空间中能隙量子态的monodromy（拓扑序），其中分类空间的基本群为$G$。具体方法涉及拓扑绝缘体理论、融合范畴理论和参数空间拓扑分析。

Result: 成功证明了$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$的融合规则确实可以描述分数拓扑绝缘体材料中点缺陷附近的拓扑序，建立了晶格模型任意子与Bloch哈密顿量参数空间拓扑序之间的精确对应关系。

Conclusion: Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$不仅适用于晶格模型中的任意子描述，也能有效刻画分数拓扑绝缘体材料中点缺陷附近的拓扑序，为不同物理系统中的拓扑相提供了统一的数学框架。

Abstract: The Drinfeld center fusion category $\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$ famously models anyons in certain lattice models. Here we demonstrate how its fusion rules may also describe topological order in fractional topological insulator materials, in the vicinity of point defects in the Brillouin zone.
  Concretely, we prove that $\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$ reflects, locally over a punctured disk in the Brillouin zone, the monodromy (topological order) of gapped quantum states over the parameter space of Bloch Hamiltonians whose classifying space has fundamental group $G$.

</details>


### [125] [Intrinsic Topological Weyl Phase Transition Induced by a Magnetostructural Transformation in a Kagome Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20610)
*Tsung-Han Yang,Satoshi Okamoto,D. Alan Tennant,Michael A. McGuire,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MnGa kagome磁体通过磁结构相变在近室温下实现了从主要type-II Weyl态到不同Weyl态的拓扑相变，伴随异常霍尔效应和拓扑霍尔效应的显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁性Weyl半金属中拓扑相变与结构、磁性和Weyl物理的相互作用，实现在近室温下两个不同Weyl态之间的本征Weyl相变。

Method: 采用高分辨率中子衍射、磁化表征和第一性原理计算，研究MnGa kagome磁体的磁结构相变过程。

Result: MnGa在485K以下经历手性反铁磁转变，随后在近室温下发生磁结构相变转变为单斜结构并具有高度倾斜的反铁磁序，这些协同变化重组了Weyl节点，驱动了从主要type-II Weyl态到不同Weyl态的相变。

Conclusion: 磁结构相变为发现新型拓扑Weyl态和潜在自旋电子学应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Topological phase transitions provide a unique window into the interplay between structure, magnetism, and Weyl physics in magnetic Weyl semimetals. However, realizing an intrinsic Weyl phase transition between two distinct Weyl states near room temperature remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate that a magnetostructural transition effectively induces such a transition in the kagome magnet \MnGa{}. High-resolution neutron diffraction, magnetization characterizations and first-principles calculations reveal that \MnGa{} undergoes a chiral antiferromagnetic transition below 485~K, followed by a magnetostructural transition to a monoclinic structure with highly canted antiferromagnetic order near room temperature. These cooperative changes in lattice and magnetic symmetries reorganize Weyl nodes, driving a transition from a primary type-II Weyl state to a distinct Weyl state, accompanied by dramatic variations in the anomalous Hall effect and appearance of topological Hall effect. Our findings open a new pathway for discovering novel topological Weyl states and potential spintronic applications.

</details>


### [126] [Tracking the local order parameter through the Hubbard exciton decoherence time in the Mott-Hubbard insulator LaVO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20693)
*Alessandra Milloch,Paolo Franceschini,Pablo Villar-Arribi,Sandeep Kumar Chaluvadi,Pasquale Orgiani,Giancarlo Panaccione,Giorgio Rossi,Yang Liu,Darrell G. Schlom,Kyle M. Shen,Massimo Capone,Claudio Giannetti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过超快光学泵浦探测和二维电子光谱研究LaVO₃中局域自旋和轨道序参数涨落对高能电子-空穴激发寿命的影响，发现泵浦扰动序参数会改变Hubbard激子的退相干时间，从而影响其均匀线宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强关联量子材料中交织序参数（自旋和轨道序）的动力学，探索如何利用多维超快光学光谱技术追踪这些序参数的演化。

Method: 结合超快光学泵浦探测光谱和二维电子光谱技术，测量LaVO₃中Hubbard激子的退相干时间和线宽变化；通过动力学平均场理论计算验证激子散射率与自旋和轨道晶格有序度的关系。

Result: 泵浦诱导的序参数扰动会改变Hubbard激子的退相干时间，从而影响其均匀线宽；理论计算证实激子散射率对自旋和轨道晶格的有序度高度敏感。

Conclusion: 多维超快光学光谱技术可用于追踪序参数的动力学演化，为研究具有交织序的强关联量子材料开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The prototypical Mott-Hubbard insulator LaVO3 undergoes a structural phase transition accompanied by the onset of spin and orbital ordering below 140 K. By combining ultrafast optical pump-probe spectroscopy and two-dimensional electronic spectroscopy, we investigate the interplay between fluctuations of the local spin and orbital order parameter and the lifetime of high-energy electron-hole excitations. Specifically, we demonstrate that the pump-induced perturbation of the order parameter leads to a change of the Hubbard exciton decoherence time and, consequently, of its homogeneous linewidth. Dynamical mean-field theory calculations confirm that the exciton scattering rate is crucially affected by the degree of order of the spin and orbital lattices in LaVO3. Our results demonstrate that multi-dimensional ultrafast optical spectroscopy can be used to track the dynamics of the order parameter, thus opening new routes in the study of correlated quantum materials characterized by intertwined orders.

</details>


### [127] [Role of interstitial $s$ orbital in a model of infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20705)
*Yan Peng,Rui Peng,Mi Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 使用DQMC研究无限层镍酸盐的三轨道Emery模型与间隙s轨道耦合，发现s轨道电子口袋在强相互作用下显著减小但持续存在，d轨道色散被强重整化，且d-p-s模型增强了短程反铁磁关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期无限层镍酸盐角分辨光电子能谱实验的启发，研究强关联和多轨道效应对其低能电子结构和多体关联的影响。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法研究三轨道Emery模型（d-p模型）与保留三维色散的间隙s轨道耦合的d-p-s模型。

Result: 1) 间隙s轨道衍生的电子口袋在强相互作用下显著减小但在20%空穴掺杂下持续存在，尺寸与实验观测相当；2) d_{x^2-y^2}轨道色散被强相互作用重整化，表现出与ARPES测量一致的弱k_z依赖性；3) 相比传统三轨道d-p模型，d-p-s模型表现出增强的短程反铁磁关联。

Conclusion: 强关联和多轨道效应在塑造无限层镍酸盐低能电子结构和多体关联中起关键作用，需要在现实多轨道框架内处理相互作用驱动的多体物理。

Abstract: Motivated by recent angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) experiments on infinite-layer (IL) nickelates, we employ determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) to study the three-orbital Emery model ($d$-$p$ model) coupled to an additional interstitial $s$ orbital retaining the three-dimensional dispersion. Our large-scale simulations reveal that: (1) the interstitial $s$-orbital-derived electron pocket is significantly reduced by the strong interaction but persists upon 20\% hole doping, reaching a size comparable to experimental observations; (2) the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-orbital dispersion is strongly renormalized by interactions, leading to a weak $k_z$ dependence consistent with ARPES measurements. Furthermore, compared with the conventional three-orbital $d$-$p$ model, the $d$-$p$-$s$ model exhibits enhanced short-range antiferromagnetic correlations. These results highlight the crucial role of strong correlations and multi-orbital effects in shaping the low-energy electronic structure and many-body correlations in IL nickelates, and demonstrate the necessity of treating interaction-driven many-body physics within a realistic multi-orbital framework.

</details>


### [128] [Many-body electronic structure, self-doped double-exchange, and Hund metallicity in 1T-CrTe2 bulk and monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20713)
*Dong Hyun David Lee,Hyeong Jun Lee,Taek Jung Kim,Min Yong Jeong,Myung Joon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 1T-CrTe2是一种vdW铁磁体，通过DFT+DMFT计算被确认为具有显著Hund金属性的自掺杂双交换铁磁体，其高居里温度源于Cr-d轨道的双重电子性质及Hund耦合驱动的双交换机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-CrTe2作为新兴自旋电子学平台，具有高居里温度和有趣的输运性质，但其高Tc背后的电子关联与磁性的基本相互作用机制尚不清楚，需要深入理解其多体电子结构。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论加动态平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）方法，首次对1T-CrTe2的多体电子结构进行详细分析，研究其电子关联效应和磁性机制。

Result: 发现1T-CrTe2是自掺杂双交换铁磁体，具有显著的Hund金属性；Cr-d轨道呈现双重电子性质：巡游的eg电子与局域的t2g磁矩共存；Hund耦合介导的轨道间相互作用驱动双交换铁磁性；单层极限下物理图像保持，但结构形变（而非维度降低）显著降低Tc。

Conclusion: 研究为1T-CrTe2的高Tc铁磁性提供了新视角，揭示了其作为Hund金属的机制，这一机制可能对其他相关二维vdW金属磁体也至关重要。

Abstract: The van der Waals (vdW) ferromagnet 1T-CrTe2 is an emerging spintronics platform, notable for its high Curie temperature (Tc) and intriguing transport properties. However, the fundamental interplay between the electron correlations and magnetism underlying its high Tc still remains elusive. Here, using density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT), we identify 1T-CrTe2 as a self-doped double-exchange ferromagnet with pronounced Hund metallicity. This identification is grounded in the first detailed analysis of its many-body electronic structure, which reveals a dual electronic nature of Cr-d orbitals where itinerant eg electrons coexist with localized t2g moments. The interaction between these orbitals, mediated by Hund's coupling, drives the double-exchange ferromagnetism, establishing 1T-CrTe2 as a Hund metal reminiscent of orbital-selective Mott systems. In the monolayer limit, while this physical picture persists, structural deformation, rather than reduced dimensionality, notably reduces Tc.Our findings offer a new perspective on the high-Tc ferromagnetism in 1T-CrTe2, a mechanism potentially pivotal for other correlated two-dimensional vdW metallic magnets.

</details>


### [129] [Chiral Spin Liquid in Rydberg Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21147)
*Yu-Feng Mao,Shicheng Ma,Yong Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在呼吸卡戈米晶格中实验实现的偶极XY模型中发现了手征自旋液体相，并通过数值计算提供了强有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征自旋液体作为一种拓扑有序相，虽然具有长期的理论研究兴趣，但一直未能在实验中观察到。本研究旨在探索在实验可实现的系统中寻找这种相的可能性。

Method: 使用无限密度矩阵重整化群方法，数值计算基态的手征序参数、自旋-自旋关联、陈数和纠缠谱，分析呼吸卡戈米晶格中偶极XY模型的相行为。

Result: 数值结果提供了手征自旋液相存在的强有力证据，并识别出从各向同性卡戈米晶格到呼吸卡戈米晶格调谐时，从狄拉克自旋液体到手征自旋液体的量子相变。

Conclusion: 在实验实现的呼吸卡戈米晶格偶极XY模型中发现了手征自旋液体相，为实验观测这一长期寻求的拓扑相提供了新途径。

Abstract: Despite long-standing theoretical interest, the chiral spin liquid, a topologically ordered phase, has yet to be observed experimentally. Here we surprisingly find its emergence in an experimentally realized dipolar $\text{XY}$ model when Rydberg atoms are arranged in a breathing kagome lattice. Using the infinite density matrix renormalization group, we numerically calculate the ground state's chiral order parameter, spin-spin correlations, Chern number, and entanglement spectrum. Our numerical results provide strong evidence for the chiral spin liquid phase. Furthermore, we identify a quantum phase transition from a Dirac spin liquid to a chiral spin liquid as the lattice geometry is tuned from the isotropic kagome to the breathing kagome lattice.

</details>


### [130] [Orbital-specific Itinerancy and Localization in a Kagome Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21203)
*S. V. Streltsov,H. Y. Huang,A. Ushakov,C. I. Wu,A. Singh,J. Su,J. Okamoto,C. T. Chen,K. Wang,A. I. Poteryaev,S-W. Cheong,A. Fujimori,D. J. Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在YMn₆Sn₆中，几何阻挫的kagome晶格与多轨道关联效应协同作用，实现了同一Mn 3d壳层内轨道选择性分化，形成巡游电子与局域电子共存的新奇量子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索kagome晶格的几何结构是否能够稳定轨道选择性相——这是多轨道关联系统中Hund物理的标志性特征。传统上，轨道选择性相主要出现在具有强关联效应的多轨道系统中，但kagome晶格的特殊几何结构（包含平带、狄拉克费米子和van Hove奇点）是否也能支持这种相变一直是个未解之谜。

Method: 采用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）实验技术，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）和动力学平均场理论（DMFT）进行多尺度研究。RIXS用于探测电子结构和轨道特性，DFT提供基础能带结构，DMFT则处理强关联效应，共同揭示轨道选择性的微观机制。

Result: 发现YMn₆Sn₆中Mn 3d轨道自发分化：沿Mn-Mn键方向的轨道形成相干准粒子和金属能带，而指向配体方向的轨道则表现出强关联特征和非费米液体行为。Hund原子内交换抑制了轨道涨落，稳定了这种二分性，并为观测到的铁磁双层耦合提供了类似双交换的机制。

Conclusion: YMn₆Sn₆作为kagome平台，实现了轨道选择性、平带拓扑和Hund金属性的汇聚。研究表明几何阻挫和关联驱动的轨道分化可以协同设计超越传统Mott物理或能带拓扑范式的新奇量子相，为探索多轨道关联系统中的量子相变提供了新方向。

Abstract: The kagome lattice naturally hosts flat bands, Dirac fermions, and van Hove singularities, yet whether its geometry can stabilize orbital-selective phases - a hallmark of Hund's physics in multi-orbital correlated systems - has remained an open question. Here, we combine resonant inelastic X-ray scattering with density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory to demonstrate that YMn$_6$Sn$_6$ exhibits a spontaneous orbital differentiation into coexisting itinerant and localized electrons within the same Mn $3d$ manifold. Orbitals directed along Mn-Mn bonds provide coherent quasiparticles and metallic bands, while those pointing toward ligands become strongly correlated and display non-Fermi-liquid behavior. Hund's intra-atomic exchange suppresses orbital fluctuations, stabilizing this dichotomy and providing a natural double-exchange-like mechanism for the observed ferromagnetic bilayer coupling. Our work establishes YMn$_6$Sn$_6$ as a kagome platform where orbital selectivity, flat-band topology, and Hund's metallicity converge - revealing that geometric frustration and correlation-driven orbital differentiation can cooperatively design exotic quantum phases beyond the canonical paradigms of Mott physics or band topology alone.

</details>


### [131] [Green parafermions as emergent flat-band excitations in condensed matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21238)
*Huan-Qiang Zhou,Ian P. McCulloch,Murray T. Batchelor*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文表明Green parafermions（绿色仲费米子）可以在具有B型Goldstone模的凝聚态系统中作为复合激发出现，而非仅仅是数学奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Green parafermions最初由Green引入，经Greenberg和Messiah通过三线性及相对三线性对易关系扩展，超越玻色-费米统计，但通常被视为没有物理实现的数学奇异性。本文旨在证明这些仲粒子实际上可以在凝聚态系统中作为可观测的准粒子出现。

Method: 通过引入定义在部分平移对称性破缺产生的涌现元胞上的辅助Majorana费米子。当辅助Majorana被视为物理自由度时，得到的Green parafermion态（投影算符后）对应平带激发，其产生湮灭算符满足三线性代数；当被视为虚构时，该构造解释了指数级简并基态的出现，并揭示了Green parafermions与自相似几何对象（如黄金螺旋）的对应关系。

Result: 在铁磁自旋1双二次模型和铁磁SU(2)平带Tasaki模型中展示了具体实现，表明具有B型Goldstone模的凝聚态系统为Green parafermions作为涌现、可能可观测的准粒子提供了自然场景。

Conclusion: Green parafermions可以在具有自发对称性破缺和B型Goldstone模的凝聚态系统中作为复合激发出现，为这些原本被视为数学奇异性的仲粒子提供了物理实现途径，并揭示了其与几何结构的深刻联系。

Abstract: Green parafermions, originally introduced by Green and extended by Greenberg and Messiah through trilinear and relative trilinear commutation relations beyond Bose-Fermi statistics, are generally regarded as mathematical curiosities without physical realization. We show that these paraparticles can in fact emerge as composite excitations in a broad class of condensed-matter systems undergoing spontaneous symmetry breaking with type-B Goldstone modes. The key ingredient is the introduction of auxiliary Majorana fermions defined on emergent unit cells produced by partial translational-symmetry breaking. When the auxiliary Majoranas are treated as physical degrees of freedom, the resulting Green parafermion states (up to a projection operator) correspond to flat-band excitations, whose creation and annihilation operators satisfy the trilinear algebra. When they are regarded as fictitious, the same construction explains the appearance of exponentially many degenerate ground states and reveals a surprising correspondence between Green parafermions and self-similar geometric objects, such as the golden spiral. Explicit realizations are demonstrated for the ferromagnetic spin-1 biquadratic model and the ferromagnetic $\rm {SU}(2)$ flat-band Tasaki model, showing that condensed-matter systems with type-B Goldstone modes provide a natural setting for Green parafermions as emergent, possibly observable quasiparticles.

</details>


### [132] [Symmetry group factorization and unitary equivalence among Temperley-Lieb integrable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21242)
*Huan-Qiang Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Temperley-Lieb可积模型序列间的隐藏联系：q态量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链之间存在酉等价性，并建立了对称群因子化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Temperley-Lieb可积模型序列间的深层联系，特别是量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链之间的隐藏关系，探索对称群因子化现象及其物理意义。

Method: 通过酉等价性分析，建立q态量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链之间的对应关系，研究对称群因子化条件（q=q₁×q₂，n=n₁×n₂）下的模型等价性。

Result: 发现：1）q态量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链存在酉等价性；2）对称群可因子化时，模型可分解为对称群乘积的等价模型；3）铁磁与反铁磁情况存在对偶关系；4）通过组合不同酉等价性得到本质上相同但形式不同的模型族。

Conclusion: 揭示了Temperley-Lieb可积模型序列间的深层统一性，建立了量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链的酉等价关系，阐明了对称群因子化的物理意义，为理解不同可积模型间的内在联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: It is shown that there is a hidden connection between the two well-studied sequences of the Temperley-Lieb (TL) integrable models -- the $q$-state quantum Potts (QP) models at the self-dual points and the staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chains with $n=2s+1$ ($s \ge 1$), in addition to the uniform ${\rm SU}(2)$ spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg model. For each sequence, symmetry group factorization arises, in the sense that if $q$ is factorized into $q_1$ and $q_2$, then the $q$-state QP model is unitarily equivalent to a combined QP model with the symmetry group ${\rm S}_{q_1} \times {\rm S}_{q_2}$ or if $n$ is factorized into $n_1$ and $n_2$, then the staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain with the symmetry group ${\rm SU}(n)$ is unitarily equivalent to a combined staggered ${\rm SU}(n_1) \times {\rm SU}(n_2)$ spin chain with the symmetry group ${\rm SU}(n_1) \times {\rm SU}(n_2)$, valid for both ferromagnetic (FM) and antiferromagnetic (AF) cases. Moreover, the FM (AF) staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain is unitarily equivalent to the AF (FM) $q$-state QP model with $q=n^2$, as long as the size of the AF (FM) staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain is doubled. A combination of the two distinct types of unitary equivalences yields a family of models such that they are essentially identical, but appear in different guises. Some physical implications for unitary equivalence among different TL integrable models are clarified.

</details>


### [133] [Non-invertible symmetries and boundary conditions for the transverse-field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21290)
*Huan-Qiang Zhou,Qian-Qian Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文为非可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶对称性在横向场伊辛模型自对偶点构建了系统框架，通过引入额外自由度实现平移不变性，并推导出与Tambara-Yamagami Z2融合范畴相似的格点融合规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为横向场伊辛模型在不同边界条件下构建非可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶对称性，确保所有边界条件在热力学极限下给出收敛结果，并建立与融合范畴理论的联系。

Method: 通过引入额外自由度扩展希尔伯特空间，将原始非平移不变的哈密顿量转化为扩展空间中的平移不变哈密顿量，并提升恒等算符、Z2对称算符和非可逆KW对偶算符到扩展空间，针对四种环面边界条件构建格点融合规则。

Result: 成功构建了非可逆KW对偶对称性，推导出与Tambara-Yamagami Z2融合范畴相似的格点融合规则，重现了Seiberg等人对周期和反周期边界条件的结果，但揭示了对偶扭曲边界条件存在差异。

Conclusion: 该构造满足物理基本要求（所有边界条件在热力学极限下收敛），建立了格点模型与融合范畴理论之间的联系，为理解非可逆对称性在量子多体系统中的实现提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Non-invertible Kramers-Wannier (KW) duality symmetries are constructed for the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) at the self-dual point under various boundary conditions (BCs), as long as the resultant Hamiltonian commutes with the ${\rm Z}_2$ symmetry operator. This is achieved by introducing extra degrees of freedom into the Hilbert space, in order to turn a non-translation-invariant Hamiltonian in the original Hilbert space into a translation-invariant Hamiltonian in the augmented Hilbert space. One may lift the trivial identity operator, the ${\rm Z}_2$ symmetry operator and the non-invertible KW duality symmetry operator to their counterparts in the augmented Hilbert space, valid for each of four types of toroidal BCs. As it turns out, they yield a lattice version of fusion rules, which bears a resemblance to the Tambara-Yamagami ${\rm Z}_2$ fusion category. Our construction is thus consistent with the basic physical requirement that all possible BCs should yield a converging result in the thermodynamic limit. In particular, the lattice versions of fusion rules, constructed by Seiberg, Seifnashri and Shao [SciPost Phys. \textbf{16}, 154 (2024)], are reproduced for periodic and anti-periodic BCs, but a discrepancy is revealed for duality-twisted BCs.

</details>


### [134] [Universally Diverging Grüneisen Ratio of Holographic Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21320)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Enze Lv,Wei Li,Li Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用全息对偶研究具有动力学指数z=3的场致量子临界点，发现新的普适性类别，其中Grüneisen比以T^{-2/3}的普适标度发散，与重费米子材料CeRh6Ge4的实验结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子临界性是强关联电子系统（如重费米子材料和高温超导体）的重要特征。全息对偶为研究这些系统提供了强大框架，通过将其映射到高一维的弱耦合经典引力理论中。本文旨在利用这一方法研究场致量子临界点。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-陈-西蒙斯理论中的全息对偶方法，研究具有动力学指数z=3的场致量子临界点。通过分析该系统的热力学性质来探索其临界行为。

Result: 发现了新的普适性类别，特别识别出Grüneisen比以T^{-2/3}的普适标度发散。这一行为与重费米子材料CeRh6Ge4的最新实验结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 这些发现推进了对金属量子临界性的理解，并突显了全息对偶作为研究关联量子物质工具的巨大潜力。理论与实验的一致性验证了全息方法在研究强关联系统量子临界现象中的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum criticality is a hallmark of strongly correlated electron systems, as seen in heavy-fermion materials and high-temperature superconductors. Holographic duality provides a powerful framework to investigate these systems by translating them into weakly coupled classical gravity living in one higher dimension. Here, we harness this approach to study a field-induced quantum critical point with dynamical exponent $z=3$ in Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory. Our analysis of its thermodynamic properties reveals a new universality class. Notably, we identify a diverging Grüneisen ratio with universal scaling $\sim T^{-2/3}$, a behavior that closely mirrors recent experiments on the heavy-fermion material CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$. These findings advance our understanding of metallic quantum criticality and highlight the potential of holographic duality as a tool for studying correlated quantum matters.

</details>


### [135] [Colossal Terahertz Magnetoresistance from Magnetic Polarons in EuZn$_2$P$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21423)
*E. Marulanda,M. Dutra,N. M. Kawahala,E. D. Stefanato,G. G. Vasques,J. Munevar,M. A. Avila,F. G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: EuZn₂P₂中磁极化子导致太赫兹波段巨磁阻，太赫兹光谱揭示磁极化子对低能电动力学的控制作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁极化子可在磁性半导体中产生巨磁阻效应，但其太赫兹电动力学特性尚未充分研究。本文旨在探索Eu基Zintl反铁磁体EuZn₂P₂中磁极化子的太赫兹电动力学行为。

Method: 采用磁-太赫兹光谱技术研究EuZn₂P₂，分析低频电导率的非Drude行为，测量极化子弛豫时间随温度和磁场的变化，并计算太赫兹波段的磁阻效应。

Result: 电导率显示从孤立到重叠磁极化子的演化特征；极化子弛豫时间在奈尔温度处达到最大值且具有强磁场依赖性；太赫兹波段磁阻达到约90%（1.5 THz），是零频极限值的三倍。

Conclusion: 磁极化子强烈控制低能电动力学，太赫兹光谱对关联磁性半导体中的极化子磁输运具有高灵敏度，为理解磁极化子介导的巨磁阻机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Magnetic polarons can generate colossal magnetoresistance in magnetic semiconductors, yet their terahertz electrodynamics remain largely unexplored. Here we report magneto-terahertz spectroscopy of the Eu-based Zintl antiferromagnet EuZn$_2$P$_2$. The low-frequency conductivity shows pronounced non-Drude behavior consistent with an evolution from isolated to overlapping magnetic polarons upon cooling. The polaron relaxation time reaches a maximum at the Néel temperature and exhibits a strong magnetic-field dependence. This polaron-driven reshaping of the conductivity leads to a strongly frequency-dependent magnetoresistance that becomes colossal in the terahertz range, reaching about 90 % at 1.5 THz, roughly three times larger than the zero-frequency limit value. These results demonstrate that magnetic polarons strongly govern the low-energy electrodynamics and highlight the sensitivity of terahertz spectroscopy to polaronic magnetotransport in correlated magnetic semiconductors.

</details>


### [136] [Band structure control in the altermagnetic candidate MnTe by temperature and strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21455)
*Shin-ichi Kimura,Hironao Suwa,Kangle Yuan,Hiroshi Watanabe,Takuto Nakamura,Haan Kyul Yun,Myung-Hwa Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过测量六方碲化锰的光导谱，发现太赫兹区域存在从自旋劈裂能带到受主能级的吸收，其温度依赖性与铁磁相变一致但材料无净磁性，证实了交替磁性的电子结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六方碲化锰(MnTe)的光学性质，特别是太赫兹区域的吸收特性，以探索其独特的磁性行为。尽管MnTe没有净磁性，但其温度依赖性与铁磁相变相似，这促使研究者探究其潜在的交替磁性电子结构。

Method: 测量六方碲化锰光学导谱的温度和应变依赖性，分析太赫兹区域的吸收峰特征。观察光学声子吸收的Fano-like反对称线形，并在负单轴压力下研究太赫兹峰的移动行为。结合理论预测验证实验结果。

Result: 发现太赫兹吸收峰的温度依赖性与铁磁相变一致，但材料无净磁性。观察到光学声子与自旋劈裂能带相互作用导致的Fano-like线形。在负单轴压力下，太赫兹峰远离费米能级移动，与自旋劈裂角减小的理论预测一致。这些结果明确表明MnTe具有交替磁性电子结构。

Conclusion: 六方碲化锰表现出交替磁性特征，其太赫兹吸收峰的温度依赖性、Fano-like声子线形以及压力响应行为共同证实了自旋劈裂能带的存在和交替磁性电子结构，为理解这类无净磁性但具有铁磁相变特征的材料提供了实验证据。

Abstract: The temperature and strain dependences of the optical conductivity spectrum of hexagonal manganese telluride (MnTe) were measured, revealing absorption in the terahertz (THz) region from spin-split bands to acceptor levels. The temperature dependence of the THz absorption peak is consistent with that of a ferromagnetic phase transition, even though MnTe exhibits no net magnetism. The temperature dependence was attributed to a change in the altermagnetic electronic structure. A Fano-like antisymmetric line shape in the optical phonon absorption was observed, which originates from the interaction between optical phonons and the spin-split bands. Additionally, under negative uniaxial pressure, the THz peak shifts away from the Fermi level (EF), suggesting that spin-splitting bands at energies away from EF, consistent with the theoretical prediction that the spin-splitting angle decreases. The observed behavior of the THz peak clearly shows that MnTe has the altermagnetic electronic structure.

</details>


### [137] [Dynamical spin correlations in kagome antiferromagnets: comparison of Abrikosov fermion and Schwinger boson approaches beyond mean field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21513)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究比较了Abrikosov费米子和Schwinger玻色子两种部分子方法在Kagome反铁磁海森堡模型中的自旋动力学结构因子，发现两种方法给出不同低能结构，且超越平均场的多体效应对低能谱有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome反铁磁海森堡模型是量子自旋液体的候选体系，但其激发谱性质（特别是自旋能隙和与分数量子粒子耦合的规范结构）存在争议。Abrikosov费米子和Schwinger玻色子两种部分子方法被广泛使用，但此前独立研究，不清楚它们在自旋动力学和规范结构方面的结果如何比较。

Method: 研究采用Abrikosov费米子平均场理论和Schwinger玻色子平均场理论，计算了Kagome晶格上具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的反铁磁海森堡模型的自旋动力学结构因子，该模型与herbertsmithite材料相关。同时考察了超越平均场近似的多体效应。

Result: Abrikosov费米子平均场理论得到的自旋动力学结构因子呈现圆顶状特征，其连续谱结构显著依赖于自旋液体ansatz的规范结构。Schwinger玻色子平均场理论在低能区产生下凹结构，与Abrikosov费米子方法的结果不同。超越平均场近似的多体效应显著降低了低能能隙并增强了低能谱权重，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在Schwinger玻色子理论中考虑多体效应对于准确描述Kagome反铁磁体的低能自旋动力学至关重要。两种部分子方法给出了不同的低能谱结构，强调了规范结构和多体效应在理解量子自旋液体激发谱中的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids exhibit fractionalized spin excitations as a consequence of strong quantum many-body effects. The kagome antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model is a promising candidate for a quantum spin-liquid ground state; however, the nature of its excitation spectrum remains controversial, particularly regarding the presence of a spin gap and the gauge structure coupled to fractional quasiparticles. To address these issues, parton approaches have been extensively employed, where spin operators are represented in terms of fermionic or bosonic quasiparticles within the Abrikosov fermion and Schwinger boson frameworks. Thus far, these approaches have been pursued independently, and it has remained unclear how the results obtained from these frameworks compare, particularly with respect to the spin dynamics and gauge structure of the kagome antiferromagnet. Here, we investigate the dynamical spin structure factor of the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model with a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction on the kagome lattice, relevant to herbertsmithite, by employing both approaches. We find that the dynamical spin structure factor obtained from the Abrikosov fermion mean-field theory exhibits dome-shaped features, and that its continuum structure significantly depends on the gauge structure of the spin-liquid ansatz. On the other hand, the Schwinger boson mean-field theory yields a concave-down structure in the low-energy region, distinct from that obtained using the Abrikosov fermion approach. Moreover, incorporating many-body effects beyond the mean-field approximation substantially reduces the low-energy gap and enhances the low-energy spectral weight, consistent with experimental observations. Our results suggest the importance of many-body effects in the Schwinger boson theory for capturing the low-energy spin dynamics of kagome antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [138] [Hall viscosity and putative quantum Hall states without positive-definite K-matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21794)
*Emanuele Di Salvo,Dirk Schuricht,Joost K. Slingerland,Mikael Fremling*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了K矩阵为(1 1 3)的量子霍尔态，通过环面几何计算其霍尔黏度，探讨其作为与Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相不同的新相的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于解释热霍尔电导率实验结果，这些结果支持存在粒子-空穴对称的Pfaffian拓扑序，同时考虑朗道能级混合效应。K矩阵为(1 1 3)的态被提出作为与Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相不同的新相。

Method: 在环面上定义这些态，通过边界条件、模不变性和基态简并度要求唯一确定波函数形式。发展了"单值性匹配"方法，并在Laughlin态和分层态上进行验证。计算了Halperin版本和完全反对称化版本的霍尔黏度。

Result: 这些态表现出团簇形成现象，反对称化操作不足以阻止团簇化。霍尔黏度依赖于几何形状和粒子数，计算了Halperin版本和完全反对称化版本的霍尔黏度。

Conclusion: K矩阵为(1 1 3)的态确实表现出独特的性质，包括团簇形成和几何依赖的霍尔黏度，这支持它们作为与Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相不同的新相的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate putative quantum Hall effect states, labeled by their K-matrix equal to (1 1 3), by defining them on the torus and computing their Hall viscosity. Such states have been introduced on the sphere as a phase distinct from Pfaffian and anti-Pfaffian ones. This was done in order to explain certain results on thermal Hall conductivity in favor of particle-hole symmetric Pfaffian topological order in presence of Landau level mixing. The requirements of boundary conditions, modular invariance and ground state degeneracy are enough to uniquely fix the form of the proposed wave functions. We generalize a method to enforce them which we call monodromy matching and check our results on wave functions and Hall viscosity against realizations on the torus of Laughlin and hierarchical states. We highlight the issues in the realization of these states, which turn out to exhibit the formation of clusters. We show that the effect of anti-symmetrization on the system is not enough to prevent clustering; we compute the Hall viscosity for the Halperin version of these states and the fully anti-symmetrized one and we find them being dependent on the geometry and the particle number.

</details>


### [139] [Tangent equations of motion for nonlinear response functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22137)
*Atsushi Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于Gateaux导数的切线方程运动方法，直接计算非线性响应函数，避免多点关联函数组合爆炸和数值不稳定问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性响应函数在非线性输运和光学现象中至关重要，但传统计算方法存在组合爆炸（阶乘增长）和严重数值误差问题，高阶计算变得不可行

Method: 基于Gateaux导数在函数空间中对外部场求导，建立切线方程运动（TEOM）的封闭层次结构。将TEOM与原始动力学方程一起传播，高精度地分离每个微扰阶次，提供物理贡献的逐项分解

Result: 成功计算了固态电子模型的五阶频率分辨响应函数，并在经典Duffing振子中获得了高达49阶的非线性响应函数且精度可控。该方法在零频率附近仍保持数值稳定，可直接评估光学电导率

Conclusion: TEOM方法为计算量子和经典动力学系统中的非线性响应函数提供了一个通用框架，可无缝集成到现有实时演化方法中，避免了传统方法的组合爆炸问题

Abstract: Nonlinear response functions, formulated as multipoint correlation functions or Volterra kernels, encode the dynamical and spectroscopic properties of physical systems and underpin a wide range of nonlinear transport and optical phenomena. However, their evaluation rapidly becomes prohibitive at high orders because of combinatorial (often factorial) scaling or severe numerical errors. Here, we establish a systematic and efficient framework to compute nonlinear response functions directly from real-time dynamics, without explicitly constructing multipoint correlators or relying on numerically unstable finite-difference methods for order-resolved extraction. Our approach is based on the Gateaux derivative with respect to the external field in function space, which yields a closed hierarchy of tangent equations of motion (TEOM). Propagating the TEOM alongside the original dynamics isolates each perturbative order with high accuracy, providing a term-by-term decomposition of physical contributions. The computational cost scales exponentially with response order in the fully general setting and reduces to polynomial complexity when all perturbation directions are identical; both regimes avoid the factorial scaling of explicit multipoint-correlator evaluations. We demonstrate the power of TEOM by computing frequency-resolved fifth-order response functions for a solid-state electron model and by obtaining nonlinear response functions up to the 49th order with controlled accuracy in a classical Duffing oscillator. We further show that our time-evolution formulation allows optical conductivities to be evaluated directly while remaining numerically stable even near zero frequency. TEOM can be incorporated seamlessly into existing real-time evolution methods, yielding a general framework for computing nonlinear response functions in quantum and classical dynamical systems.

</details>


### [140] [An Exact Conjugation Identity for the Many-Body Wilson-Loop Beyond Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22217)
*Kai Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文建立了多体重叠Wilson环的精确共轭恒等式W(-δ)=W(δ)*，证明了在U(1)通量穿线循环中，键二聚化参数δ反号时Wilson环互为复共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体系统中Wilson环在参数空间中的对称性质，特别是键二聚化参数δ反号时的共轭关系，这有助于理解Berry相在非对称量子化区域的对称性。

Method: 建立复合反幺正映射的充分条件，在二聚化交错Hubbard环中微观构造映射，并使用密度矩阵重整化群验证共轭恒等式在非简并基态下的成立。

Result: 证明了精确的Wilson环共轭恒等式W(-δ)=W(δ)*，该恒等式在非钉扎能隙区域仍然成立，导致Berry相满足γ(-δ)=-γ(δ) (模2π)。

Conclusion: Wilson环共轭恒等式为理解多体系统中Berry相的对称性提供了新视角，该关系适用于任何通量穿线哈密顿量在键图案方向反转和通量方向反转下闭合的晶格模型。

Abstract: We establish an exact Wilson-loop conjugation identity, $W(-δ)=W(δ)^*$, for the many-body overlap Wilson-loop $W(δ)$ accumulated along a $U(1)$ flux-threading (twist) cycle parametrized by $θ\in[0,2π]$, where $δ$ denotes the bond-dimerization parameter.
