A minimal sufficient condition is the existence of a composite antiunitary mapping acting on the flux-threaded Hamiltonian family that implements $(δ,θ)\mapsto(-δ,-θ)$. As a concrete demonstration, we construct such a mapping microscopically in a dimerized staggered Hubbard ring at half filling. We then verify the conjugation identity using the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) for gapped, nondegenerate ground states along the twist cycle. Importantly, the identity persists in depinned gapped regimes where the Berry-phase $γ\equiv-\arg W$ is not symmetry-quantized; as a corollary, $γ(-δ)=-γ(δ)$ (mod $2π$). More generally, the same conjugation relation applies to any lattice model whose flux-threaded Hamiltonian family is closed under an orientation reversal of the bond pattern (a suitable permutation of link-hopping parameters) combined with a reversal of the flux orientation.

</details>


### [141] [Emergent relativistic symmetry from interacting fermions on the honeycomb bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22259)
*Zi Hong Liu,Lukas Janssen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究双层蜂窝晶格中电荷中性点处相互作用自旋less费米子的相图，发现弱相互作用诱导二次能带接触点分裂为狄拉克点，强相互作用导致层极化电荷密度波绝缘相，相变属于Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双层蜂窝晶格中相互作用自旋less费米子在电荷中性点的量子相变行为，探索从半金属到绝缘体的连续相变机制及其普适类特征。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法，研究双层蜂窝晶格中相互作用自旋less费米子模型，分析不同相互作用强度下的相图、能带结构和临界行为。

Result: 弱至中等相互作用使二次能带接触点分裂为四个沿高对称方向的狄拉克点；强相互作用导致层极化电荷密度波绝缘相；半金属-绝缘体相变为连续相变，临界指数与八分量狄拉克费米子的2+1D Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类吻合（偏差<5%）；确定了从z=2非相对论半金属到z=1狄拉克半金属的交叉尺度。

Conclusion: 双层蜂窝晶格中相互作用自旋less费米子体系呈现丰富的相行为，半金属-绝缘体相变属于Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类，存在温度相关的交叉行为，为理解相互作用拓扑材料的量子相变提供了重要范例。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram of interacting spinless fermions on the honeycomb bilayer at charge neutrality using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations. In the noninteracting limit, the low-energy spectrum features quadratically dispersing bands that touch at the corners of the hexagonal Brillouin zone. Weak to intermediate interactions induce a splitting of each of the quadratic band touching points into four Dirac points, located along high-symmetry directions of the reciprocal lattice. Strong interactions lead to the formation of a layer-polarized charge density wave, which spontaneously breaks the $\mathbb Z_2$ layer inversion symmetry and opens an insulating gap in the spectrum. We show that the semimetal-to-insulator quantum phase transition as a function of interaction is continuous and characterized by emergent relativistic symmetry. Our results for the values of the correlation-length exponent $ν$, the order-parameter anomalous dimension $η_φ$, and the fermion anomalous dimension $η_ψ$ agree with those of the theoretically predicted 2+1D Gross-Neveu-Ising universality class with eight two-component Dirac fermions within less than 5\%\ deviation. We also determine the crossover scale as a function of interaction strength between the nonrelativistic semimetal state at high temperatures, characterized by dynamical critical exponent $z = 2$, and the Dirac semimetal state at intermediates temperatures, characterized by $z=1$. Further reducing the temperature below the crossover scale at a fixed value of the interaction strength above the quantum critical point results in a classical ordering transition in the 2D Ising universality class.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Singular equivalences and homological conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20643)
*Zhenxian Chen,Changchang Xi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了中心化矩阵代数的奇异性范畴、奇异等价及同调猜想，证明了中心化矩阵代数满足所有历史同调猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于每个有限维代数都同构于两个矩阵的中心化子，这启发通过矩阵的中心化代数来研究有限维代数的表示论问题。第一个自然的问题是研究单个矩阵的中心化代数（称为中心化矩阵代数）的相关问题。

Method: 论文给出了中心化矩阵代数的奇异性范畴和奇异等价的完整描述，并验证了中心化矩阵代数满足Auslander-Reiten（或Gorenstein投射）猜想和Cartan行列式猜想。

Result: 所有历史同调猜想（有限维数、Wakamatsu倾斜、倾斜（投射）补、强Nakayama、广义Nakayama和Nakayama猜想）对于域上的中心化矩阵代数都成立。此外，还证明了中心化矩阵代数奇异等价的一些同调不变量。

Conclusion: 中心化矩阵代数具有完整的奇异性范畴描述和奇异等价性质，且满足所有重要的历史同调猜想，这为通过矩阵中心化代数研究更一般的有限维代数提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The fact that each finite-dimensional algebra over a field is isomorphic to the centralizer of two matrices, has suggested to investigate representation theoretical problems of finite-dimensional algebras through centralizer algebras of matrices. The first natural question is to study the problems for the centralizer algebra of one matrix, called a centralizer matrix algebra. In this paper we give complete descriptions of the singularity categories and singular equivalences of centralizer matrix algebras, and verify the Auslander--Reiten (or Gorenstein projective) and Cartan determinant conjectures for centralizer matrix algebras. Consequently, all historical homological conjectures (the finitistic dimension, Wakamatsu tilting, tilting (projective) complement, strong Nakayama, generalized Nakayama and Nakayama conjectures) are true for centralizer matrix algebras over fields. Moreover, we prove some homological invariants of singular equivalences for centralizer matrix algebras.

</details>


### [143] [Grassmannian cluster subcategories and positroid varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21458)
*Bernt Tore Jensen,Liam Riordan,Xiuping Su*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Grassmannian簇范畴CM C_{k,n}的子范畴GP B具有簇子结构，产生的簇代数A_{clu}是C[Gr(k,n)]的子代数，且局部化代数(A_{clu})_B与开正丛簇的坐标环自然同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Grassmannian簇范畴CM C_{k,n}的子范畴GP B的簇结构，探索其产生的簇代数A_{clu}与Grassmannian C[Gr(k,n)]及正丛簇坐标环之间的关系。

Method: 1. 证明GP B具有CM C_{k,n}的簇子结构；2. 分析冰Gabriel箭图Q°_U与Q°_T的关系；3. 应用Canakci-King-Pressland结果连接GP B与Muller-Speyer构造；4. 构造C[Gr(k,n)]的子代数和理想的基；5. 证明局部化代数(A_{clu})_B与开正丛簇坐标环的同构。

Result: 1. GP B具有簇子结构，A_{clu}是C[Gr(k,n)]的子代数；2. 冰Gabriel箭图Q°_U是Q°_T的子箭图；3. (A_{clu})_B与Muller-Speyer簇代数A_{MS}同构；4. (A_{clu})_B自然同构于开正丛簇的坐标环；5. 为Galashin-Lam定理提供了新证明。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了Grassmannian簇范畴子范畴GP B的簇结构与正丛簇几何之间的深刻联系，为理解Grassmannian簇代数和正丛簇的代数结构提供了新的范畴视角和证明方法。

Abstract: A class of subcategories GP $B$ of the Grassmannian cluster category CM $C_{k, n}$ was constructed by Jensen--King--Su from certain superorders $B$ of $C_{k, n}$, which they showed are in bijection with Grassmannian positroids of type $(k, n)$. We prove that GP $B$ admits a cluster substructure of CM $C_{k, n}$, giving rise to a cluster algebra $A_{clu}$. This naturally raises questions regarding the relationship of $A_{clu}$ to $C[Gr(k, n)]$ and to the coordinate ring of the positroid variety associated to $B$.
  Using the cluster substructure, we show that the ice Gabriel quiver $Q^\circ_U$ of a cluster tilting object $U\in$ GP $B$, consisting of rank one modules, is a subquiver of $Q^\circ_T$ with $T$ a cluster tilting object in CM $C_{k, n}$ containing $U$ as a summand. We also deduce that $A_{clu}$ is a subalgebra of $C[Gr(k, n)]$. Moreover, applying a result of Canakci--King--Pressland on the Gabriel quiver $Q_U$ in the case where $B$ is connected (i.e., has no repeated direct summands), we deduce that $Q^\circ_U$, for arbitrary $B$, coincides with the quiver constructed by Muller-Speyer from a plabic graph whose face labels agree with the indices of the indecomposable summands of $U$. Consequently, the localised algebra $(A_{clu})_B$ is isomorphic to the cluster algebra $A_{MS}$ of Muller-Speyer. We then construct bases for certain subalgebras and for an ideal of $C[Gr(k, n)]$, and apply these to prove that $(A_{clu})_B$ is naturally isomorphic to the coordinate ring of the open positroid variety.
  As a consequence, we obtain a new proof of Galashin--Lam's Theorem, identifying $A_{MS}$ with the coordinate ring of the open positroid variety, which was originally conjectured by Muller-Speyer. In the connected case, we note also that Pressland gave a categorification of the cluster structure following Galashin-Lam.

</details>


### [144] [Holographic operators for the tensor products of the spaces of holomorphic functions on Hermitian symmetric spaces of tube type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21472)
*Ryosuke Nakahama*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Hermitian对称管型空间上全纯函数空间的张量积分解，构造了从不可约子表示到张量积的积分型交织算子（全息算子），推广了Kobayashi-Pevzner (2016)的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hermitian对称管型空间上全纯函数空间张量积的分解问题，旨在构造具体的交织算子来实现不可约表示到张量积的映射，推广已有的数学结果。

Method: 采用积分算子方法构造交织算子（全息算子），将每个不可约和项映射到两个全纯函数空间的张量积中，通过积分表示实现这一映射。

Result: 成功构造了从不可约子表示到张量积的积分型交织算子，实现了Kobayashi-Pevzner (2016)结果的推广，为Hermitian对称管型空间上的表示理论提供了新的算子构造方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构造积分型交织算子，解决了Hermitian对称管型空间上全纯函数空间张量积分解中的算子实现问题，推广了相关数学理论，为表示论和调和分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We consider a tensor product of two spaces of holomorphic functions on a Hermitian symmetric space of tube type. Then generically this is decomposed into a direct sum of irreducible subrepresentations. In this manuscript, we construct the intertwining operator (holographic operator) from each irreducible summand to the tensor product as an integral operator. This gives a generalization of the result by Kobayashi--Pevzner (2016).

</details>


### [145] [Critical CoHAs, vertex coalgebras and Deformed Drinfeld coproducts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21707)
*Shivang Jindal,Sarunas Kaubrys,Alexei Latyntsev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在带势的箭图的Kontsevich-Soibelman上同调Hall代数上构造顶点余积，证明其构成顶点双代数，并通过顶点代数版本的Majid-Radford玻色化扩展该代数以包含Cartan部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为带势的箭图的上同调Hall代数建立顶点代数结构，特别是顶点余积，以连接Yangian理论和上同调积分定理。

Method: 基于Joyce(2018)的工作构造顶点余积，证明其构成顶点双代数；应用顶点代数版本的Majid-Radford玻色化方法扩展代数结构；与Davison的局部化余积和Dotsenko-Mozgovoy的构造进行比较。

Result: 成功构造了顶点余积并证明其构成顶点双代数；在ADE箭图情形下恢复Drinfeld的Yangian变形余积；为对称箭图且势平凡的情形提供了上同调积分定理的新证明。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了带势箭图上同调Hall代数的顶点代数结构，连接了Yangian理论和上同调积分定理，为相关代数结构提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We construct a vertex coproduct on the Kontsevich--Soibelman cohomological Hall algebra (CoHA) of a quiver with potential, following Joyce (2018). We show it forms a vertex bialgebra. By applying a vertex algebraic analogue of Majid--Radford bosonisation, we form an extension of the CoHA of quivers with potential which incorporates a Cartan part. In the case of ADE quivers our vertex coproduct recovers Drinfeld's deformed coproduct on the Yangian. We compare the vertex coproduct with a localised coproduct defined by Davison and with the construction of Dotsenko--Mozgovoy when the potential is trivial. Our construction gives a new proof of the cohomological integrality theorem for symmetric quivers with trivial potential.

</details>


### [146] [Super-immanants and Littlewood correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21893)
*Naihuan Jing,Yinlong Liu,Jian Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入超矩阵的超-不变量的概念，利用超Schur-Weyl对偶性证明其在一般线性李超代数的协变张量表示中起重要作用，推广了Kostant迹公式到超情形，并建立了超-不变量与超对称多项式之间的代数同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超矩阵的超-不变量概念，探索其在一般线性李超代数表示理论中的作用，将经典的不变量理论和迹公式推广到超对称情形。

Method: 引入超矩阵的超-不变量定义，利用超Schur-Weyl对偶性分析其在协变张量表示中的角色，推导超迹公式，并通过Littlewood对应建立超-不变量与超对称多项式之间的代数关系。

Result: 证明了超-不变量在一般线性李超代数的协变张量表示中起重要作用；获得了超迹公式，将Kostant迹公式推广到超情形；建立了超-不变量与超对称多项式之间的代数同构。

Conclusion: 超-不变量是超矩阵理论中的重要概念，在超对称表示理论中具有核心地位，其与超对称多项式的对应关系揭示了两种代数结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the notion of super-immanants for supermatrices over a supercommutative algebra. Using the super Schur-Weyl duality we show that the super immanants play a significant role in covariant tensor representations of the general linear Lie superalgebra. Among various things, we obtain a supertrace formula for super-immanants, which generalizes Kostant's trace formula to the super setting. Furthermore, we show that the Littlewood correspondences between super-immanants and supersymmetric polynomials establish an isomorphism between their corresponding algebras.

</details>


### [147] [On the full set of unitarizable supermodules over $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21929)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 提出基于代数二次Dirac算子和Dirac不等式的特殊线性李超代数的酉化超模分类新方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为特殊线性李超代数的酉化超模提供系统分类框架，利用代数二次Dirac算子这一新工具

Method: 采用Huang和Pandžić引入的代数二次Dirac算子，结合相应的Dirac不等式进行分析

Result: 建立了特殊线性李超代数酉化超模的新分类体系

Conclusion: 代数二次Dirac算子和Dirac不等式是研究特殊线性李超代数酉化超模分类的有效工具

Abstract: We present a novel classification of unitarizable supermodules over special linear Lie superalgebras using an algebraic quadratic Dirac operator introduced by Huang and Pandžić and a corresponding Dirac inequality.

</details>


### [148] [Abelian Galois cohomology of quasi-connected reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22059)
*Mikhail Borovoi,Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Labesse关于拟连通约化群的阿贝尔伽罗瓦上同调理论，将其扩展到任意约化群（不必连通或拟连通），并推广到任意特征局部域和整体域，同时研究了函子性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Labesse在1999年引入了拟连通约化群并研究了其在特征0的局部和整体域上的阿贝尔伽罗瓦上同调。本文旨在将这一理论推广到更一般的情形：从拟连通约化群推广到任意约化群，从特征0推广到任意特征，并研究其函子性性质。

Method: 1. 将Labesse关于拟连通约化群的构造推广到任意约化群（不必连通或拟连通）；2. 将Labesse关于拟连通约化群阿贝尔伽罗瓦上同调的结果推广到任意特征的局部和整体域；3. 研究阿贝尔伽罗瓦上同调的函子性性质，特别是引入拟连通约化群的主同态概念。

Result: 证明了如果G是正特征局部或整体域k上的拟连通约化群，则第一伽罗瓦上同调集H^1(k,G)具有典范的阿贝尔群结构，且该结构对于主同态具有函子性。

Conclusion: 本文成功推广了Labesse的理论框架，建立了更一般的约化群阿贝尔伽罗瓦上同调理论，为正特征情形提供了系统处理，并揭示了主同态下的函子性结构。

Abstract: In 1999 Labesse introduced quasi-connected reductive groups and investigated their abelian Galois cohomology over local and global fields of characteristic 0. We (1) generalize some of the constructions of Labesse from quasi-connected reductive groups to arbitrary reductive groups, not necessarily connected or quasi-connected; (2) generalize results of Labesse on the abelian Galois cohomology of quasi-connected reductive groups to the case of local and global fields of arbitrary characteristic; and (3) investigate the functoriality properties of the abelian Galois cohomology. In particular, we introduce the notion of a principal homomorphism of quasi-connected reductive groups, and show that if G is a quasi-connected reductive group over a local or global field $k$ of *positive* characteristic, then the first Galois cohomology set H^1(k,G) has a canonical structure of abelian group, which is functorial with respect to *principal* homomorphisms.

</details>


### [149] [Entropy of affine permutations and universality of affine atomic lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22256)
*Nathan Chapelier-Laget,Thomas Gerber,Nicolas Jacon,Cédric Lecouvey*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了仿射排列的熵概念，证明了它与A型仿射根系统中基本权和对应的原子长度一致，并建立了Granville-Ono定理的类似结果，证明了任何非负整数都可以作为仿射排列的熵实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射排列的熵概念，探索其与仿射根系统中原子长度的关系，并验证这种度量的普遍性（即任何非负整数都能被实现）。

Method: 使用Hall关于模n排列差集的加性组合定理来证明普遍性结果；对于C_n型，基于Alon的组合零点定理建立了超八面体群的Hall定理类似结果；通过计算证据支持猜想。

Result: 证明了仿射排列的熵与A型仿射根系统中基本权和的原子长度一致；建立了Granville-Ono定理的仿射类似，证明了任何非负整数都可以作为仿射排列的熵实现；对于C_n型，当2n+1为素数时证明了熵的普遍性；提出了更一般的猜想。

Conclusion: 仿射排列的熵是一个重要的组合不变量，与仿射根系统的原子长度密切相关，并且在A型和特定条件下的C_n型中具有普遍性；在任意仿射经典型中，当秩足够大且权重适当调整时，原子长度的普遍性问题会简化。

Abstract: We introduce and study the notion of entropy of affine permutations and prove that it coincides with the atomic length associated with the sum of the fundamental weights for a type $A$ affine root system, as defined by the first two authors. We then establish an analogue of the Granville-Ono theorem by showing that any nonnegative integer can be realised as the entropy of an affine permutation or alternatively, as the size of a core multipartition as introduced by the last two authors. Our proof uses an additive combinatorics theorem due to Hall on difference sets of permutations modulo $n$. More generally, we give a polynomial expression of the atomic length associated with any dominant weight in affine type $A$ and investigate the problem of its universality. Beyond type $A$, we are able to prove that the entropy of affine type $C_n$ permutations is universal when $2n+1$ is prime. This is achieved by establishing an analogue of Hall's theorem for the hyperoctahedral group based on Alon's combinatorial Nullstellensatz. We also propose conjectures generalising the results presented in the paper, each supported by computational evidence. Finally, we show that in any affine classical type, the problem of the universality of the atomic length simplifies in large rank when the weight considered is conveniently adjusted.

</details>


### [150] [On corank 4 unitary representations of classical groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22255)
*Baiying Liu,Chi-Heng Lo,Brian Wen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文明确分类了特征零非阿基米德局部域上辛群或分裂奇特殊正交群的秩4酉表示，通过分类秩4的Arthur表示并验证最近提出的酉对偶猜想


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决辛群和分裂奇特殊正交群在非阿基米德局部域上的秩4酉表示的分类问题，验证HJLLZ24中提出的酉对偶猜想

Method: 方法包括：1）分类秩4的Arthur表示；2）验证HJLLZ24中提出的酉对偶猜想；3）通过Arthur参数和局部Langlands对应来明确分类酉表示

Result: 获得了辛群和分裂奇特殊正交群在特征零非阿基米德局部域上的秩4酉表示的完整分类

Conclusion: 成功完成了秩4酉表示的分类，验证了HJLLZ24中的酉对偶猜想，为相关群表示论研究提供了重要结果

Abstract: In this paper, we explicitly classify the corank 4 unitary representations of symplectic or split odd special orthogonal groups over non-Archimedean local fields of characteristic zero, by classifying Arthur representations of corank 4 and verifying the corresponding unitary dual conjecture recently proposed by Hazeltine-Jiang-Liu-Lo-Zhang in [HJLLZ24].

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Relating Mahler measures and Dirichlet $L$-values: new evidence for Chinburg's conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20820)
*David Hokken,Mahya Mehrabdollahei,Berend Ringeling*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文验证了Chinburg猜想的新实例，将强猜想证明的数值例子从18个增加到26个，弱猜想实例从原有基础上增加了18个，并证明了允许分圆系数时的弱猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chinburg在1984年提出了关于奇二次Dirichlet特征值L函数导数与多项式Mahler测度之间关系的猜想，该猜想在数论和代数几何中具有重要意义。先前仅对18个f值证明了强猜想形式，需要扩展验证范围并深化理论理解。

Method: 采用基于Boyd和Rodriguez-Villegas工作的显式方法，通过构造具有特定性质的积分二元有理函数（强猜想要求积分多项式），计算其Mahler测度并验证与L'(χ_{-f},-1)的有理倍数关系。对于弱猜想证明，允许使用分圆系数。

Result: 获得了8个新的强猜想实例和18个新的弱猜想实例，使强猜想证明的总数达到26个。证明了当允许多项式系数为分圆数时，Chinburg弱猜想成立。

Conclusion: 研究显著扩展了Chinburg猜想的验证范围，显式方法能捕获几乎所有已知结果，并建立了允许分圆系数时的弱猜想理论证明，为进一步研究该猜想提供了新工具和方向。

Abstract: Let $χ_{-f}$ be the odd quadratic Dirichlet character of conductor $f$, and let $\mathrm{m}(P)$ denote the Mahler measure of a polynomial $P$. In 1984, Chinburg conjectured that for any such $χ_{-f}$ there exist an integral bivariate rational function $P$ (and, in the strong form, an integral polynomial) such that $\mathrm{m}(P)$ is a rational multiple of $L'(χ_{-f},-1)$. The strong form of the conjecture was previously proven for $18$ values of $f$. We double the number of numerical examples, giving $8$ new instances of the strong and $18$ new instances of the weak conjecture. Our examples arise from an explicit approach, which also captures almost all of the previously known results, and is based on work of Boyd and Rodriguez-Villegas. Moreover, we prove Chinburg's weak conjecture if we allow cyclotomic coefficients.

</details>


### [152] [Upper and lower estimates for integer complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20876)
*Sergei Konyagin,Kristina Oganesyan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了整数复杂度函数‖n‖，证明了对于几乎所有整数n，存在改进的上界‖n‖≤C_avg log n + o(log n)和下界‖n‖≥3.06 log₃ n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数复杂度函数‖n‖表示用1通过加法、乘法和括号表示n所需的最少1的个数。已知3log₃ n ≤ ‖n‖ ≤ 3log₂ n，但上界估计仍有改进空间。虽然典型数（几乎所有数）满足‖n‖ ≤ C_avg log n（其中C_avg ≈ 3.295），但需要更精确的渐近行为分析。

Method: 使用概率方法和数论技术分析整数复杂度的渐近行为。通过改进对典型数的分析，获得更精确的上界估计和下界估计。

Result: 证明了对于几乎所有整数n，有‖n‖ ≤ C_avg log n + o(log n)，从而得到limsup_{n→∞} ‖n‖/log n ≤ C_avg。同时获得了第一个非平凡的下界：对于几乎所有n，有‖n‖ ≥ 3.06 log₃ n。

Conclusion: 该研究显著改进了对整数复杂度函数渐近行为的理解，为典型数提供了更精确的上下界估计，填补了该领域的重要空白。

Abstract: Let $\|n\|$ stand for the integer complexity of the number $n$, i.e. for the least number of $1$'s needed to write $n$ using arbitrary many additions, multiplications, and parentheses. The two-sided inequality $3\log_3 n\leq\|n\|\leq 3\log_2 n$ for all $n$ is well known and reveals the logarithmic behaviour of the complexity function $\|n\|$. While the lower bound $3\log_3 n$ is attained infinitely many times at powers of $3$, the best upper estimate is still unknown, although there are some improvements of the trivial bound $3\log_2 n$. Besides, for $``$typical$"$ numbers, i.e. for almost all numbers $n$, the better inequality $\|n\|\leq C_{avg}\log n$ holds, where, importantly, $C_{avg}\approx 3.295<\sup_{n} \frac{\|n\|}{\log n}$.
  We show that in fact $\|n\|\leq C_{avg}\log n+o(\log n)$ as $n\to\infty$, which, in particular, yields that $\limsup\limits_{n\to\infty}\frac{\|n\|}{\log n}\leq C_{avg}$. We also obtain the first nontrivial lower bound $\|n\|\geq 3.06\log_3 n$ for almost all numbers $n$.

</details>


### [153] [A refined non-vanishing of the $p$-adic logarithm of a rational point on an abelian variety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20886)
*Ashay Burungale,Christopher Skinner,Xin Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文基于BDP公式，研究GL2型阿贝尔簇上p-adic对数中非挠点非零性问题，主要关注与希尔伯特模新形式和Heegner点相关的阿贝尔簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到Bertolini、Darmon和Prasanna提出的BDP公式启发，研究阿贝尔簇p-adic对数中非挠点的非零性问题，特别是在GL2型阿贝尔簇与希尔伯特模新形式相关的背景下。

Method: 主要采用p-adic解析子群定理作为核心工具，应用于与希尔伯特模新形式和Heegner点相关的GL2型阿贝尔簇。

Result: 论文未在摘要中明确说明具体结果，但表明通过p-adic解析子群定理解决了GL2型阿贝尔簇p-adic对数中非挠点非零性的相关问题。

Conclusion: p-adic解析子群定理是研究GL2型阿贝尔簇p-adic对数中非挠点非零性问题的有效工具，特别是在与希尔伯特模新形式和Heegner点相关的背景下。

Abstract: Inspired by a beautiful formula of Bertolini, Darmon, and Prasanna -- the oft-termed BDP formula -- we address questions about the non-vanishing of non-torsion points under $p$-adic logarithms of abelian varieties. We largely consider situations most applicable to ${\mathrm GL}_2$-type abelian varieties associated with Hilbert modular newforms and Heegner points. Not surprisingly, the main tool employed is the $p$-adic analytic subgroup theorem.

</details>


### [154] [Graham conjecture on small sets in abelian groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20961)
*Simone Costa,Stefano Della Fiore,Mattia Fontana,Lluís Vena*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究Graham猜想在一般阿贝尔群中的序列化问题，将已知结果从|A|≤9提升到|A|≤20，对零和子集提升到|A|≤22，并对CMPP猜想证明了无逆对零和子集在|A|≤23时可序列化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graham猜想断言任意非零子集A⊆ℤ_p\{0}可排序使得所有部分和互不相同。虽然Pham和Sauermann已证明该猜想对大素数成立，但在一般阿贝尔群（包括循环群ℤ_k）中仍为开放问题。本文旨在研究小规模子集在一般阿贝尔群中的序列化性质。

Method: 采用递归方法研究一般阿贝尔群中子集的序列化问题。通过系统分析小规模子集（|A|≤20）的排列可能性，证明这些子集均可找到满足条件的排序。对零和子集和无逆对零和子集分别采用特定技术改进边界。

Result: 1. 证明任意子集A⊆G\{0}在|A|≤20时可序列化（此前仅知|A|≤9）；2. 对零和子集改进到|A|≤22；3. 对CMPP猜想相关情况，证明无逆对零和子集在|A|≤23时可序列化。

Conclusion: 通过递归方法显著推进了小规模子集在一般阿贝尔群中的序列化边界，为完全解决Graham猜想在一般群中的推广提供了重要进展。结果展示了递归方法在研究此类组合数论问题中的有效性。

Abstract: A famous conjecture of Graham asserts that every set $A \subseteq \mathbb{Z}_p \setminus \{0\}$ can be ordered so that all partial sums are distinct. Although this conjecture was recently proved for sufficiently large primes by Pham and Sauermann in~\cite{PM} (combined with earlier results of \cite{BBKMM}), it remains open for general abelian groups, even in the cyclic case $\mathbb{Z}_k$.
  In this paper, using a recursive approach, we investigate the sequenceability of subsets $A$ in generic abelian groups for small values of $|A|$. We prove that any subset $A \subseteq G\setminus\{0\}$ with $|A| \leq 20$ is sequenceable where previously it was known only for $|A|\leq 9$. This bound is improved to $|A| \leq 22$ for zero-sum subsets. Finally, regarding the related CMPP conjecture, we show that zero-sum subsets without inverse pairs are sequenceable for $|A| \leq 23$.

</details>


### [155] [Ties in function field prime race](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21005)
*Graeme Bates,Ryan Jesubalan,Seewoo Lee,Jane Lu,Hyewon Shim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了函数域中Chebyshev偏差的平局现象，即两个同余类a,b在无限多个N处素数计数函数π(a,m,N)=π(b,m,N)的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域中Chebyshev偏差的平局现象，探索在哪些情况下两个同余类的素数分布会出现无限多次相等的情况。

Method: 提供了两种不同的证明方法：1) 使用L函数的显式公式和矩阵版本的Möbius反演公式，通过分圆域中的例外Galois共轭元素对产生平局；2) 通过GL₂(𝔽_q)作用构造显式双射。

Result: 提供了无限多个(m,a,b)的例子，其中平局现象在满足某些特定同余条件的N处成立，这些例子包括特征2的情况。

Conclusion: 论文成功构造了函数域中Chebyshev偏差平局现象的无限多实例，并通过两种不同方法验证了这些平局的存在性，扩展了特征2情况下的研究。

Abstract: Function field analogue of the Chebyshev's bias was first studied by Cha. In this paper, we study ties in race, i.e. pairs of congruence classes $a, b \in (\mathbb{F}_q[T] / m)^\times$ where $π(a, m, N) = π(b, m, N)$ holds for infinitely many $N$. We provide infinitely many examples of $(m, a, b)$ where the tie holds for $N$ satisfying some certain congruence conditions. We give two different proofs, by 1) using the explicit formula via $L$-functions and matrix analogue of Möbius inversion formula, where exceptional Galois conjugate pairs of elements in the corresponding cyclotomic fields give ties, and 2) constructing an explicit bijection via $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{F}_q)$-action. Our examples also include characteristic 2 cases.

</details>


### [156] [Analytic Study of $p$-Bessel Functions: Fractional Calculus, Integral Representations, and Complex Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21072)
*Masaya Kitajima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了p-Bessel函数，这是一种源于p-圆域傅里叶分析的新型广义贝塞尔函数，建立了其解析性质、层次结构、积分表示和复延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然先前工作建立了p-圆域上的Hardy型振荡恒等式，但缺乏对这些p-Bessel函数本身内在解析性质的系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为各向异性振荡现象研究提供严格的解析框架。

Method: 使用Erdélyi-Kober型分数阶导数构造层次结构；推导适用于轴相关渐近估计的显式实解析积分表示；通过Poisson型积分公式将函数延拓到复域。

Result: 建立了p-Bessel函数的系统解析理论，包括层次结构构造、积分表示和复延拓，证明这些函数是真正新的振荡核，为p-圆格点问题应用奠定解析基础。

Conclusion: p-Bessel函数构成了一类新的振荡核函数，其系统解析性质的建立为研究各向异性振荡现象和p-圆格点问题提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: We present a systematic analytic study of the $p$-Bessel functions $\mathcal{J}_{ω,\varphi}^{[p]}$, a novel class of generalized Bessel functions arising from Fourier analysis on planar domains bounded by $p$-circles, including astroid-type shapes with $0<p\le2$ satisfying $(2/p)\in\mathbb{N}$. While previous work established Hardy-type oscillatory identities for these domains, expressing lattice point discrepancies via $p$-Bessel functions, the present paper focuses on the intrinsic analytic properties of the functions themselves. In particular, we (i) construct a hierarchical structure of $\{\mathcal{J}_{ω,\varphi}^{[p]}\}_{ω\ge0}$ using Erdélyi-Kober-type fractional derivatives, (ii) derive explicit real-analytic integral representations suitable for axis-dependent asymptotic estimates, and (iii) extend the functions to the complex domain through Poisson-type integral formulas. These results establish $p$-Bessel functions as genuinely new oscillatory kernels, providing a rigorous framework for studying anisotropic oscillatory phenomena and laying the analytic foundation for applications in $p$-circle lattice point problems.

</details>


### [157] [An analytic approach to $P$-adic diffeomorphism group and Teichmüller theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21074)
*Yuxiu Lu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究无限维p-adic李群，特别是p-adic球上的解析微分同胚群，建立了p-adic版本的Teichmüller理论，并将其应用于Mochizuki的p-adic Teichmüller理论和IUT理论，给出了IUT的新表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限维p-adic李群的结构，特别是解析微分同胚群的自然对数结构，探索p-adic版本的Teichmüller理论及其与流体动力学方面的联系，并将此框架应用于Mochizuki的p-adic Teichmüller理论和IUT理论。

Method: 考虑特定类的无限维p-adic李群，即p-adic球B_ε上的解析微分同胚群Diff^{an}(B_ε)。该群具有自然对数结构，这导致了微分同胚群上的p-adic版本Teichmüller理论。将此框架应用于Mochizuki的p-adic Teichmüller理论和IUT理论。

Result: 建立了p-adic球上解析微分同胚群的p-adic Teichmüller理论，该理论展现出显著的流体动力学特征。将Mochizuki的IUT理论重新表述为二维群概形自同构上的Teichmüller理论。

Conclusion: 无限维p-adic李群，特别是解析微分同胚群，具有自然的对数结构，这为p-adic Teichmüller理论提供了新框架。该框架不仅揭示了与流体动力学的联系，还为IUT理论提供了新的表述，将其理解为二维群概形自同构上的Teichmüller理论。

Abstract: We consider a specific class of infinite dimensional $p$-adic Lie groups, i.e., a sort of diffeomorphism groups on $p$-adic ball $\operatorname{Diff}^{\operatorname{an}}(B_ε)$. It turns out that this group has a natural logarithmic structure that leads to a $p$-adic version of Teichmüller theory on diffeomorphism groups, which also presents some remarkable hydrodynamic facets. We further apply this framework to Mochizuki's $p$-adic Teichmüller theory and Inter-universal Teichmüller theory (IUT), and give a new reformulation of IUT as a Teichmüller theory on automorphisms of two-dimensional group schemes.

</details>


### [158] [Integral Representations for Multiple Apéry-Like Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21079)
*Jorge Antonio González Layja*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文通过初等方法获得了多个Apéry-like级数族的积分表示，这些表示包含多重调和和与中心二项式因子，基于分部积分和关键傅里叶展开的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Apéry-like级数的积分表示，这类级数在数论和特殊函数理论中具有重要意义，但通常难以直接计算。通过获得积分表示，可以更系统地研究这些级数的性质并推导新的恒等式。

Method: 使用初等方法，基于分部积分的系统应用和两个关键的傅里叶展开。通过这种方法将多重Apéry-like级数转化为包含多重调和和的积分表示。

Result: 获得了多个Apéry-like级数族的积分表示，这些表示包含多重调和和与中心二项式因子。应用这些结果恢复了已知的求值，并推导出了新的恒等式。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究Apéry-like级数提供了有效的工具，通过积分表示可以更系统地处理这类级数，不仅能够验证已知结果，还能发现新的数学恒等式。

Abstract: By elementary methods, we obtain integral representations for several families of multiple Apéry-like series. These involve multiple harmonic sums in addition to the characteristic central binomial factors. The derivations are based on systematic applications of integration by parts together with two key Fourier expansions. As applications, we recover known evaluations and derive new identities.

</details>


### [159] [Effective count of integer points on ternary affine quadrics and effective equidistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21080)
*Runlin Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文通过混合方法研究无限齐次测度的有效等分布及相关计数问题，获得了Oh-Shah和Kelmer-Kontorovich计数定理的光滑版本，并在附录中应用Hilbert-Asai Eisenstein级数的亚纯延拓获得精确计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限齐次测度的有效等分布问题，为相关计数定理提供光滑版本，改进Oh-Shah和Kelmer-Kontorovich在数域上的计数结果。

Method: 采用混合方法研究无限齐次测度的有效等分布，通过Hilbert-Asai Eisenstein级数的亚纯延拓获得精确计数结果。

Result: 获得了Oh-Shah和Kelmer-Kontorovich计数定理的光滑版本，通过Eisenstein级数方法实现了精确计数。

Conclusion: 混合方法为无限齐次测度的等分布研究提供了有效工具，Hilbert-Asai Eisenstein级数的应用使得精确计数成为可能，扩展了数域上计数定理的应用范围。

Abstract: We study the effective equidistribution of certain infinite homogeneous measures and related counting problems through mixing. In this way, we obtain smooth versions of counting theorems studied by Oh-Shah and later by Kelmer-Kontorovich over a number field. In the appendix, we apply the meromorphic continuation of Hilbert-Asai Eisenstein series to obtain the authentic counting.

</details>


### [160] [Large values of logarithmic derivatives of quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21256)
*Zikang Dong,Haidong Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文应用共振方法改进对数导数的条件Ω结果，推广了先前研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Mortada和Murty关于二次Dirichlet L-函数对数导数的条件Ω结果，并推广Yang的相关研究成果

Method: 应用共振方法(resonance method)推导二次Dirichlet L-函数对数导数的条件Ω结果

Result: 改进了Mortada和Murty的结果，并推广了Yang关于对数导数Ω定理的结论

Conclusion: 通过共振方法成功获得了二次Dirichlet L-函数对数导数更优的条件Ω结果，扩展了该领域的研究

Abstract: In this article, we apply the resonance method to derive conditional Omega results for logarithmic derivatives of quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions. We improve a previous result of Mortada and Murty \cite{MM13}, as well as generalize some results of Yang \cite{yang2023omegatheoremslogarithmicderivatives}.

</details>


### [161] [Explicit isomorphisms for a Herr-type complex over a metabelian extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21681)
*Anand Chitrao,Aditya Karnataki,Jishnu Ray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在p-adic Banach代数S上构造Galois表示族V的复形，在false-Tate扩张上建立其同调与Galois上同调的显式同构，推广了有限扩张情形的已有结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-adic数域的绝对Galois群在S模上的连续作用，其中S是剩余域为有限扩张的Qp-Banach代数。旨在将有限扩张情形下的Galois上同调结果推广到更一般的Banach代数框架

Method: 构造与Galois表示族V相关的复形，特别针对false-Tate扩张。建立该复形同调与Galois上同调之间的显式同构，使用p-adic分析和Galois上同调技术

Result: 成功构造了复形并证明了其同调与Galois上同调的显式同构，将Tavares Ribeiro在S为有限扩张情形下的结果推广到更一般的Banach代数情形

Conclusion: 该方法为研究p-adic Galois表示族在一般Banach代数上的上同调性质提供了有效工具，扩展了现有理论框架的应用范围

Abstract: Let $S$ be a Banach algebra over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ whose residue fields are finite extensions of $\mathbb{Q}_p$. Given an arithmetic family $V$ of Galois representations, i.e., a finite free $S$-module $V$ with a continuous action of the absolute Galois group of a $p$-adic number field, we construct a complex associated to $V$ over false-Tate extensions and construct explicit isomorphisms between its cohomology and the Galois cohomology. This recovers earlier results by Tavares Ribeiro when $S$ is a finite extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$.

</details>


### [162] [On mixed $b$-concatenations of Fibonacci and Lucas numbers that are Lucas numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21259)
*Herbert Batte,Prosper Kaggwa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究Lucas数能否表示为以b为基的Fibonacci数和Lucas数的混合连接形式，即解决两个Diophantine方程，证明只有有限个这样的Lucas数存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Fibonacci数和Lucas数在数论中的特殊关系，研究它们通过基b表示时的混合连接性质，这属于组合数论和Diophantine方程的研究范畴。

Method: 使用对数线性形式的Diophantine逼近理论和基于连分数的约化方法，处理两个关键方程：L_n = b^d L_m + F_k 和 L_n = b^d F_m + L_k，其中d是F_k或L_k在基b下的位数。

Result: 证明了只有有限个Lucas数可以表示为基b混合连接的Fibonacci数和Lucas数，具体给出了这些方程的解集是有限的。

Conclusion: 通过Diophantine逼近和连分数方法，完全解决了Lucas数作为Fibonacci数和Lucas数基b混合连接表示的问题，确定了此类表示的数量有限性。

Abstract: Let $(F_n)_{n\ge0}$ and $(L_n)_{n\ge0}$ denote the sequences of Fibonacci and Lucas numbers respectively. This paper determines all Lucas numbers that can be represented as base $b$ mixed concatenations of a Fibonacci number and a Lucas number. Mathematically, we study of two Diophantine equations $L_n=b^dL_m+F_k$ and $L_n=b^dF_m+L_k$, where $d$ is the number of digits of $F_k$ or $L_k$ in base $b$. To tackle these equations, we combine tools from Diophantine approximation on non-zero linear forms in logarithms and reduction methods based on continued fractions. This allows us to prove that only finitely many such Lucas numbers exist.

</details>


### [163] [Eisenstein-series evaluations for a family of hyperbolic cosine Lambert series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21265)
*Nikita Kalinin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究一类双曲Lambert级数，证明特定值下的精确结果，并给出二次双曲级数的求值，基于模形式理论


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类具有双曲余弦分母的特殊Lambert级数，探索其精确求值规律，揭示其与模形式理论的深刻联系

Method: 将双曲核重写为Lambert级数，将求和转化为Eisenstein级数在CM点i/2、i、2i处的导数，利用E2、E4、E6的显式恒等式及2i处的theta常数关系，通过经典Eisenstein级数Gk中高斯格点的奇偶分解获得一般消失结果

Result: 证明S0=1/12，S1=1/(2π²)，当m>1时Sm=0；二次双曲级数求值为(77-234/π)/72

Conclusion: 为双曲余弦恒等式族提供了统一的模解释，揭示了这类特殊级数与模形式理论的深刻联系，展示了模形式方法在特殊函数求值中的强大应用

Abstract: We study a family of hyperbolic Lambert series of the form \[ S_m=\sum_{n=1}^\infty\left( \frac{n^{2m}}{\cosh(πn)-1} -\frac{(2^{2m+1}-(-1)^{m(m+1)/2}2^{m+1}+4) n^{2m}}{\cosh(2πn)-1} +\frac{2^{2m+2}n^{2m}}{\cosh(4πn)-1} \right). \] We prove that \[ S_0=\frac1{12},\qquad S_1=\frac1{2π^2},\qquad S_m=0 \quad (m>1). \] We also evaluate the quadratic hyperbolic series \[ \sum_{n=1}^\infty \left( \frac{4}{(\cosh(πn)-1)^2} -\frac{55}{(\cosh(2πn)-1)^2} +\frac{16}{(\cosh(4πn)-1)^2} \right) = \frac{77-234/π}{72}. \] The proof is based on rewriting the hyperbolic kernels as Lambert series and identifying the resulting sums with derivatives of Eisenstein series at the CM points $i/2$, $i$, and $2i$. The initial evaluations are reduced to explicit identities for $E_2$, $E_4$, and $E_6$, together with a theta-constant relation at $2i$, while the general vanishing result is obtained from a parity decomposition of the Gaussian lattice in the classical Eisenstein series $G_k$. This gives a uniform modular explanation for a family of hyperbolic cosine identities.

</details>


### [164] [The de Rham and the syntomic logarithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21471)
*Matthias Flach,Achim Krause,Baptiste Morin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文定义了de Rham对数映射作为Bloch-Kato指数映射逆的积分细化，利用基于过滤棱镜上同调的syntomic对数技术，证明了Beilinson纤维平方定理，解决了Fontaine-Perrin-Riou猜想，并计算了Bloch-Kato Tamagawa数猜想中的修正因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Bloch-Kato指数映射逆的积分细化，发展基于过滤棱镜上同调的新工具，解决算术几何中的几个重要猜想，包括Beilinson纤维平方、Fontaine-Perrin-Riou猜想和Bloch-Kato Tamagawa数猜想中的修正因子计算。

Method: 定义de Rham对数映射作为Bloch-Kato指数映射逆的积分细化；引入基于Antieau-Krause-Nikolaus过滤棱镜上同调理论的syntomic对数构造；利用syntomic对数技术证明Beilinson纤维平方定理的推广形式；应用这些技术解决Fontaine-Perrin-Riou猜想和计算Flach-Morin修正因子。

Result: 成功定义了de Rham对数映射；证明了所有拟紧拟分离导出形式概形的Beilinson纤维平方定理；证明了所有局部域K/Q_p的Fontaine-Perrin-Riou猜想C_EP(Q_p(n))；计算了光滑射影概形X在数环上Zeta函数的Bloch-Kato Tamagawa数猜想中的修正因子C(X,n)。

Conclusion: de Rham对数映射和syntomic对数技术为算术几何提供了强大的新工具，成功解决了多个重要猜想，建立了棱镜上同调理论与经典算术几何问题之间的深刻联系，为Tamagawa数猜想等核心问题的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We define and study an integral refinement of the inverse of the Bloch-Kato exponential map which we call the de Rham logarithm. Our main tool to analyze the de Rham logarithm is the syntomic logarithm, a certain limit construction based on the theory of filtered prismatic cohomology initiated by Antieau, Krause and Nikolaus. We use the syntomic logarithm to prove a version of the Beilinson fibre square for all quasicompact, quasiseparated derived formal schemes. We also use our techniques to prove Conjecture $C_{EP}(\bq_p(n))$ of Fontaine and Perrin-Riou for all local fields $K/\bq_p$ and to compute the correction factor $C(X,n)$ introduced by Flach and Morin in their reformulation of the Bloch-Kato Tamagawa number conjecture for the Zeta function of a smooth projective scheme $X$ over a number ring.

</details>


### [165] [The Littlewood-Richardson rule for Schur $P$-, $Q$-multiple zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21480)
*Hikari Hanaki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Schur P-和Q-多重zeta函数的展开公式，通过对称群求和获得了Schur P-多重zeta函数的线性展开，并引入了斜Schur Q-多重zeta函数的展开公式及其细化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nakasuji和Takeda受Schur P-和Q-函数的表格表示启发定义了Schur P-和Q-多重zeta函数。由于两个Schur P-函数的乘积可以展开为Schur P-函数的线性组合，作者希望为Schur P-多重zeta函数建立类似的展开公式。

Method: 通过在所有变量上进行对称群求和，获得了Schur P-多重zeta函数的展开公式。同时引入了斜Schur Q-多重zeta函数的展开公式，通过将对称群限制到特定子群来细化该公式。

Result: 成功推导出Schur P-多重zeta函数的展开公式，类似于Schur P-函数的乘积展开。建立了斜Schur Q-多重zeta函数的展开公式，并展示了通过限制对称群到特定子群可以进一步细化该公式。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了Schur P-和Q-多重zeta函数的理论，建立了与经典Schur函数类似的展开公式，并通过对称群及其子群的限制提供了更精细的结构分析。

Abstract: The Schur $P$-, $Q$-multiple zeta functions were defined by Nakasuji and Takeda inspired by the tableau representation of Schur $P$-, $Q$-functions. While a product of two Schur $P$-functions expands as a linear combination of Schur $P$-functions, we obtain a similar expansion formula for the Schur $P$-multiple zeta functions by taking summation over the symmetric group permutating all the variables. We also introduce a expansion formula of skew Schur $Q$-multiple zeta functions by taking summation over the symmetric group. Furthermore, this skew type formula can be refined by restricting the symmetric group to its specific subgroup.

</details>


### [166] [Zero-free regions inspired by work of Heath-Brown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21490)
*Chiara Bellotti,Tim Trudgian,Andrew Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了一个新的黎曼ζ函数显式零点自由区域，改进了现有的零点自由边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼ζ函数的零点分布是数论中的核心问题，特别是零点自由区域的研究对于理解素数分布和黎曼猜想具有重要意义。现有结果存在改进空间，需要寻找更优的零点自由边界。

Method: 基于Heath-Brown关于Linnik常数的开创性工作，采用解析数论方法，通过改进技术手段来推导新的零点自由区域。

Result: 证明了当$t\geq 3$且$σ\geq 1- 1/(4.896\log t)$时，$ζ(σ+ it)\ne 0$，即得到了一个改进的显式零点自由区域。

Conclusion: 该研究成功扩展了黎曼ζ函数的零点自由区域，为素数分布理论和黎曼猜想相关研究提供了新的技术工具和理论进展。

Abstract: We prove a new explicit zero-free region for the Riemann zeta-function, drawing substantially on Heath-Brown's seminal work on Linnik's constant. Using these ideas we are able to prove that $ζ(σ+ it)\ne 0$ whenever $t\geq 3$ and $σ\geq 1- 1/(4.896\log t)$.

</details>


### [167] [Beyond Endoscopy for $\mathrm{GL}(3, \mathbb{Q})$: Poisson Summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21506)
*Taiwang Deng,Malors Espinos*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Altuğ为GL(2,ℚ)开发的泊松求和方法到GL(3,ℚ)，用于超越自守形式的策略。在假设猜想A的前提下，从迹公式的正则椭圆部分分离出平凡表示的贡献，并得到具体展开式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越自守形式（Beyond Endoscopy）是Langlands纲领的重要发展方向，旨在通过迹公式直接构造L-函数。Altuğ为GL(2,ℚ)开发了泊松求和方法，本文旨在将此方法推广到更高维的GL(3,ℚ)，以处理更复杂的自守表示问题。

Method: 1. 将正则椭圆部分重新表述为与特征多项式相关的三次序；2. 为这些序构造zeta函数（通过其超序定义）并证明其完备形式的函数方程；3. 应用近似函数方程将椭圆项改写为适合泊松求和的形式；4. 利用周期性定理证明相关系数仅依赖于参数的有限模，从而在三次数据索引的积分参数上执行泊松求和；5. 主要项由Kloosterman型和相关的二重Dirichlet级数控制，并显式计算。

Result: 1. 成功将泊松求和方法推广到GL(3,ℚ)；2. 在假设猜想A的前提下，从迹公式的正则椭圆部分分离出平凡表示的贡献，得到具体展开式I_ell(f)-Tr(1(f))；3. 通过留数定理找到平凡表示的迹作为留数；4. 恢复了"特殊"表示的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Altuğ的泊松求和方法从GL(2,ℚ)推广到GL(3,ℚ)，为超越自守形式策略在更高维群上的应用提供了关键工具。方法的核心是将椭圆迹公式项与三次序的算术结构联系起来，通过zeta函数、函数方程和周期性定理实现泊松求和，最终分离出平凡表示和特殊表示的贡献。

Abstract: We generalize to $\mathrm{GL}(3,\mathbb{Q})$ the Poisson Summation method developed by Altuğ for $\mathrm{GL}(2, \mathbb{Q})$ for the strategy of Beyond Endoscopy. Concretely, assuming Conjecture A, we isolate the contribution of the trivial representation from the regular elliptic part of the trace formula and obtain a concrete expansion of \[ \mathrm{I}_{\mathrm{ell}}(f)-\mathrm{Tr}(\mathbf{1}(f)). \] Our starting point is a reformulation of the regular elliptic part in terms of cubic orders attached to characteristic polynomials. To these orders we associate a zeta function, defined through their overorders, prove a functional equation for its completion, and apply an approximate functional equation to rewrite the elliptic term in a form suitable for Poisson Summation. A key arithmetic input is a periodicity theorem showing that the relevant coefficients depend only on the parameters modulo a finite modulus. This makes it possible to perform Poisson Summation on the integral parameters indexed by the cubic data.
  The resulting main terms are governed by Kloosterman-type sums and an associated double Dirichlet series, which we evaluate explicitly. From this evaluation we are able to apply the residue theorem and find the trace of the trivial representation as a residue. We also recover the contribution of the \say{special} representation.

</details>


### [168] [On the series expansion of the prime zeta function about $s=1$ and its coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21535)
*Artur Kawalec*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文推导了素数zeta函数在s=1对数奇点处的级数展开，证明了其展开系数的一般公式，并将其视为Mertens定理的高阶推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数zeta函数在s=1处的对数奇点行为，建立类似于Riemann zeta函数Stieltjes展开系数的理论框架，将Mertens定理推广到更高阶。

Method: 推导素数zeta函数在s=1处的级数展开，证明展开系数的一般公式，并进行高精度数值验证和计算。

Result: 获得了素数zeta函数在s=1对数奇点处的级数展开公式，证明了展开系数的一般表达式，数值验证了公式的正确性和高精度。

Conclusion: 成功建立了素数zeta函数在s=1处的系统展开理论，将Mertens定理推广到高阶，为素数分布研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: In this article, we derive a series expansion of the prime zeta function about the $s=1$ logarithmic singularity and prove general formula for its expansion coefficients, which is similar to the Stieltjes expansion coefficients for the Riemann zeta function. These results can also be viewed as a generalization of Mertens's Theorems to higher order. We also numerically verify and compute the presented formulas to high precision for several test cases.

</details>


### [169] [On the series expansion of the secondary zeta function about $s=1$ and its coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21555)
*Artur Kawalec*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了黎曼假设下定义的二次zeta函数，推导了其在s=1处正则部分展开系数的类似Stieltjes常数的公式，并进行了数值验证和高精度计算，同时应用Brent的BPT定理改进收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次zeta函数（基于黎曼假设下非平凡零点虚部定义的广义zeta级数）在s=1双极点处的Laurent展开，特别是正则部分的展开系数，旨在建立类似黎曼zeta函数Stieltjes常数的公式表示。

Method: 推导二次zeta函数正则部分展开系数的新公式，该公式类似于黎曼zeta函数的Stieltjes常数公式。进行数值验证和高精度计算多个测试案例。应用Brent的BPT定理改进主要公式的收敛性。

Result: 成功推导出二次zeta函数在s=1处正则部分展开系数的新公式，该公式与Stieltjes常数公式类似。数值验证表明公式有效，能够进行高精度计算。应用BPT定理显著改善了主要公式的收敛性。

Conclusion: 建立了二次zeta函数正则部分展开系数的解析公式，该公式与黎曼zeta函数的Stieltjes常数公式具有相似性。数值计算验证了公式的正确性，BPT定理的应用有效提升了收敛性能，为二次zeta函数的进一步研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The secondary zeta function is defined as a generalized zeta series over the imaginary parts of non-trivial zeros assuming (RH). This function admits Laurent series expansion at the double pole at $s=1$. In this article, we derive a new formula for the expansion coefficients of the regular part, which is similar to the Stieltjes constants formula for the Riemann zeta function. We also numerically verify and compute the new formula to high precision for several test cases. Lastly, we also apply the Brent's (BPT) Theorem for improving convergence of the main formula.

</details>


### [170] [The second moment of derivatives of quadratic twists of modular $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21739)
*Yujiao Jiang,Quanli Shen,Ziyang Tang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了二次扭模L函数一阶导数的二阶矩渐近公式，得到了三个主项，优于先前只有一个主项的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Kumar等人的工作只得到了二次扭模L函数一阶导数二阶矩的第一个主项，本文旨在改进这一结果，获得更精确的渐近公式。

Method: 证明基于Li建立的大筛法型不等式，关键技巧是将问题转化为计算具有大位移的完整扭模L函数的渐近公式。

Result: 成功证明了二次扭模L函数一阶导数二阶矩的渐近公式，包含三个主项，改进了先前只有一个主项的结果。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了二次扭模L函数一阶导数二阶矩的渐近估计，为相关数论问题提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic formula for the second moment of the first derivative of quadratic twists of modular $L$-functions with three leading order main terms. It improves the previous result of Kumar et al. with the first main term. The proof is based on the large sieve type inequality established by Li, with a key input that we convert the problem into computing an asymptotic formula for the completed twisted modular $L$-functions with large shifts.

</details>


### [171] [$p$-Ordinary Part of Hyperbolic Cycles on Modular Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21945)
*Hohto Bekki,Ryotaro Sakamoto*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了SL₂(ℤ)同余子群在局部系数下一维同调群的p-寻常部分由双曲循环生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SL₂(ℤ)同余子群在局部系数下一维同调群中的双曲循环结构，探索这些循环在生成同调群中的作用

Method: 使用代数数论和群上同调方法，分析同余子群在局部系数下的同调结构，特别关注双曲循环的性质

Result: 对于任意素数p，证明了SL₂(ℤ)同余子群在局部系数下一维同调群的p-寻常部分完全由双曲循环生成

Conclusion: 双曲循环在同余子群局部系数同调群的p-寻常部分中起到基本生成元的作用，揭示了这类群上同调的结构特征

Abstract: In this paper, we study hyperbolic cycles in the first homology group with local coefficients of congruence subgroups of $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. We prove that, for any prime number $p$, the $p$-ordinary part of the first homology group is generated by hyperbolic cycles.

</details>


### [172] [Alternating geometric progressions modulo one and Sturmian words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22045)
*Qing Lu,Weizhe Zheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 使用Sturmian词描述所有无理实数ξ，使得序列(ξ(-b)^n)在R/Z中的像包含在长度为b^{-1}+b^{-2}-b^{-3}的区间内


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究序列(ξ(-b)^n)在实数模整数环R/Z中的分布特性，特别是确定该序列像集能被包含的最小区间长度

Method: 利用Sturmian词理论来描述满足条件的无理实数ξ，Sturmian词是描述无理数连分数展开的符号序列

Result: 完全描述了所有使得序列(ξ(-b)^n)在R/Z中的像包含在长度为b^{-1}+b^{-2}-b^{-3}的区间内的无理实数ξ

Conclusion: 该结果是最优的，因为作者在先前工作中已证明序列像集不能被包含在更短的区间内，从而确定了最小可能的区间长度

Abstract: Let $b\ge 2$ be an integer. Using Sturmian words we describe all irrational real numbers $ξ$ such that the image in $\mathbb{R}/\mathbb{Z}$ of the sequence $(ξ(-b)^n)_{n\ge 0}$ is contained in an interval of length $b^{-1}+b^{-2}-b^{-3}$. In previous work (arXiv:2603.16794) we showed that the image cannot be contained in a shorter interval.

</details>


### [173] [Non-Vanishing of Dirichlet $L$-functions at the central point with restricted root number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22124)
*Adam Earnst*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明具有素数模q的偶本原特征，当q→∞时，中心值L(1/2,χ)非零的比例为正


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirichlet L-函数中心值的非零性问题，特别是针对具有素数模的偶本原特征，旨在证明当模趋于无穷时，非零中心值具有正比例

Method: 通过计算具有角度限制（基于根数）的Dirichlet L-函数子族的平滑化一阶和二阶矩，利用这些矩估计来推导中心值的非零比例

Result: 证明了对于具有素数模q的偶本原特征，当q→∞时，中心值L(1/2,χ)非零的比例为正

Conclusion: 该研究为Dirichlet L-函数中心值的非零性提供了新的渐近结果，表明在素数模的偶本原特征中，非零中心值占有显著比例

Abstract: We prove asymptotics for mollified first and second moments of subfamilies of Dirichlet $L$-functions given by shrinking angular restrictions on the root number. Using these moments, we prove that for even primitive characters with prime conductor $q$, a positive proportion of the central values $L(1/2,χ)$ do not vanish as $q\to\infty$.

</details>


### [174] [A note on polynomial equidistribution and recurrence in finite characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22143)
*Ethan Ackelsberg,Vitaly Bergelson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 修正Bergelson和Leibman论文中的两个小错误，并在函数域上证明关于多项式序列等分布和递归的新结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域上的数论和加性组合论正在发展中，需要修正相关文献中的错误，并推进对有限特征域上多项式序列等分布和递归性质的理解

Method: 识别并修正Bergelson和Leibman论文中的两个错误，通过代数、组合和动力系统方法证明有限特征域上相交多项式的新特征化结果

Result: 成功修正了原论文中的错误，并获得了有限特征域上相交多项式的多种等价特征化，包括代数性质、组合性质和动力系统性质

Conclusion: 该研究不仅纠正了文献中的错误，还推进了函数域上多项式序列等分布理论的发展，为有限特征域上的数论和加性组合论提供了新的工具和结果

Abstract: This paper addresses the topic of equidistribution and recurrence for polynomial sequences over function fields. The main focus is to note and correct two small errors in [V. Bergelson and A. Leibman, A Weyl-type equidistribution theorem in finite characteristic, Adv. Math. 289 (2016) 928-950], contextualized within the broader developing literature on number theory and additive combinatorics in function fields. Connected with the resolution of these issues, we also prove new results characterizing intersective polynomials in finite characteristic in terms of various algebraic, combinatorial, and dynamical properties.

</details>
