### [175] [Broad presence of ferromagnetism in bees and relationship to phylogeny, natural history, and sociality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20312)
*Laura Russo,Caleb Allen,Cameron S. Jorgensen,Lizabeth Quigley,C. Charlotte Buchanan,Michael Winklhofer,Seán G. Brady,Laurence Packer,Anne Murray,Dustin A. Gilbert*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 研究发现蜜蜂和其他昆虫中基于铁磁性颗粒的磁感受能力广泛存在，无系统发育信号，且与体型大小和社会行为相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知许多动物具有磁感受能力，但对其系统发育模式、与物种特征和自然历史的关系了解甚少，特别是在蜜蜂等真社会性昆虫中

Method: 测试96种蜜蜂物种，检测基于铁磁性颗粒的磁感受能力，分析其系统发育信号，并考察与体型大小、社会行为等特征的关系

Result: 72/96种蜜蜂具有磁感受能力，无系统发育信号；非蜜蜂外群中也检测到该能力，表明其早于Anthophila进化；磁信号强度随体型增大和社会行为增强

Conclusion: 基于铁磁性颗粒的磁感受在蜜蜂中广泛存在且无系统发育限制，该能力可能为祖先特征，其强度与体型和社会性相关

Abstract: Scientists have long been fascinated by magnetoreception, the innate capacity of many animals to sense and use the Earth's magnetic field for navigation. In eusocial insects like honey bees, magnetoreception has been linked to communication and foraging. However, little is known about magnetoreception's phylogenetic patterns and relationship to species traits and natural history. Here, we demonstrate that putative magnetoreception based on ferromagnetic particles is widespread across a diversity of bee species (72 out of 96 species tested), with no phylogenetic signal. We also detected such putative magnetoreception in non-bee outgroups, suggesting this magnetic capacity predates the evolution of the Anthophila. While magnetic signals were found across a diversity of life history traits, the strength of the magnetic signal varied within and between species, and increased with body size and social behavior.

</details>


### [176] [Coexistence coalitions in propagule disperser quasi-communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20707)
*Leonardo Aguirre,José A. Capitán,David Alonso*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 提出统一随机模型框架，整合竞争-拓殖权衡、环境异质性和生态中性三种共存机制，开发基于矩阵谱的算法预测平衡群落组成，避免计算精确平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自然生态系统中物种数量远超资源数量却仍能共存的"竞争排斥悖论"，整合不同共存机制解释框架，提供统一分析工具。

Method: 开发统一随机模型类，结合竞争-拓殖权衡、环境异质性和生态中性三种共存叙事；证明宏观平衡共存定理；提出基于矩阵谱的算法确定平衡联盟。

Result: 模型框架涵盖多个经典模型；算法能仅从矩阵谱确定平衡群落组成，避免计算精确平衡态；通过示例展示量化不同共存机制相对贡献和协同效应的潜力。

Conclusion: 统一框架为研究多种共存机制的相互作用提供了有效工具，基于矩阵谱的算法简化了平衡群落预测，有助于深入理解生物多样性维持机制。

Abstract: Many natural ecosystems harbor large numbers of coexisting species competing for far fewer distinct resources, in apparent defiance of the competitive exclusion principle. Various mechanisms have been proposed to explain this apparent paradox, among the most prominent being competition--colonization trade-offs, environmental heterogeneity, and ecological neutrality. We develop a unified stochastic model class that combines all three coexistence narratives in the context of propagule disperser communities and show that this setting encompasses several important classical models. We then prove a general theorem on coexistence at macroscopic equilibria and provide an algorithm that determines equilibrium coalitions solely from readily available matrix spectra, thereby bypassing the costly computation of exact equilibrium states. Using illustrative examples, we demonstrate the potential of this approach for quantifying the relative merits of different coexistence narratives and for studying their synergistic effects.

</details>


### [177] [Spectral Geometry and Heat Kernels on Phylogenetic Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20922)
*Ángel Alfredo Morán Ledezma*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文为有限超度量系统树开发了统一的谱框架，通过超度量拉普拉斯算子将系统发育结构分析与算子理论和随机动力学联系起来，实现了谱重构、进化模式检测和性状分解等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有限超度量系统树建立统一的谱分析框架，将系统发育结构分析建立在算子理论和随机动力学基础上，旨在提供精确且具有生物学解释性的数学工具来分析进化树的结构和动态。

Method: 引入超度量拉普拉斯算子作为连续时间马尔可夫链的生成元，建立完整的谱理论，获得显式闭特征值和基于树簇的特征基。将超度量系统树与规范算子关联，证明树的几何拓扑结构完全编码在算子的谱和特征向量中。

Result: 获得了四个主要贡献：1）线性复杂度O(|X|)的谱重构定理；2）谱间隙作为不同进化模式检测器的严格几何解释；3）将生物性状方差分解为系统发育各分裂贡献的特征模分解；4）超度量空间上连续时间马尔可夫链的闭形式中心性指数。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统发育分析提供了精确且具有生物学解释性的数学工具，所有结果都是精确的并得到经验灵长类数据的数值实验支持，实现了算子理论、随机动力学与系统发育分析的统一。

Abstract: We develop a unified spectral framework for finite ultrametric phylogenetic trees, grounding the analysis of phylogenetic structure in operator theory and stochastic dynamics in the finite setting. For a given finite ultrametric measure space $(X,d,m)$, we introduce the ultrametric Laplacian $L_X$ as the generator of a continuous time Markov chain with transition rate $q(x,y)=k(d(x,y))m(y)$. We establish its complete spectral theory, obtaining explicit closed-form eigenvalues and an eigenbasis supported on the clades of the tree. For phylogenetic applications, we associate to any ultrametric phylogenetic tree $\mathcal{T}$ a canonical operator $L_{\mathcal{T}}$, the ultrametric phylogenetic Laplacian, whose jump rates encode the temporal structure of evolutionary divergence. We show that the geometry and topology of the tree are explicitly encoded in the spectrum and eigenvectors of $L_{\mathcal{T}}$: eigenvalues aggregate branch lengths weighted by clade mass along ancestral paths, while eigenvectors are supported on the clades, with one eigenspace attached to each internal node. From this we derive three main contributions: a spectral reconstruction theorem with linear complexity $O(|X|)$; a rigorous geometric interpretation of the spectral gaps of $L_{\mathcal{T}}$ as detectors of distinct evolutionary modes, validated on an empirical primate phylogeny; an eigenmode decomposition of biological traits that resolves trait variance into contributions from individual splits of the phylogeny; and a closed-form centrality index for continuous-time Markov chains on ultrametric spaces, which we propose as a mathematically grounded measure of evolutionary distinctiveness. All results are exact and biologically interpretable, and are supported by numerical experiments on empirical primate data.

</details>


### [178] [Pattern Formation in a Spatial Public Goods Dilemma due to Diffusive or Directed Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21025)
*Yuxuan Zhao,Kaisheng Zhu,Yefei Zhang,Daniel B. Cooney*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究通过偏微分方程模型分析空间结构对公共物品博弈中合作行为演化的影响，发现扩散驱动的图灵不稳定性可促进合作，而趋化式定向运动则会降低合作水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解空间结构如何影响公共物品博弈中的合作演化。公共物品提供存在社会困境：集体受益但个体有搭便车动机。空间结构（如公共物品扩散和个体定向运动）可能与种群动态相互作用，产生策略和资源的空间异质性分布模式。

Method: 基于Young和Belmonte的模型，推导出描述策略和公共物品空间分布的偏微分方程组，包含个体的扩散运动、资源的扩散运动以及个体对公共物品浓度梯度的趋化式定向运动。通过线性稳定性分析研究空间模式的形成机制，结合弱非线性稳定性分析和数值模拟探索涌现的空间模式。

Result: 线性稳定性分析表明，空间模式可通过两种机制产生：1）高背叛者扩散率导致的图灵不稳定性；2）合作者对资源浓度梯度强敏感性导致的定向运动不稳定性。扩散驱动的不稳定性似乎能增加整个空间域的合作和公共物品水平，而合作者向高公共物品区域的定向运动则倾向于降低整个环境的合作水平和环境质量。

Conclusion: 空间结构对公共物品博弈中的合作演化有重要影响。扩散驱动的空间异质性可促进合作，而基于资源梯度的定向运动可能反而损害合作。这为理解空间动态如何塑造社会困境中的合作行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The costly provision of public goods serves as a model problem for the evolution of cooperative behavior, presenting a social dilemma between the collective benefits of shared resources and the individual incentive to free-ride in resource production. The spatial structure of populations can also impact cooperation over public goods, as diffusion of public goods and intentional motion of individuals towards regions with greater resources can interact with population and public goods dynamics to produce heterogeneous patterns in the spatial distribution of strategies and resources. In this paper, we build off a model introduced by Young and Belmonte for the reaction dynamics of interacting individuals and explicit public good, deriving a system of PDEs that describes the spatial profiles of strategies and the public good in the presence of both diffusive motion of individuals and resources and chemotaxis-like directed motion of individuals in response to gradients in the concentration of public goods. Through linear stability analysis, we show that spatial patterns in strategic and public goods profiles can emerge due to either Turing instability with high defector diffusivity or a directed-motion instability through strong sensitivity of cooperators towards increasing resource concentration. We further explore the emergent spatial patterns with a mix of weakly nonlinear stability analysis and numerical simulation, showing that diffusion-driven instability appears to increase cooperation and public goods across the spatial domain, while directed motion of cooperators towards regions with great public goods provision tends to decrease cooperation and environmental quality across the environment.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [On the Limits of Prediction: Forecastability Profiles and Information Decay in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20546)
*Peter Maurice Catt*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 论文提出信息论框架量化预测极限：预测精度受限于未来信息，在log损失下分解为不可约项（条件熵）和近似项（散度），定义可预测性为最大可实现的log损失减少，形成随预测时域变化的可预测性剖面，识别有意义预测可行的信息时域集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测精度受限于可用信息，但现有方法缺乏量化这种信息限制的严格框架。需要区分数据本身固有的不可预测性与模型引入的近似误差，为建模前评估各预测时域的可预测性提供理论基础。

Method: 采用信息论工具，在对数损失下将预测性能分解为不可约项（条件熵）和近似项（散度）。定义可预测性为条件熵与无条件基线之间的差距（互信息），构建随预测时域变化的可预测性剖面，并基于实用阈值定义信息时域集。

Result: 建立了预测极限的严格信息论框架：可预测性剖面反映过程依赖结构，不必单调，可能在特定时域（如季节性）集中预测信息。信息时域集识别了有意义预测可行的时域，为建模努力分配提供原则性基础。

Conclusion: 该框架将可学习与不可学习的内容分离，区分数据固有极限与模型误差，作为建模前诊断工具识别有意义的预测时域，为跨预测时域的建模努力分配提供原则性指导。

Abstract: Forecasting accuracy is bounded by the information available about the future. This paper makes that statement precise using information-theoretic tools. Under logarithmic loss, the expected performance of any probabilistic forecast decomposes into two parts: an irreducible component and an approximation component. The irreducible term is the conditional entropy of the future given the available information, while the approximation term is the divergence between the true conditional distribution and the forecasting method. The gap between this conditional-entropy limit and an unconditional baseline is exactly the mutual information between the future observation and the declared information set. This leads to a definition of forecastability as the maximum achievable reduction in expected log loss. Evaluated across horizons, forecastability forms a profile that describes how predictive information varies with lead time. This profile reflects the dependence structure of the process and need not be monotone: predictive information may be concentrated at particular lags, including seasonal horizons, even when intermediate horizons contain little useful signal. From this profile, the paper defines the informative horizon set: the horizons at which forecastability exceeds a practical threshold. At horizons not in this set, the achievable gain over the unconditional baseline is necessarily small, regardless of the forecasting method used. The framework therefore separates what is learnable from what is not, and distinguishes limits imposed by the data from errors introduced by modelling. The result is a pre-modelling diagnostic that identifies where meaningful prediction is feasible before any model is chosen, providing a principled basis for allocating modelling effort across forecast horizons.

</details>


### [180] [Simultaneous Estimation of Ballpark Effects and Team Defense Using Total Bases Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21163)
*Jhe-Jia Wu,Tian-Li Yan,Ting-Li Chen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出基于总垒打数残差(TBR)的简单可解释框架，用于分离棒球中球场环境和球队防守对击球结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球击球结果受接触质量、球场环境、防守表现和随机变异等多因素影响，现有方法难以准确分离球场效应和防守效应。

Method: 使用2015-2024年Statcast数据，基于出射速度和发射角构建期望总垒打数，定义相对于基线的残差，在统一回归框架中同时估计球场环境和球队防守效应。

Result: 当估计值与MLB官方指标存在差异时，差异可由主客场表现的一致模式解释；结果与现有防守指标比较显示相似模式；结果反映了联盟趋势变化，与数据驱动定位、防守布阵限制和棒球物理特性变化一致。

Conclusion: TBR框架能有效分离球场效应和防守效应，引入标准化指数便于跨球队、球场和赛季比较，为棒球分析提供实证支持。

Abstract: Estimating ballpark effects and team defense in baseball is challenging because batted-ball outcomes are influenced by multiple factors, including contact quality, ballpark environment, defensive performance, and random variation. In this study, we propose a simple and interpretable framework based on Total Bases Residuals (TBR). Using Statcast data from 2015 to 2024, we construct expected total bases conditional on exit velocity and launch angle, and define residuals relative to this baseline. These residuals allow us to separate the effects of ballpark environment and team defense and to estimate them simultaneously within a unified regression framework. Our results show that, when our estimates differ from official MLB metrics, the differences can be explained by consistent patterns in home and away performance for both teams and their opponents, providing empirical support for our approach. Similar patterns are also observed in comparisons with existing defensive metrics. The results also suggest changes in league-wide outcomes and are broadly consistent with developments in the game, including the increased use of data-driven positioning, the restriction on defensive shifts, and possible changes in the physical properties of the baseball. We further introduce a standardized index that facilitates comparison across teams, ballparks, and seasons by expressing effects in units of standard deviation.

</details>


### [181] [Data Curation for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials by Determinantal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22160)
*Joanna Zou,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 使用行列式点过程(DPPs)从量子力学计算中选择信息量大的原子构型子集，用于构建紧凑而多样的机器学习势能训练数据集，在氧化铪数据上验证了该方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势能的发展面临关键计算瓶颈：生成和标记有用训练数据集的计算成本高昂。需要从昂贵的量子力学方法中选择信息量大的原子构型子集进行标记。

Method: 提出行列式点过程(DPPs)的新应用，利用分子描述符的核函数选择信息量大的原子构型子集，用于构建紧凑而多样的训练集。该方法可用于无监督训练数据整理、异构或多模态数据处理，以及分子动力学模拟中的在线主动学习方案。

Result: 在氧化铪数据上的实验表明，DPPs方法在构建紧凑但多样的训练集方面与现有方法相比具有竞争力，能够提高机器学习表示分子系统的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DPPs为机器学习原子间势能的训练数据选择提供了有前景的方向，特别是在无监督数据整理、异构数据处理和在线主动学习方案中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: The development of machine learning interatomic potentials faces a critical computational bottleneck with the generation and labeling of useful training datasets. We present a novel application of determinantal point processes (DPPs) to the task of selecting informative subsets of atomic configurations to label with reference energies and forces from costly quantum mechanical methods. Through experiments with hafnium oxide data, we show that DPPs are competitive with existing approaches to constructing compact but diverse training sets by utilizing kernels of molecular descriptors, leading to improved accuracy and robustness in machine learning representations of molecular systems. Our work identifies promising directions to employ DPPs for unsupervised training data curation with heterogeneous or multimodal data, or in online active learning schemes for iterative data augmentation during molecular dynamics simulation.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Convergence Analysis of the Random Bisection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20483)
*Ludovick Bouthat,Philippe-André Luneau,Philippe Petitclerc*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种广义二分法，其中分割点根据任意分布随机选择，计算了相对于根位置先验分布的期望收敛率，发现仅取决于切割点c的期望E[c(1-c)]，并推广到K个随机切割的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二分法使用固定中点分割，本文研究随机选择分割点的广义版本，探索随机化对收敛速度的影响，为数值方法提供更灵活的理论框架。

Method: 提出广义随机二分法，切割点c服从任意分布；推导期望收敛率公式，证明仅依赖于E[c(1-c)]；推广到K个随机切割的情况；使用统计模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 发现收敛率仅取决于切割点分布的E[c(1-c)]，与传统二分法相比提供了更一般的收敛性分析框架；K切割版本进一步扩展了方法的适用性；数值模拟验证了理论推导的正确性。

Conclusion: 随机化二分法提供了一种灵活的数值求解框架，收敛性能由切割点分布的简单统计量决定，为传统确定性方法提供了概率视角的扩展，具有理论和应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a generalized version of the bisection method where the cutting point between the two subintervals is chosen at random following an arbitrary distribution. We compute expected convergence rates with respect to any arbitrary a priori distribution for the position of the root in the initial interval and proved that it depends only on the the expectation $\mathbb{E}[c(1-c)]$ of the cut $c$. We also provide a generalization of the method for $K$ random cuts and study its convergence properties. Most probabilistic derivations are kept fairly simple for the ease of understanding of a larger audience. Our theoretical results are then validated numerically using statistical simulation.

</details>


### [183] [Applying acceleration to Krylov subspace eigenvalue solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20590)
*Michelle Baker,Sara Pollock*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 将Nesterov加速梯度下降和Polyak重球法应用于Golub-Ye的无逆预处理Krylov子空间方法，加速求解对称广义特征值问题的最小代数特征值，扩展到块版本并证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Golub和Ye提出的无逆预处理Krylov子空间方法是基于最速下降法的改进，因此考虑应用最速下降法的加速技术（Nesterov加速和Polyak重球法）来进一步提高收敛速度，特别是在求解多个聚类特征值时。

Method: 将Nesterov加速梯度下降和Polyak重球法应用于Golub-Ye的无逆预处理Krylov子空间方法，扩展到块版本，支持更广义的子空间选择，并证明收敛性。研究了固定和自适应保护的动量参数选择策略。

Result: 数值实验显示，与相同子空间大小的LOBPCG相比，加速方法在更少的外迭代次数内收敛，并且在求解小维度多个聚类特征值时通常比基础方法迭代次数更少。提供了Polyak重球法加速的理论解释和参数范围合理性证明。

Conclusion: 成功将加速技术应用于无逆预处理Krylov子空间方法，显著提高了求解对称广义特征值问题最小代数特征值的收敛速度，特别是在处理多个聚类特征值时效果明显，为该方法提供了理论支持和实用参数选择策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we apply acceleration to the inverse-free preconditioned Krylov subspace method introduced by Golub and Ye, which solves the symmetric generalized eigenvalue problem for the algebraically smallest eigenvalue. As the method is an improvement on steepest descent, we consider acceleration based on Nesterov accelerated steepest descent and Polyak's heavy-ball method. We extend acceleration to the block version of the Krylov subspace method and prove convergence for a more generalized choice of subspace. We present numerical results demonstrating the effect of fixed and safeguarded-adaptive choice of the momentum parameter, which show convergence in fewer outer iterations compared with LOBPCG with the same subspace size and generally fewer iterations than the base method when solving for multiple clustered eigenvalues with small dimension size. We also provide an explanation for the acceleration seen from implementing Polyak's heavy-ball method, including justifying the given parameter range.

</details>


### [184] [A posteriori analysis of a virtual element approach on polytopal meshes for the buckling eigenvalue problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20599)
*Franco Dassi,Andres E Rubiano,Iván Velásquez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于C¹虚拟单元法（VEM）屈曲特征值问题的残差型后验误差估计器，该估计器在一般多面体网格上完全可计算，并通过数值实验验证了其最优精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为复杂板结构的预测分析提供可靠的自适应网格细化工具，需要开发适用于C¹虚拟单元法的后验误差估计器，该估计器需在一般多面体网格上完全可计算。

Method: 提出了一种残差型后验误差估计器，利用多项式投影、稳定化贡献和插值估计来界定残差方程，并通过气泡函数论证确保效率。该估计器在开源vem++库中实现。

Result: 估计器在2D和3D数值实验中表现出最优精度和鲁棒性，能够有效指导自适应网格细化，适用于包含非线性平面应力效应的屈曲特征值问题。

Conclusion: 该残差型后验误差估计器为C¹虚拟单元法屈曲分析提供了可靠的自适应网格细化工具，在复杂板结构的预测分析中具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a novel residual-based a posteriori error estimator for the conforming $C^1$ Virtual Element Method (VEM) applied to the buckling eigenvalue problem, incorporating nonlinear plane stress effects in both two and three dimensions. The estimator is fully computable on general polyhedral meshes and implemented within the open-source \texttt{vem++} library. Its reliability is rigorously justified via bounds on the residual equation using polynomial projections, stabilisation contributions, and interpolation estimates, while efficiency is ensured through the use of bubble function arguments. Comprehensive numerical experiments in 2D and 3D illustrate the estimator's optimal accuracy and robustness, highlighting its potential for predictive analysis of complex plate structures.

</details>


### [185] [On Optimal Convergence Rates for the Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation with a Wave Operator via Localized Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20627)
*Hanzhang Hu,Zetao Ma,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于二维时变非线性薛定谔方程的局部正交分解方法，证明了该方法保持守恒律且具有唯一数值解，获得了无条件最优阶超收敛误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对二维时变非线性薛定谔方程，需要开发能够保持物理守恒律、具有良好数值稳定性和高精度的高效数值方法。

Method: 采用局部正交分解方法处理二维时变非线性薛定谔方程，该方法能够保持守恒律并确保数值解的唯一性。

Result: 证明了该方法保持守恒律且具有唯一数值解，获得了无条件最优阶超收敛L^p误差估计，数值模拟验证了理论结果并展示了问题的结构特性。

Conclusion: 提出的局部正交分解方法对于二维时变非线性薛定谔方程是有效的，能够保持物理守恒律、确保数值稳定性，并实现无条件最优阶超收敛精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a Localized Orthogonal Decomposition (LOD) method for the two-dimensional time-dependent nonlinear Schrödinger equation with a wave operator. We prove that our method preserves conservation laws and admits a unique numerical solution; furthermore, we obtain unconditional (i.e., time-step restriction-free) optimal-order superconvergent \(L^p\) error estimates. To complement the theoretical analysis, we present a series of numerical simulations that verify the analytical results and further illustrate structural aspects of the problem.

</details>


### [186] [Residual Recombination Methods as Anderson-like Acceleration: An Algebraic Interpretation of BoostConv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20749)
*Vincenzo Citro,Davide Palitta*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: BoostConv是一种非线性迭代过程的加速技术，本文提出了更鲁棒的算法版本并首次提供了严格的收敛性证明，建立了数学框架并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoostConv虽然在实际应用中表现出色，但其理论性质尚未完全阐明。本文旨在填补这一空白，为该方法提供严格的数学基础，建立收敛性保证，并阐明其作为非线性收敛加速器的解释。

Method: 提出了BoostConv算法的更鲁棒公式化版本，首次提供了严格的收敛性证明。分析将BoostConv置于精确的数学框架中，阐明其作为非线性收敛加速器的解释，并建立了保证收敛到不动点的充分条件。

Result: 通过从线性问题到低维基准测试再到大规模不可压缩Navier-Stokes模拟的多个数值示例，验证了所提方法的鲁棒性和实际相关性。这些结果展示了该方法的有效性，并弥合了经验性能与严格分析之间的差距。

Conclusion: 本文首次为BoostConv提供了严格的收敛性证明，建立了其数学理论基础，阐明了其作为非线性收敛加速器的机制，并通过数值实验验证了其鲁棒性和实用性，为复杂非线性问题的进一步发展和应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: BoostConv has been introduced in earlier works as an effective acceleration technique for nonlinear iterative processes and has been successfully employed in a variety of applications to enhance convergence rates or to compute unstable fixed points that are otherwise inaccessible through standard approaches. Despite its demonstrated practical effectiveness, the theoretical properties of the method have not yet been fully characterized. In this work, we present a more robust formulation of the BoostConv algorithm and, for the first time, provide a rigorous proof of its convergence. The proposed analysis places BoostConv within a precise mathematical framework, clarifying its interpretation as a nonlinear convergence accelerator and establishing sufficient conditions under which convergence to a fixed point is guaranteed. The theoretical findings are illustrated through several numerical examples, spanning from a linear problem to a low-dimensional benchmark and a large-scale incompressible Navier-Stokes simulation. These results demonstrate the robustness and practical relevance of the proposed method and bridge the gap between empirical performance and rigorous analysis, paving the way for further developments and applications to complex nonlinear problems.

</details>


### [187] [Convergence of a finite volume method to weak solutions for the compressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20758)
*Eduard Feireisl,Maria Lukacova-Medvidova,Bangwei She,Yuhuan Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明了上风型有限体积法对具有Dirichlet边界条件的Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统弱解的强收敛性，极限解满足质量和动量方程的弱形式以及熵和弹性能量不等式的弱形式，并符合弱-强唯一性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统数值方法的收敛性，特别是证明有限体积法能够收敛到满足物理约束（如熵不等式）的弱解，这对于数值模拟的可靠性和物理一致性至关重要。

Method: 采用上风型有限体积法，使用分段常数空间近似。收敛证明基于精细的一致性估计与通过连续性方程重整化分析数值密度振荡的细致分析相结合。

Result: 证明了该方法强收敛到Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统的弱解，该解满足质量、动量方程的弱形式，以及熵和弹性能量不等式的弱形式，并满足弱-强唯一性原理。

Conclusion: 该上风型有限体积法能够可靠地收敛到满足物理约束的Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统弱解，为这类复杂流体力学问题的数值模拟提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We prove strong convergence of an upwind-type finite volume method to a weak solution of the Navier-Stokes-Fourier system with the Dirichlet boundary conditions. The limit solution satisfies a weak form of the mass and momentum equations, together with a weak form of the entropy and ballistic energy inequalities, and complies with the weak-strong uniqueness principle. The finite volume method uses piecewise-constant spatial approximations. The convergence proof is based on a combination of delicate consistency estimates with a careful analysis of the oscillations of numerical densities via renormalisation of the continuity equation.

</details>


### [188] [Asymptotic error distribution of Mittag--Leffler Euler method for a fractional stochastic differential equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20840)
*Xinjie Dai,Baiping Zhang,Diancong Jin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究多维分数阶随机微分方程Mittag-Leffler Euler方法的归一化误差渐近分布，针对具有非对角矩阵值核的随机Volterra方程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法渐近误差分布理论主要针对对角核或标量核，对于非对角矩阵值核的分数阶随机微分方程缺乏相关理论分析，需要建立相应的误差分布理论框架

Method: 将分数阶随机微分方程重构为具有奇异核K(u)=u^{α-1}E_{α,α}(Au^α)的随机Volterra方程；采用左矩形规则离散化奇异核；引入辅助K-非离散化方案连接精确解与MLE方法；结合Jacod条件高斯鞅的稳定收敛理论和SVE方法学

Result: 首次建立了包含非对角矩阵值核的数值方法的渐近误差分布理论，为这类复杂分数阶随机系统的数值分析提供了理论基础

Conclusion: 成功解决了非对角矩阵值奇异核分数阶随机微分方程数值方法的渐近误差分布问题，填补了该领域理论空白，为更广泛类型的分数阶随机系统数值分析奠定了基础

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the asymptotic distribution of the normalized error for the Mittag--Leffler Euler (MLE) method applied to a class of multidimensional fractional stochastic differential equations. These equations are reformulated as stochastic Volterra equations (SVEs) featuring a non-diagonal, matrix-valued kernel $K(u)=u^{α-1}E_{α,α}(Au^α)$ with singular exponent $α\in (\frac{1}{2}, 1)$. To enhance computational efficiency, the singular kernel is discretized using the left-rectangle rule, posing technical challenges for the theoretical analysis. To address this, we introduce an auxiliary $K$-undiscretized scheme to bridge the gap between the exact solution and the MLE method, integrating Jacod's stable convergence theory for conditional Gaussian martingales with methodologies developed for SVEs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to establish the asymptotic error distribution for numerical methods incorporating non-diagonal matrix-valued kernels.

</details>


### [189] [Error Analysis of the Explicit Splitting Scheme for Fluid-Poroelastic Structure Interaction Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21088)
*Yifan Wang,Jeonghun Lee,Suncica Canic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对时变Stokes-Biot问题，提出了一种完全离散、可并行、显式松耦合格式的先验误差分析，证明了该格式在时间上具有一阶精度，在空间上具有最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够将流体和孔隙弹性子问题完全解耦的显式松耦合格式，使得每个子问题可以在每个时间步独立求解，同时保持界面条件的一致处理，从而获得稳定的收敛格式。

Method: 采用完全离散、可并行的显式松耦合格式，在每个子域引入Ritz型投影，从时间离散的连续公式中减去完全离散格式，得到简化误差方程。通过离散误差能量恒等式和Gronwall型论证，建立无条件误差估计。

Result: 推导出离散误差能量恒等式，在组合能量-耗散范数下建立了无条件误差估计。证明了时间上的一阶精度和空间上的最优收敛率，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 提出的显式松耦合格式能够有效解耦Stokes-Biot问题，在保持稳定性和收敛性的同时实现可并行计算，为复杂流固耦合问题提供了高效数值求解方法。

Abstract: We present a priori error analysis for a fully discrete, parallelizable, explicit loosely coupled scheme for the time-dependent Stokes-Biot problem. The method decouples the fluid and poroelastic subproblems in a fully explicit fashion, allowing each problem to be solved independently at each time step, with a consistent treatment of the interface conditions that provides stability and convergence of the scheme. The error analysis is carried out in a discrete energy framework. More specifically, we introduce Ritz-type projections in each subdomain, and subtract the fully discrete scheme from the time-discrete continuous formulation. This yields reduced error equations in which the dominant interpolation contributions cancel. The remaining consistency terms stem primarily from time discretization residuals and lagged interface data inherent to the explicit splitting. The main result of this manuscript is the derivation of a discrete error energy identity, and establishment of unconditional error estimates in a combined energy-dissipation norm via a Gronwall-type argument. These estimates demonstrate first-order accuracy in time and optimal spatial convergence rates, as determined by the degree of the finite element polynomials. Numerical experiments based on a manufactured solution corroborate the theory, confirming first-order temporal convergence for all variables, and spatial convergence orders consistent with the chosen approximation spaces.

</details>


### [190] [Efficient Nehari manifold optimization algorithms for computing ground state solutions of nonlinear elliptic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20859)
*Zhaoxing Chen,Wei Liu,Ziqing Xie,Wenfan Yi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的流形优化算法RAG-$\mathcal{N}$，用于计算半线性椭圆系统的基态解，通过结合Nehari流形优化和Nesterov加速技术，显著减少了迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性椭圆系统的基态解是变分泛函的不稳定鞍点，传统方法计算困难。这些解可以表征为Nehari流形$\mathcal{N}$上的局部极小值点，但存在内在不稳定性和分量耦合带来的计算复杂度增加问题。

Method: 结合Nehari流形优化方法(NMOM)和Nesterov型加速思想，提出了Nehari流形上的黎曼加速梯度算法(RAG-$\mathcal{N}$)。核心是在$\mathcal{N}$上执行易于实现的非线性外推步，然后在外推点进行黎曼最速下降更新。为增强鲁棒性，进一步将非单调步长搜索策略整合到算法中。

Result: 数值实验表明，RAG-$\mathcal{N}$算法相比NMOM的黎曼最速下降算法显著减少了迭代次数。算法成功应用于计算具有2、3、4个分量的半线性椭圆系统的基态解，并在不同耦合系数和各种设置下（包括高斯型外部势和奇异扩散系数）验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的RAG-$\mathcal{N}$算法为计算半线性椭圆系统的基态解提供了一种高效稳定的流形优化方法，通过加速技术和鲁棒性增强策略，有效解决了不稳定鞍点计算中的挑战。

Abstract: This paper presents a class of efficient manifold optimization algorithms for computing the ground state solutions of a semilinear elliptic system, which are unstable saddle points of the variational functional. Variational arguments show that these unstable saddle points can be characterized as the local minimizers of the variational functional constrained to the Nehari manifold $\mathcal{N}$. The Nehari manifold optimization method (NMOM) proposed in [Z. Chen, W. Liu, Z. Xie, and W. Yi. SIAM J. Sci. Comput., 47(4): A2098-A2126, 2025] provides a Riemannian gradient descent framework on $\mathcal{N}$ for such constrained minimization problems. To deal with both the intrinsic instability of the solutions and the increased computational complexity introduced by the coupling between components, we combine the ideas from the NMOM and the Nesterov-type acceleration to develop a new efficient Riemannian accelerated gradient algorithm on $\mathcal{N}$ (RAG-$\mathcal{N}$). The key idea is to perform an easy-to-implement nonlinear extrapolation step on $\mathcal{N}$, followed by a Riemannian steepest-descent update at the extrapolated point. To enhance the robustness, we further incorporate a nonmonotone step-size search strategy into the RAG-$\mathcal{N}$ algorithm, obtaining a variant with improved stability. Numerical experiments show that the RAG-$\mathcal{N}$ algorithms substantially reduce the number of iterations compared with the Riemannian steepest descent algorithm of NMOM. Finally, we apply the RAG-$\mathcal{N}$ algorithms to compute the ground state solutions of semilinear elliptic systems with two, three and four components, and investigate their behavior under different coupling coefficients and various settings, including Gaussian-type external potentials and singular diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [191] [Parallel-in-iteration optimization using multigrid reduction-in-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20879)
*G. H. M. Araújo,O. A. Krzysik,H. De Sterck*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种"并行迭代"框架，利用并行时间积分算法（特别是MGRIT）来并行化优化算法的迭代过程，以减少求解优化问题的实际计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于梯度的优化算法（如梯度下降及其非光滑问题的扩展）在病态问题上收敛缓慢，有时需要数万次迭代。由于这些迭代是顺序计算的，在大规模并行模拟中可能成为计算瓶颈。需要减少解决优化问题的实际计算时间。

Method: 将优化算法重新解释为时间相关微分方程（如梯度流）的离散化，然后重新利用并行时间积分算法（特别是多重网格缩减时间方法MGRIT）来并行化迭代过程。该方法原则上允许使用任何其他并行时间方法。

Result: 在两个模型问题上进行了数值演示：标准凸二次问题和非光滑弹性障碍问题（一维和二维空间）。观察到MGRIT的快速收敛，类似于其在扩散型偏微分方程上的典型性能。提供了理论分析将MGRIT收敛与底层优化算法收敛联系起来，并给出了理论预测的并行加速结果。

Conclusion: 提出的并行迭代框架能够有效减少优化问题的实际计算时间，通过将并行时间积分方法应用于优化算法的迭代并行化。MGRIT在模型问题上表现出良好的收敛特性，理论分析支持了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Standard gradient-based iteration algorithms for optimization, such as gradient descent and its various proximal-based extensions to nonsmooth problems, are known to converge slowly for ill-conditioned problems, sometimes requiring many tens of thousands of iterations in practice. Since these iterations are computed sequentially, they may present a computational bottleneck in large-scale parallel simulations. In this work, we present a "parallel-in-iteration" framework that allows one to parallelize across these iterations using multiple processors with the objective of reducing the wall-clock time needed to solve the underlying optimization problem. Our methodology is based on re-purposing parallel time integration algorithms for time-dependent differential equations, motivated by the fact that optimization algorithms often have interpretations as discretizations of time-dependent differential equations (such as gradient flow). Specifically in this work, we use the parallel-in-time method of multigrid reduction-in-time (MGRIT), but note that our approach permits in principle the use of any other parallel-in-time method. We numerically demonstrate the efficacy of our approach on two different model problems, including a standard convex quadratic problem and the nonsmooth elastic obstacle problem in one and two spatial dimensions. For our model problems, we observe fast MGRIT convergence analogous to its prototypical performance on partial differential equations of diffusion type. Some theory is presented to connect the convergence of MGRIT to the convergence of the underlying optimization algorithm. Theoretically predicted parallel speedup results are also provided.

</details>


### [192] [An Explicit Symmetric Exponential Integrator and Its Error Estimate for the Relativistic Charged-Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21137)
*Zhirui Shen,Bin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李分裂的显式对称指数积分器，用于求解一般磁场中相对论带电粒子的运动方程，具有无条件稳定性和二阶收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般磁场中相对论带电粒子的运动方程求解问题，需要开发高效、稳定且能保持长时间哈密顿量守恒的数值方法。

Method: 通过四维时空重构动力学方程，分离线性和非线性部分，基于李分裂构造显式对称指数积分器。

Result: 理论分析证明了该方法的无条件稳定性和二阶收敛性；数值实验验证了其在精度、效率和长时间哈密顿量守恒方面的优越性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的指数积分器为求解相对论带电粒子在一般磁场中的运动提供了高效、稳定且守恒的数值工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates the equations of motion for a relativistic charged particle in a general magnetic field. By reformulating the dynamics in four-dimensional spacetime and separating the linear and nonlinear parts, we construct an explicit symmetric exponential integrator based on Lie splitting. Rigorous analysis establishes its unconditional stability and second-order convergence. Numerical experiments confirm its superior performance, including accuracy, effciency and long-time Hamiltonian conservation.

</details>


### [193] [A globally convergent Carleman-Picard method for an inverse initial-value problem for a nonlinear diffusive coagulation-fragmentation equation coagulation-fragmentation equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21185)
*Thuy T. Le,Minh-Binh Tran,Loc H. Nguyen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Legendre-指数时间降维和Carleman-Picard迭代的全局收敛数值方法，用于从边界观测数据反演非线性扩散凝聚-破碎方程的初始粒子尺寸分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性扩散凝聚-破碎方程的逆初始密度问题，旨在从时间相关的边界观测数据（解及其尺寸导数）中恢复有限区间上的未知初始粒子尺寸分布，这在颗粒系统、气溶胶动力学等应用中具有重要意义。

Method: 开发了一种全局收敛的数值方法：1) 使用Legendre-指数时间降维将原问题转化为空间模态系数的非线性耦合系统；2) 采用Carleman权重和相应的Carleman估计，通过Carleman-Picard迭代实现全局收敛，无需良好初始猜测。

Result: 证明了所提方法的收敛性，获得了完整的初始密度重建程序。数值实验表明，即使在含噪声的边界数据下，该方法也能对多个代表性测试剖面实现准确且稳定的重建。

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性扩散凝聚-破碎方程的逆初始密度问题提供了一种有效的全局收敛数值解法，能够从边界观测中稳定地恢复初始粒子尺寸分布，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study an inverse initial-density problem for a nonlinear diffusive coagulation--fragmentation equation with known coagulation and fragmentation kernels. The objective is to recover the unknown initial particle-size distribution on a finite interval from time-dependent boundary observations of the solution and its size derivative. To solve this inverse problem, we develop a globally convergent numerical method based on a Legendre--exponential time reduction and a Carleman--Picard iteration. The time reduction transforms the original problem into a nonlinear coupled system for the spatial mode coefficients, while the Carleman weight and the corresponding Carleman estimate guaranty the global convergence of the Picard iteration without requiring a good initial guess. We prove the convergence of the proposed method and obtain a complete reconstruction procedure for the initial density. Numerical experiments with noisy boundary data demonstrate that the method yields accurate and stable reconstructions for several representative test profiles.

</details>


### [194] [GasNiTROM: Model Reduction via Non-Intrusive Optimization of Oblique Projection Operators and Guaranteed-Stable Latent-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21254)
*Cole J. Errico,Alberto Padovan,Daniel J. Bodony*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种非侵入式降阶建模框架，通过结合Lyapunov稳定性参数化和斜投影算子，同时实现全局渐近稳定性和高精度预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非侵入式降阶模型在处理具有大瞬态和远离平衡态的系统时存在两个主要问题：1) 预测精度差且可能不稳定；2) 基于正交投影的降维方法可能截断动态重要状态。需要同时解决稳定性和精度问题

Method: 提出非侵入式框架，结合Lyapunov稳定性参数化（确保全局渐近稳定）和矩阵流形参数化的斜投影算子（捕捉系统敏感机制）。使用伴随反向传播计算梯度，无需自动微分

Result: 在三维常微分方程系统和二维雷诺数Re=8300的驱动腔流测试中，相比现有方法，所提模型不仅具有全局渐近稳定性（由构造保证），而且预测精度显著提高

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了非侵入式降阶建模中稳定性和精度的权衡问题，通过Lyapunov稳定性参数化和斜投影算子的结合，实现了既稳定又准确的模型预测

Abstract: Non-intrusive reduced-order modeling techniques are necessary for systems that are simulated using black-box solvers or known only from data. For systems exhibiting large transients and operating far away from equilibria, current non-intrusive models often exhibit poor forecasting accuracy and can even be unstable in infinite or finite time. Recent developments have addressed the stability issue by seeking structure-preserving latent-space architectures when reducing Hamiltonian or Lagrangian full-order dynamics, or by enforcing global stability via Lyapunov-informed parameterizations in the latent space. However, such developments do not necessarily improve the forecasting accuracy of the resulting models, since these formulations achieve dimensionality reduction using orthogonal projections that accidentally truncate dynamically-important states. In this paper, we address both issues by introducing a non-intrusive framework designed to simultaneously identify globally-asymptotically-stable latent-space dynamics, and oblique projection operators capable of capturing the sensitivity mechanisms of the system. In particular, given a Lyapunov-based parameterization of the latent-space tensors, and a matrix-manifold parameterization of the oblique projection operators, we fit a model against high-fidelity training trajectories. Furthermore, we show that the gradient of the objective function can be written in closed form using adjoint-based backpropagation in the latent space, eliminating the need for automatic differentiation. We compare our formulation with state-of-the-art methods on a three-dimensional system of ordinary differential equations, and a two-dimensional lid-driven cavity flow at Reynolds number Re=8300. We demonstrate that our models are not only globally asymptotically stable (as expected by construction), but they are also significantly more accurate.

</details>


### [195] [A Practical Mode-parallel Implementation of the (H-)Tucker Decomposition via Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21379)
*Martina Iannacito,Sascha Portaro,Davide Palitta,Claudio Arlandini,Domitilla Brandoni*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机采样的模式并行张量分解方法，用于Tucker和H-Tucker分解，显著降低了计算时间和存储需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量分解能有效揭示多维数据中的隐藏结构，但传统方法对高维张量的计算能耗高、内存需求大，且现有并行化方法通常仍按模式顺序处理，限制了性能提升

Method: 采用模式并行策略，结合纤维采样和随机范围查找等前沿随机化技术，实现所有模式操作的并行执行，而非传统的顺序模式处理

Result: 提供了分解误差期望值的上界理论保证；数值实验显示方法能显著减少运行时间和存储需求；HPC环境下实验证明了良好的可扩展性

Conclusion: 提出的模式并行随机化张量分解方法有效解决了高维张量分解的计算瓶颈，在保持精度的同时大幅提升了计算效率和可扩展性

Abstract: In the last decades, tensors have emerged as the right tool to represent multidimensional data in a compact yet informative manner. Moreover, it is well-known that by performing low-rank factorizations of such tensors one is often able to effectively unveil possible hidden structure in data, mainly due to unexpected dependencies among the different variables encoded in the given tensor. However, computing these factorizations is extremely energy-consuming and memory-demanding, especially for high-dimensional tensors, namely those with a large number of modes. In this paper we focus on two state-of-the-art tensor decompositions: the Tucker and H-Tucker decompositions. We propose novel numerical strategies able to perform these factorizations in a \emph{mode-parallel} fashion, that is the operations required by the algorithm along all modes are performed in parallel. This is in contrast to what is achieved by many procedures available in the literature that parallelize some of the operations along each mode, e.g., tensor-times-matrix steps, while still visiting one mode at the time in a sequential manner. Our strategies make use of cutting-edge randomization techniques comprising fiber sampling and randomized range-finding steps. We provide upper bounds on the expected value of the error provided by our factorizations while a panel of numerical results showcases the potential of our approach in reducing both the running time and the storage demand of the whole procedure. Moreover, experiments carried out in HPC environments illustrate the good scaling of our mode-parallel approach.

</details>


### [196] [Wavelet-based Galerkin Scheme with Arbitrarily High-Order Convergence for 1D Elliptic Interface Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21394)
*Bin Han,Michelle Michelle*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一维椭圆界面问题提出基于小波的Galerkin方法，使用局部支撑双正交小波基，证明了在H¹范数下达到m-1阶、L²范数下达到m阶的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆界面问题的解在界面处梯度不连续，导致整体正则性低（u∉H¹·⁵(Ω)），传统数值方法难以获得最优收敛率。本文旨在为一维情况设计能处理界面奇异性并获得最优收敛的小波方法。

Method: 提出基于有界区间上局部支撑双正交小波基的Galerkin方法，近似阶为m（m≥2）。通过引入更高尺度层级的小波基函数来捕捉界面邻域的奇异性，从而处理梯度不连续性。

Result: 严格证明了该方法在H¹(Ω)范数下达到m-1阶收敛，在L²(Ω)范数下达到m阶收敛，这些收敛率相对于近似阶m是最优的。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理一维椭圆界面问题的奇异性并获得最优收敛率，与作者先前关于高维（d≥2）界面问题的工作形成互补和鲜明对比。

Abstract: The solution $u$ of an elliptic interface problem in a domain $Ω$ is often smooth away from the interface $Γ\subset Ω$, but its gradient is discontinuous across $Γ$, resulting in low regularity; in particular, $u \notin H^{1.5}(Ω)$. This paper focuses on 1D elliptic interface problems using wavelet methods. We propose a Galerkin method using locally supported biorthogonal wavelet bases on bounded intervals with $m$th approximation order for any integer $m \ge 2$. Additionally, we rigorously prove that its convergence rates are of order $m-1$ in the $H^1(Ω)$-norm and order $m$ in the $L^2(Ω)$-norm, which are optimal with respect to the scheme's approximation order $m$. Our approach involves incorporating wavelet basis functions from higher scale levels to capture the singularity in the neighbourhood of the interface $Γ$. The results in this paper both complement and sharply contrast our findings in Han and Michelle (2024), where we consider a similar wavelet-based method for solving $d$-dimensional elliptic interface problems with $d\ge 2$.

</details>


### [197] [Local linear stability of dual-pairing summation-by-parts methods for nonlinear conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21457)
*Dougal Stewart,Kenneth Duru*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该研究分析了双配对求和-差分（DP SBP）方法在非线性守恒律中的局部能量稳定性，证明了其熵稳定体积迎风滤波器可确保局部能量稳定性，为设计同时具有熵稳定性和局部能量稳定性的高阶方法提供了新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gassner等人的研究表明，局部能量稳定性对于非线性守恒律的精确数值模拟至关重要，但目前尚不清楚高阶数值方法是否能同时实现熵稳定和局部能量稳定。本研究旨在探究最近发展的熵稳定高阶DP SBP方法是否具备局部能量稳定性。

Method: 分析最近发展的熵稳定高阶双配对求和-差分（DP SBP）方法，特别是其内置的熵稳定体积迎风滤波器，研究这些方法在非线性守恒律中的局部能量稳定性特性。

Result: 分析表明，DP SBP方法中的熵稳定体积迎风滤波器能够确保局部能量稳定性。数值实验（包括1D和2D的无粘性Burgers方程和非线性浅水方程）支持理论发现。此外，2D正压剪切不稳定性的精确数值模拟展示了该方法在解析湍流尺度方面的效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计可靠的高阶数值方法提供了新策略，这些方法可同时证明具有熵稳定性和局部能量稳定性，为非线性守恒律的精确模拟提供了理论保证和实际验证。

Abstract: A recent study by Gassner et al. [J. Sci. Comput. 90:79 (2022)] demonstrates that local energy stability--that is, ensuring the asymptotic numerical growth rate does not exceed the continuous growth rate--is crucial for achieving accurate numerical simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. While nonlinear entropy stability is necessary for numerical stability (i.e., ensuring the boundedness of nonlinear numerical solutions), local energy stability is essential to prevent unresolved high-frequency wave modes from dominating the simulation. Currently, it remains an open question whether high-order numerical methods for nonlinear conservation laws can be simultaneously entropy-stable and locally energy-stable. In this work, we examine the local energy-stability properties of recently developed entropy-stable, high-order accurate dual-pairing (DP) SBP methods, as introduced by Duru et al. [arXiv: 2411.06629], for nonlinear conservation laws. Our analysis indicates that the entropy-stable volume upwind filter inherent in these methods can ensure local energy stability. This approach offers a novel numerical strategy for designing reliable high-order methods for nonlinear conservation laws that are provably entropy-stable and locally energy-stable. The theoretical findings are supported by numerical experiments involving the inviscid Burgers equation and nonlinear shallow water equations, in 1D and 2D. Furthermore, we present accurate numerical simulations of 2D barotropic shear instability, with fully developed turbulence, demonstrating the efficiency of the DP SBP method in resolving turbulent scales.

</details>


### [198] [A fully decoupled and structure-preserving relaxation Crank--Nicolson finite element method for Gross--Pitaevskii--Poisson model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21589)
*Dongqian Li,Huini Liu,Yin Yang,Peimeng Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全解耦、结构保持的松弛Crank-Nicolson有限元方法，用于求解描述超冷等离子体的耦合Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson系统，该方法保持物理不变量并实现二阶时间精度和最优空间收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发高效、稳定的数值方法来求解耦合Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson系统，该系统用于模拟超冷等离子体。现有方法在处理非线性相互作用和电荷密度项时存在耦合困难，需要设计能够保持物理不变量且计算效率高的完全解耦算法。

Method: 通过引入合适的辅助变量重新表述非线性相互作用和电荷密度项，将原始系统转化为等价形式，从而设计出线性、完全解耦的松弛Crank-Nicolson有限元格式。该方法在完全离散层面保持各组分质量和修正的离散能量等关键物理不变量。

Result: 证明了格式的适定性和唯一性，并严格推导了最优误差估计：时间上达到二阶精度，空间上对于P^k有限元近似实现最优(k+1)阶收敛。数值实验验证了理论结果，表明该方法能有效保持守恒性质并准确捕捉耦合GPP系统的复杂动力学行为。

Conclusion: 提出的完全解耦、结构保持的松弛Crank-Nicolson有限元方法为求解耦合Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson系统提供了一种高效、精确的数值工具，在保持物理守恒律的同时实现了计算效率和解耦优势，适用于超冷等离子体等复杂物理系统的模拟。

Abstract: We propose a fully decoupled, structure-preserving relaxation Crank--Nicolson finite element method (FEM) for the coupled Gross--Pitaevskii--Poisson (GPP) system modeling ultracold plasmas. By introducing suitable auxiliary variables to reformulate the nonlinear interaction and charge density terms, the original system is recast into an equivalent form that enables a linear, fully decoupled numerical scheme. The proposed method preserves key physical invariants, including the mass of each component and a modified discrete energy, at the fully discrete level. We establish the well-posedness and uniqueness of the scheme and rigorously derive optimal error estimates, achieving second-order accuracy in time and optimal $(k+1)$-th order convergence in space for $P^k$ finite element approximations. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of the method in preserving conservation properties and accurately capturing complex dynamical behaviors of the coupled GPP system.

</details>


### [199] [Piecewise-polynomial interpolations and quadratures for parametric PDEs with log-Laplace random inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21628)
*Dinh Dũng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文建立了参数椭圆PDE解的Laguerre广义分段多项式混沌展开系数的稀疏性，并推导了半离散近似的收敛率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有对数拉普拉斯随机输入的参数椭圆偏微分方程解的稀疏表示，为高效数值方法提供理论基础

Method: 建立Laguerre广义分段多项式混沌展开系数的ℓp可求和性与加权ℓ2可求和性稀疏性，推导半离散近似的收敛率

Result: 获得了稀疏网格、分段多项式插值及相关最小二乘近似的收敛率，这些收敛率对生成的求积公式同样有效

Conclusion: 所建立的稀疏性理论为参数椭圆PDE的高效数值求解提供了理论保证，支持稀疏网格和最小二乘等方法的收敛性分析

Abstract: We establish a sparsity in terms of $\ell_p$-summability and weighted $\ell_2$-summability for the coefficients of the Laguerre generalized piecewise-polynomial chaos expansion of solutions to parametric elliptic PDEs with log-Laplace random inputs. From the sparsity, we derive convergence rates for semi-discrete approximations with respect to parametric variables. These rates are valid for sparse-grid, piecewise-polynomial interpolations and the generated quadratures, and to related extended least-squares approximations and generated quadratures.

</details>


### [200] [A Perturbation Method for Index Detection for Linear Matrix Pencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21771)
*Hanna Blazhko,Michał Wojtylak*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无穷远处特征值的Puiseux展开的严格非渐近界，提供了误差分析，估计了随机扰动矩阵特征向量条件数的期望值，并将结果应用于线性铅笔的Cayley变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无穷远处特征值的Puiseux展开的精确界和误差分析，以及随机扰动下特征向量条件数的统计特性，为数值计算提供理论保证。

Method: 采用严格的非渐近分析方法推导Puiseux展开的误差界，使用随机矩阵理论估计特征向量条件数的期望值，并将结果应用于线性铅笔的Cayley变换。

Result: 获得了无穷远处特征值Puiseux展开的严格非渐近界和误差分析，估计了随机扰动矩阵特征向量条件数的期望值，并通过数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为特征值在无穷远处的渐近行为提供了严格的数学保证，为随机扰动下特征向量条件数的统计分析提供了理论工具，对数值线性代数和扰动理论有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Rigorous, non-asymptotic bounds for the Puiseux expansion of the eigenvalue at infinity are given. Error analysis is provided. Further, the expected value of the eigenvector condition number of a randomly perturbed matrix is estimated. The latter result is applied to the Cayley transform of the linear pencil. Numerical simulations illustrating the theoretical findings are provided.

</details>


### [201] [Isogeometric analysis with $C^1$ cubic Powell-Sabin splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21821)
*Jan Grošelj,Ada Šadl Praprotnik,Hendrik Speleers*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究C¹三次Powell-Sabin样条在平面和空间曲面域边界值问题数值解中的应用，展示其作为C⁰三次拉格朗日元和双三次NURBS替代方案的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法如C⁰三次拉格朗日元和双三次NURBS在曲面域边界值问题求解中存在局限性，需要更灵活且精确的几何表示方法。

Method: 回顾非结构化三角剖分上多项式与有理C¹三次Powell-Sabin样条的构造与基本性质，利用这些灵活表示创建适合曲面域精确描述的几何映射，并在等几何分析框架中求解泊松和双调和问题。

Result: C¹三次Powell-Sabin样条能够精确描述各类曲面域，在等几何分析中有效求解边界值问题，相比传统方法展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: C¹三次Powell-Sabin样条是C⁰三次拉格朗日元和双三次NURBS的强大替代方案，特别适用于曲面域边界值问题的数值求解。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider $C^1$ cubic Powell-Sabin splines for the numerical solution of boundary value problems on planar and spatial surface domains. We first review the construction and basic properties of polynomial and rational $C^1$ cubic Powell-Sabin spline representations on unstructured triangulations. Then, we discuss how these flexible representations can be exploited to create geometry mappings suited for a precise description of (classes of) surface domains. This is illustrated with several examples. Finally, the obtained domain descriptions are utilized in the isogeometric analysis framework for solving various Poisson and biharmonic problems. It is demonstrated that $C^1$ cubic Powell-Sabin splines form a powerful alternative to $C^0$ cubic Lagrange elements and bicubic NURBS.

</details>


### [202] [On splitting strategies for the numerical solution of stochastic delay differential equations with correlated noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21858)
*Cónall Kelly,Wenshi Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有相关噪声的标量半线性随机延迟微分方程（SDDE）的数值解法，分析了Lie-Trotter分裂方法的均方强收敛性，发现当噪声不相关（ρ=0）时方法具有1/2阶收敛性，但相关噪声（ρ≠0）时收敛性无法保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有相关噪声的随机延迟微分方程的数值解法，这类方程可以自然分解为线性无延迟SDE和非线性SDDE两个子系统。分裂方法通过分别求解子系统并组合结果来实现数值求解，但相关噪声对收敛性的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用分裂方法，特别是Lie-Trotter分裂策略，将原SDDE分解为线性随机微分方程（无延迟）和非线性随机延迟微分方程两个子系统分别求解。通过理论分析建立均方误差上界，并进行数值实验验证理论结果，同时对比了Strang分裂方法。

Result: 理论分析表明：当噪声不相关（ρ=0）时，Lie-Trotter分裂方法具有1/2阶均方强收敛性；当噪声相关（ρ≠0）时，全局均方误差上界包含与相关性大小成线性关系的项，且与步长无关，收敛性无法保证。数值实验证实了ρ=0时的收敛阶，并显示随着|ρ|增大，误差迅速衰减至接近零。

Conclusion: Lie-Trotter分裂方法在求解具有相关噪声的随机延迟微分方程时，仅在噪声不相关的情况下能保证收敛性（1/2阶）。相关噪声会引入与步长无关的误差项，破坏收敛性保证。Strang分裂方法表现出类似的数值行为。这表明在处理相关噪声的SDDE时需要特别谨慎。

Abstract: In this article we investigate the numerical solution of a scalar semilinear stochastic delay differential equation (SDDE) where the linear instantaneous feedback and nonlinear delayed feedback terms are perturbed by a pair of standard Brownian motions with correlation $ρ$. Such SDDEs may be naturally decomposed into two subsystems: a linear stochastic differential equation (SDE) without delay, and a nonlinear SDDE.
  Splitting methods work by solving each subsystem separately and composing the results over a single step. Our main theoretical result provides a bound on the mean-square error of a particular strategy for doing this, known as Lie-Trotter splitting. This bound implies that the method is mean-square strongly convergent with order $1/2$ when $ρ=0$, so that the noises are uncorrelated, but assurances of convergence are lost when $ρ\neq 0$. Indeed we develop an upper bound on the global mean-square error with a term depends linearly on the magnitude of the correlation, and is independent of the stepsize.
  While our theoretical error bound is an estimate from above, we conduct numerical experiments that confirm the order of mean-square strong convergence of Lie-Trotter splitting in the $ρ=0$ case, and demonstrate a rapid fall-off to effectively zero as $|ρ|$ increases. Similar numerical results are observed for an alternative commonly used strategy known as Strang splitting.

</details>


### [203] [Long-time behavior of multi-step Finite Difference schemes with boundary via steepest descent and analytic combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21899)
*Thomas Bellotti,Tommaso Tenna*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文使用解析组合数学中的最速下降法和奇点分析方法，准确描述了线性数值格式（包括著名的蛙跳格式）在稳定和不稳定边界条件下长时间极限的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性数值格式（特别是蛙跳格式）在边界条件下的长时间行为，传统分析方法存在局限性，需要更精确的数学工具来描述稳定和不稳定边界条件对数值解长期演化的影响。

Method: 采用解析组合数学中的最速下降法（steepest descent arguments）和奇点分析（singularity analysis）方法，这些方法源自复分析和渐近分析，用于研究生成函数在奇点附近的行为，从而推导数值格式的长时间渐近行为。

Result: 该方法能够准确描述线性数值格式在稳定和不稳定边界条件下的长时间极限行为，包括对蛙跳格式这种复杂情况的精确分析，提供了传统方法难以获得的精确渐近结果。

Conclusion: 解析组合数学的工具为分析线性数值格式在边界条件下的长时间行为提供了强大而精确的数学框架，特别适用于处理传统方法难以分析的复杂情况如蛙跳格式。

Abstract: We demonstrate how steepest descent arguments and singularity analysis from analytic combinatorics allow for an accurate description of the behavior of linear numerical schemes -- including the notorious leap-frog scheme -- in presence of stable and unstable boundary conditions in the long-time limit.

</details>


### [204] [A Novel Method for Enforcing Exactly Dirichlet, Neumann and Robin Conditions on Curved Domain Boundaries for Physics Informed Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21909)
*Suchuan Dong,Yuchuan Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种在任意曲线边界四边形域上精确强制Dirichlet、Neumann和Robin边界条件的系统方法，结合TFC约束表达式和超限插值，并处理边界交点处的兼容性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有复杂曲线边界的四边形域上精确强制各种边界条件（Dirichlet、Neumann、Robin）是科学计算和机器学习中的重要问题，特别是在边界交点处需要处理兼容性约束以确保边界条件的精确满足。

Method: 基于一般四边形域与标准域之间的精确映射，结合TFC（函数连接理论）约束表达式和超限插值，开发了四步程序来系统构造满足边界条件的函数形式，特别处理了Neumann或Robin边界相交时的兼容性约束。

Result: 方法已与极端学习机（ELM）技术结合实现，在多种具有复杂几何形状的二维域上进行了大量线性/非线性静态/动态问题的数值实验，边界条件误差达到机器精度水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在曲线边界上精确强制Dirichlet、Neumann和Robin边界条件，特别是在处理边界交点兼容性约束方面表现出色，为科学机器学习中的边界条件处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present a systematic method for exactly enforcing Dirichlet, Neumann, and Robin type conditions on general quadrilateral domains with arbitrary curved boundaries. Our method is built upon exact mappings between general quadrilateral domains and the standard domain, and employs a combination of TFC (theory of functional connections) constrained expressions and transfinite interpolations. When Neumann or Robin boundaries are present, especially when two Neumann (or Robin) boundaries meet at a vertex, it is critical to enforce exactly the induced compatibility constraints at the intersection, in order to enforce exactly the imposed conditions on the joining boundaries. We analyze in detail and present constructions for handling the imposed boundary conditions and the induced compatibility constraints for two types of situations: (i) when Neumann (or Robin) boundary only intersects with Dirichlet boundaries, and (ii) when two Neumann (or Robin) boundaries intersect with each other. We describe a four-step procedure to systematically formulate the general form of functions that exactly satisfy the imposed Dirichlet, Neumann, or Robin conditions on general quadrilateral domains. The method developed herein has been implemented together with the extreme learning machine (ELM) technique we have developed recently for scientific machine learning. Ample numerical experiments are presented with several linear/nonlinear stationary/dynamic problems on a variety of two-dimensional domains with complex boundary geometries. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method has enforced the Dirichlet, Neumann, and Robin conditions on curved domain boundaries exactly, with the numerical boundary-condition errors at the machine accuracy.

</details>


### [205] [Ensemble Kalman inversion with non-smooth regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21916)
*Simon Weissmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于次梯度的集合卡尔曼反演(SEKI)方法，用于处理具有凸、非光滑正则化的变分反问题，建立了连续时间粒子系统的适定性，并提出了保持无导数结构的离散时间方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的确定性EKI及其Tikhonov正则化变体主要针对光滑目标函数进行分析，缺乏能够处理次梯度动力学的相应框架。需要建立一个能够容纳非光滑正则化器的EKI框架。

Method: 提出基于次梯度的EKI(SEKI)公式，通过协方差预处理的微分包含将非光滑正则化器纳入集合均值。在线性前向模型设置下，使用极大单调算子理论和Yosida近似建立连续时间粒子系统的适定性。基于连续时间动力学，提出保持EKI无导数结构的显式离散时间方案。

Result: 在强凸情况下分析了离散时间方案作为优化方法的收敛性。数值实验表明，在总变差正则化的计算机断层扫描和ℓ1惩罚的稀疏恢复中，非光滑正则化可以稳定且原则性地纳入集合卡尔曼反演。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了处理非光滑正则化的EKI理论框架，证明了SEKI在连续时间系统的适定性和离散时间方案的收敛性，为集合卡尔曼反演在非光滑正则化问题中的应用提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates ensemble Kalman inversion (EKI) for variational inverse problems with convex, potentially non-smooth regularization. While deterministic EKI and its Tikhonov-regularized variants have primarily been analyzed for smooth objectives, a corresponding framework accommodating subgradient dynamics has not yet been established. To address this gap, we introduce a subgradient-based formulation of EKI (SEKI) that incorporates non-smooth regularizers through a covariance-preconditioned differential inclusion for the ensemble mean. In the linear forward-model setting, well-posedness of the resulting continuous-time particle system is established under minimal assumptions on the regularization functional using maximal monotone operator theory and Yosida approximations. Motivated by the continuous-time dynamics, we propose an explicit discrete-time scheme that preserves the derivative-free structure of EKI and analyze its convergence as an optimization method in the strongly convex case. Numerical experiments in computed tomography with total variation regularization and sparse recovery with $\ell_1$ penalties illustrate that non-smooth regularization can be incorporated into ensemble Kalman inversion in a stable and principled manner.

</details>

### [206] [The Search for $K_L \rightarrow π^0π^0γγ$ and $K_L\rightarrow π^0π^0X$ where $X\rightarrow 2γ$ at the KOTO Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20960)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索稀有K介子衰变过程，特别是KL→π⁰π⁰X（X为类轴子粒子）和KL→π⁰π⁰γγ衰变，以探测超出标准模型的新物理，如类轴子粒子的存在

Method: 使用KOTO实验2021年采集的数据，在X质量160-220 MeV/c²范围内搜索KL→π⁰π⁰X衰变，同时首次搜索KL→π⁰π⁰γγ衰变。通过分析信号区域的事件数来设定分支比上限

Result: 在KL→π⁰π⁰X搜索中观测到3个事件，其中2个事件集中在X质量177 MeV/c²附近。对分支比设定了95%置信水平上限BR(KL→π⁰π⁰X) < (1-20)×10⁻⁷。对于KL→π⁰π⁰γγ，未观测到事件，设定上限BR(KL→π⁰π⁰γγ) < 1.69×10⁻⁶

Conclusion: 实验结果为KL→π⁰π⁰X和KL→π⁰π⁰γγ衰变设定了新的分支比上限，在177 MeV/c²附近观测到的两个事件值得进一步研究，但当前数据不足以得出类轴子粒子存在的结论

Abstract: We performed searches for $K_L\rightarrow π^0π^0X$ where $X$ may be an axion-like particle which promptly decays to two photons, and the first search for $K_L \rightarrow π^0π^0γγ$ at the KOTO experiment using data taken in 2021. The search is performed for $X$ mass in the range of 160$\unicode{x2013}$220 MeV/$c^2$. Three events were observed in the signal region, with two events near an $X$ mass of 177 MeV/$c^2$. This result led to a range of upper limits on the branching ratio, BR($K_L\rightarrow π^0π^0X$) $< (1\unicode{x2013}20) \times 10^{-7}$ at the 95% confidence level (C.L.). No events were observed for the analysis of $K_L \rightarrow π^0π^0γγ$, setting an upper limit on the branching ratio, BR($K_L \rightarrow π^0π^0γγ$) $< 1.69 \times 10^{-6}$ at the 95% C.L.

</details>


### [207] [Record accumulation of antiprotons in a Penning-Malmberg Trap and their preparation for improved production of antihydrogen beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21168)
*B. Lee,B. Kim,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,M. Chung,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,S. Geffroy,P. Guichard,P. A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,S. Kim,E. S. Kim,N. Kuroda,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,K. Park,E. Pérez,P. Pérez,C. Regenfus,C. Roumegou,J. Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: GBAR实验在CERN的AD/ELENA反物质工厂中，通过静电脉冲漂移管将100 keV反质子束减速，结合冷电子云库仑相互作用降低发射度增长，实现高效反氢生产用于引力研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为引力研究生产反氢原子，需要高效的反质子减速和捕获方法。传统方法使用箔片减速反质子，而GBAR实验旨在开发更有效的静电减速方案，提高反氢生产速率。

Method: 采用静电脉冲漂移管将100 keV反质子束减速至6 keV；在陷阱中利用冷电子云的库仑相互作用降低发射度增长；通过旋转壁冷却进一步压缩反质子云；可重新加速至10 keV能量，包括时间聚焦。

Result: 成功捕获ELENA束流的56(3)%；每次发射可提供6.4(0.4)×10^6个反质子用于改进反氢生产；在35分钟内累积超过6.4(0.4)×10^7个反质子，创下记录。

Conclusion: GBAR实验成功开发并验证了高效的静电减速和捕获方案，显著提高了反氢生产速率，为引力研究提供了重要技术基础。

Abstract: CERN's AD/ELENA ``antimatter factory'' - unique worldwide - serves several experiments, all of which use electromagnetic traps to accumulate antiprotons for fundamental science. The GBAR experiment employs a charge-exchange reaction between an antiproton beam and a positronium cloud to produce antihydrogen for gravitational studies. GBAR has also pioneered an electrostatic scheme using a pulsed drift tube to decelerate the 100 keV antiproton beam, rather than slowing the antiprotons in a foil, as is commonly done in other experiments. Following first results producing a 6 keV antihydrogen beam directly after the decelerator, a trap has now been installed to increase the production rate. The emittance growth resulting from the deceleration is reduced in the trap by Coulomb interaction with a cold electron cloud. The antiproton cloud is further compressed using rotating wall cooling and can be re-accelerated up to energies of 10 keV, including a time focus. Here we describe the commissioning results, trapping 56(3)\% of the ELENA beam, delivering $6.4(0.4)~\times~10^{6}$ antiprotons per shot for improved production of antihydrogen, and a record accumulation of over $6.4(0.4)~\times~10^{7}$ antiprotons in under 35 minutes.

</details>


### [208] [Charmed baryon decays at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21722)
*Jaeyoung Kim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于Belle和Belle II实验在Υ(4S)共振能区收集的1.4 ab⁻¹数据，测量了Ξ_c^{0/+}和Λ_c^+重子的新分支比，包括多个首次观测，并首次在单卡比玻压低的三体衰变中寻找CP破坏，检验U-旋对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量粲重子衰变分支比对于检验标准模型和理解强子物理至关重要。目前对Ξ_c^{0/+}和Λ_c^+重子的分支比测量仍不完善，需要更多实验数据。同时，在粲重子衰变中寻找CP破坏是检验标准模型和探索新物理的重要途径，特别是在单卡比玻压低的三体衰变中，这还能检验U-旋对称性。

Method: 利用Belle和Belle II实验在Υ(4S)共振能区收集的总积分亮度为1.4 ab⁻¹的e⁺e⁻对撞数据。通过分析Ξ_c^{0/+}和Λ_c^+重子的衰变产物，测量其分支比。对于CP破坏的搜索，专注于单卡比玻压低的三体衰变道，通过比较粒子和反粒子的衰变率来检验CP对称性，并利用U-旋对称性进行理论检验。

Result: 获得了Ξ_c^{0/+}和Λ_c^+重子的新分支比测量结果，包括多个首次观测到的衰变道。在单卡比玻压低的三体衰变中进行了首次CP破坏搜索，提供了对U-旋对称性的实验检验。

Conclusion: 该研究显著提高了对粲重子衰变分支比的测量精度，填补了多个测量空白。首次在单卡比玻压低的三体衰变中搜索CP破坏，为理解粲重子衰变中的CP破坏机制提供了重要实验数据，同时检验了U-旋对称性在粲重子衰变中的适用性。

Abstract: Belle and Belle II experiments have collected $e^+e^-$ collision data with center-of-mass energies at or near the $Υ(4S)$ resonance. Using total $1.4\,\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$ combined dataset, we present new measurements of branching fractions for $Ξ_c^{0/+}$ and $Λ_c^+$ baryons, including several first observations. Additionally, we report the initial search for $CP$ violation in singly Cabibbo-suppressed three-body decays of charmed baryons, providing a test of $U$-spin symmetry.

</details>


### [209] [Study of the Run-3 muon flux at the SND@LHC experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21878)
*The SND@LHC Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SND@LHC实验对LHC Run-3期间产生的长程μ子背景进行了全面表征，验证了蒙特卡洛模拟与实验测量的一致性，分析了2024年束流光学变化导致的背景增加，并针对未来HL-LHC配置提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATLAS相互作用点产生的长程μ子是SND@LHC实验中子相互作用搜索的主要背景，需要准确表征和模拟以理解实验性能并设计缓解策略。

Method: 通过实验测量与蒙特卡洛模拟的对比，全面表征LHC Run-3期间的μ子通量；分析束流光学变化的影响；利用模拟结果识别高角度μ子来源；设计并实验验证缓解策略；将模拟框架应用于未来HL-LHC配置。

Result: 测量与模拟的μ子率在所有Run-3配置中一致在10-15%范围内；2024年束流光学变化导致背景显著增加，2025年恢复名义光学后由于ATLAS采用水平交叉而未恢复2022-2023水平；识别出LHC色散抑制区衍射质子损失产生的高角度μ子贡献；未来HL-LHC配置将导致μ子率显著上升，但硅顶点探测器升级将保持实验效率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功表征了SND@LHC实验的μ子背景，验证了模拟工具的可靠性，识别了背景增加的关键机制，并证明了即使在HL-LHC高率环境下，通过探测器升级仍能保持实验性能。

Abstract: Long-range muons produced in proton-proton collisions at the ATLAS interaction point constitute the primary background for neutrino interaction searches at the SND@LHC experiment. This work presents a comprehensive characterization of the muon flux throughout LHC Run-3, benchmarking Monte Carlo simulations against experimental measurements. Measured and simulated muon rates agree within 10-15% across all Run-3 configurations. Following the substantial background increase in 2024 as a result of a beam optics change, the reversion to nominal optics in 2025 did not restore the 2022-2023 levels due to the unprecedented adoption of horizontal crossing in ATLAS. As enlightened by simulation results, the latter enhanced the contribution of high-angle muons originating from diffractive proton losses in the LHC Dispersion Suppressor region. Their identification enabled the design of mitigation strategies that were experimentally validated. The simulation framework was also applied to the future High-Luminosity LHC configuration, resulting in a considerable muon rate rise, driven by both the planned luminosity increase and the enlarged magnet aperture. Nevertheless, the upgrade from emulsion films to silicon vertex detectors will preserve the efficiency of the experiment even in such a high-rate environment.

</details>


### [210] [First Limits on Axion Dark Matter from a DALI Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21951)
*Javier De Miguel,Enrique Joven,Elvio Hernández-Suárez,Juan F. Hernández-Cabrera,Haroldo Lorenzo-Hernández,Dylan Carroll,Roger J. Hoyland,Edgar S. Carlin,Antonios Gardikiotis,Abaz Kryemadhi,J. Daniel Marrero-Falcón,Marios Moroudas,Chiko Otani,J. Alberto Rubiño-Martín,Konstantin Zioutas*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: DALI原型机暗物质搜索实验未发现轴子信号，在6.883-6.920 GHz频段设定了新的排除极限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证DALI（暗物质轴子光干涉仪）方法在暗物质搜索中的可行性，特别是针对轴子类粒子的探测

Method: 使用低温、磁化、按比例缩小的DALI原型机进行36小时数据采集，分析轴子类粒子信号

Result: 未发现统计显著的超额信号，在6.883-6.920 GHz频段设定了轴子-光子耦合常数的新排除极限：g_{aγγ}≲1.27×10^{-11} GeV^{-1}（对应28.54 μeV）

Conclusion: DALI方法得到验证，结果支持开发下一代光晕仪以探索更宽质量范围并升级仪器

Abstract: We report a pilot dark-matter search with a cryogenic, magnetized, scaled-down DALI prototype. An analysis of 36 hours of data reveals no statistically significant excess attributable to axionlike particles. We therefore set new exclusion limits in the 6.883--6.920 GHz band, reaching an axion-photon coupling sensitivity of $g_{aγγ}\lesssim 1.27\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ at 28.54 $μ$eV. These results consolidate the DALI approach and motivate a next-stage haloscope to explore a broader mass range with upgraded instrumentation.

</details>


### [211] [Search for low-mass vector and scalar resonances decaying into a quark-antiquark pair in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21965)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中搜索50-300 GeV质量范围的共振态衰变为夸克-反夸克对，未发现证据并设置了最严格的耦合限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找超出标准模型的新物理共振态，特别是质量在50-300 GeV范围内衰变为夸克-反夸克对的标量和矢量共振态

Method: 使用CMS实验在2016-2018年收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度13 fb⁻¹），通过硬初态辐射产生的具有双叉子结构的大半径喷注，利用ParticleNet算法区分底夸克对衰变和轻夸克对衰变，并抑制背景过程

Result: 未发现共振态存在的证据，为50-250 GeV质量范围内的新标量和矢量共振态与夸克的耦合设置了迄今为止最严格的限制

Conclusion: 在50-300 GeV质量范围内未发现衰变为夸克-反夸克对的新物理共振态，为相关理论模型提供了重要的实验约束

Abstract: A search for resonances with masses from 50 to 300 GeV decaying into a quark-antiquark pair is presented. The search uses proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC in 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 13 fb$^{-1}$. Two coupling scenarios are considered, with the resonances coupled either equally to all flavors of quarks or preferentially to bottom quarks. The search targets resonances produced in association with hard initial-state radiation, resulting in a large-radius jet with a two-pronged substructure. The ParticleNet algorithm is used to distinguish resonance decays to bottom quark pairs from lighter quark pairs and to suppress background processes. The invariant jet mass spectrum is scrutinized for peaking excesses over a falling background. No evidence for such resonances is observed. Limits are set on the couplings of new scalar and vector resonances to quarks, representing the most stringent limits to date in the mass range of 50$-$250 GeV.

</details>


### [212] [Search for Higgs boson production at high transverse momentum in the WW decay channel in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22233)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中搜索高横向动量希格斯玻色子在WW衰变道的产生，使用138 fb⁻¹数据，通过先进transformer算法识别希格斯候选喷注，未发现显著信号信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高横向动量(pT)希格斯玻色子在WW衰变道的产生，这是首次专门研究高度洛伦兹增强的H→WW衰变，补充先前在其他衰变道的高pT希格斯玻色子搜索。

Method: 使用CMS实验在2016-2018年收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据(138 fb⁻¹)。将希格斯玻色子可见衰变产物重建为单个大半径喷注，分为1轻子(1ℓ)和0轻子(0ℓ)通道。使用基于transformer的先进算法识别希格斯候选喷注，并采用Lund喷注平面重加权技术进行校准。1ℓ通道进一步分为胶子融合、矢量玻色子融合和与强子衰变矢量玻色子伴随产生类别。

Result: 测量到的截面乘以H→WW分支比相对于标准模型预期的比值为μ = -0.19⁺⁰·⁴⁸₋₀·₄₆，表明没有发现超出背景的信号证据。

Conclusion: 这是首次专门研究高度洛伦兹增强的H→WW衰变，为高pT希格斯玻色子物理提供了重要补充，虽然未发现显著信号，但建立了该衰变道的分析方法框架。

Abstract: A search for Higgs boson (\PH) production at high transverse momentum ($p_\mathrm{T}$) in the WW decay channel is presented. The analysis uses proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the CMS experiment in 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The visible decay products of the Higgs boson are reconstructed as a single large-radius jet with one isolated lepton or none (1$\ell$ and 0$\ell$, respectively; $\ell$ = e, $μ$). The \PH-candidate jets are identified using an advanced transformer-based algorithm and are calibrated with the Lund jet plane reweighting technique. The 1$\ell$ channel is further split into gluon fusion, vector boson fusion, and associated production with hadronically decaying vector boson categories, while the 0$\ell$ channel considers all production processes inclusively. The measured cross section times the H $\to$ WW branching fraction relative to the standard model expectation is $μ$ = $-$0.19$^{+0.48}_{-0.46}$, indicating no evidence of a signal above the background. This measurement represents the first dedicated study of highly Lorentz-boosted H $\to$ WW decays, complementing earlier searches for high-$p_\mathrm{T}$ Higgs boson in other decay channels.

</details>

### [213] [Propagation of Condensation via Neumann Localization in the Dilute Bose Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20776)
*Lukas Junge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明包含谱间隙的拉普拉斯算子的诺伊曼局部化不等式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉普拉斯算子的谱性质，特别是谱间隙的定量估计，这对于理解偏微分算子的局部化行为和谱理论具有重要意义

Method: 将立方体划分为重叠的子立方体族，分析相关的投影算子，通过离散诺伊曼拉普拉斯算子得到算子不等式

Result: 获得了包含谱间隙的诺伊曼局部化不等式，并得到了定量的谱间隙估计

Conclusion: 通过几何分解和离散化方法，成功建立了拉普拉斯算子的诺伊曼局部化不等式，为谱间隙的定量分析提供了新工具

Abstract: We prove a Neumann localization inequality for the Laplacian that includes a spectral gap. This result is obtained by partitioning a cube into overlapping families of subcubes and analysing the associated projection operators. The resulting operator inequality goes through a discrete Neumann Laplacian on the lattice of boxes and yields a quantitative spectral gap estimate.

</details>


### [214] [Scattering for anisotropic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21113)
*Evgeny Korotyaev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究非椭圆算子H₀与各向异性势V的散射问题，证明波算子的存在性与完备性，谱性质分析，以及时间相关势的应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非椭圆算子与各向异性势的散射理论，扩展传统椭圆算子散射理论到更一般的算子类别，处理谱分析中的特殊挑战

Method: 在H₀和V满足特定条件下，证明波算子的存在性和完备性，分析H的谱结构，包括奇异连续谱的缺失和特征值的积累性质

Result: 证明了波算子存在且完备，H没有奇异连续谱，特征值只能向0积累；对更强的V条件，H只有有限个特征值；结果应用于不变性原理和时间相关势

Conclusion: 建立了非椭圆算子与各向异性势散射理论的基本框架，为更一般的散射问题提供了理论工具，并展示了在不变性原理和时间相关势中的应用价值

Abstract: We consider the scattering for the operator $H=H_o+V$, where the unperturbed operator $H_o$ is not assumed to be elliptic and the potential $V$ is anisotropic. Under some conditions on $H_o$ and $V$ we show that the wave operators for $H_o, H$ exist and are complete, $H$ has no singular continuous spectrum and the eigenvalues of $H$ can accumulate only to zero. For stronger conditions on $V$ the operator $H$ has finite number of eigenvalues only. Moreover, these results are applied to the invariance principle and for time-dependent potentials.

</details>


### [215] [The non-uniform electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21200)
*Mihaly A. Csirik,Andre Laestadius*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为量子非均匀电子气提出了基于大正则Levy-Lieb泛函的严格定义，并建立了热力学极限框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Lewin、Lieb和Seiringer近期工作的启发，旨在为量子非均匀电子气提供严格的数学定义和理论基础，超越传统密度泛函理论中仅作为理论工具的角色。

Method: 使用大正则Levy-Lieb泛函（量子情形）和大正则严格相关电子泛函（经典情形）来定义非均匀电子气，通过任意晶格周期背景密度引入非均匀性，并建立严格的热力学极限。

Result: 成功建立了量子非均匀电子气的严格定义，证明了其作为热力学极限的存在性，并分析了该系统的基本性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为非均匀电子气提供了坚实的数学基础，为密度泛函理论中的梯度近似提供了更严格的理论支撑，并扩展了多体量子力学和量子化学的研究框架。

Abstract: The non-uniform (or inhomogeneous) electron gas has received much attention in many-body quantum mechanics and quantum chemistry in the early days of density functional theory, mainly as a theoretical device to construct gradient approximations via linear response theory. In this article, motivated by the recent works of Lewin, Lieb and Seiringer, we propose a definition of the quantum (resp. classical) non-uniform electron gas through the use of the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb functional (resp. the grand-canonical strictly correlated electrons functional), establish these systems as rigorous thermodynamic limits and analyze their basic properties. The non-uniformity of the gas comes from an arbitrary lattice-periodic background density.

</details>


### [216] [Limit shapes and harmonic tricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21255)
*Nikolai Kuchumov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述二聚体模型中的切平面方法，并将其推广到多连通区域，首次给出带孔Aztec钻石冻结边界的椭圆函数参数化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文有两个主要动机：1）为二聚体模型中的切平面方法提供自包含的阐述，该方法由Kenyon和Prause于2020年提出，用于分析北极曲线和极限形状；2）将该方法扩展到多连通区域，填补了多连通区域北极曲线参数化研究的空白

Method: 采用切平面方法分析二聚体模型，通过非平凡计算处理多连通区域，具体针对带孔的Aztec钻石进行冻结边界计算，使用椭圆函数进行参数化表示

Result: 首次获得了带孔Aztec钻石冻结边界的显式参数化，该参数化以椭圆函数形式表示，并依赖于孔的高度变化；同时推导并可视化了相应的极限高度函数

Conclusion: 成功将切平面方法扩展到多连通区域，为多连通区域北极曲线研究提供了首个显式参数化，推动了二聚体模型在复杂几何结构中的应用

Abstract: This article has two main goals. First, it provides a self-contained exposition of the tangent plane method for the dimer model - a technique for analyzing arctic curves and limit shapes introduced by R. Kenyon and I. Prause (2020). Second, it extends this method to multiply connected domains through a nontrivial computation of the frozen boundary for the Aztec diamond with a hole. This computation yields the first explicit parametrization in terms of elliptic functions of a family of arctic curves of a multiply-connected region indexed by the height change (hole height). We also derive and visualize the corresponding limit height functions.

</details>


### [217] [Homogenization of point interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21400)
*Domenico Cafiero,Michele Correggi,Davide Fermi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子粒子与大量点状奇势相互作用的均质化极限，证明当势点数量趋于无穷时，系统收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非相对论量子粒子与大量奇异零程势相互作用的均质化问题，探索当势点密度趋于无穷时，微观离散结构如何演化为宏观连续势场

Method: 采用Γ-收敛方法分析相关二次型，证明在奇势强度与点间距同时趋于零的均质化极限下，算子族强收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子

Result: 证明了当奇势具有负散射长度且均匀分布时，算子族强收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子；在外部囚禁势存在时，收敛提升为一致收敛

Conclusion: 大量点状奇势的均质化极限产生规则的静电势，为离散量子系统的宏观连续描述提供了严格的数学基础

Abstract: We consider a non-relativistic quantum particle in $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d=2$ or $d = 3$, interacting with singular zero-range potentials concentrated on a large collection of points. We analyze the homogenization regime where the intensities of the singular potentials and the distances between the points simultaneously go to zero as their number grows, while the total interaction strength remains finite. Assuming that the singular potentials have negative scattering lengths and are uniformly distributed, we prove the strong resolvent convergence as $N \to \infty$ of the family of operators to a Schrödinger operator with a regular electrostatic potential. The result is obtained via $Γ$-converge of the associated quadratic forms. Moreover, in presence of an external trapping potential, the convergence is lifted to uniform resolvent sense.

</details>


### [218] [Dimensional analysis with constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21527)
*Umpei Miyamoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于线性代数的量纲分析框架，用于处理带约束的系统，通过将对数变量中的量纲关系和约束转化为线性结构，提供系统化的无量纲量计数和消元方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量纲分析在处理变量众多或存在隐式约束关系时，直接消元法不切实际，需要更系统化的数学框架来简化分析过程

Method: 将对数变量中的量纲关系和约束表达为线性结构，建立线性代数框架，通过纯代数程序消除冗余无量纲量，无需试错

Result: 该方法能简单计算独立无量纲量的数量，特别适用于具有隐式或多重约束的系统，并以经典阻力问题为例进行了说明

Conclusion: 提出的线性代数框架为带约束系统的量纲分析提供了系统化、高效的数学工具，显著简化了复杂系统的无量纲分析过程

Abstract: We develop a linear-algebraic framework for dimensional analysis in systems with constraints, particularly when variables are numerous or related by implicit relations so that direct elimination is impractical. By expressing both dimensional relations and constraints in logarithmic variables, the problem is reduced to a linear structure. This formulation yields a simple count of independent dimensionless quantities and, more importantly, a purely algebraic procedure to eliminate redundant ones without trial and error. The method is especially effective for systems with implicit or multiple constraints, and is illustrated with the classical drag force problem.

</details>


### [219] [Character Formulas for Kirillov-Reshetikhin Modules via Folding of Supercharacters of $\mathfrak{gl}(M|N)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08791)
*Zengo Tsuboi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了量子仿射正交超对称代数与扭曲量子仿射超代数的超特征分解公式，通过超对称Schur函数的Cauchy型恒等式，将特征分解为有限型量子子超代数的超特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子仿射超代数的特征分解问题，特别是为Kirillov-Reshetikhin模块提供显式特征公式，验证从Bethe ansatz分析中提出的猜想。

Method: 采用折叠（约化）方法，将有限维一般线性李超代数gl(M|N)的超特征通过超对称Schur函数的Cauchy型恒等式进行分解，得到量子仿射正交超对称代数和扭曲量子仿射超代数的特征分解公式。

Result: 成功推导出量子仿射正交超对称代数和扭曲量子仿射超代数的超特征分解公式，作为特例给出了量子仿射代数（及其Yangian对应）的Kirillov-Reshetikhin模块的显式特征公式，证明了arXiv:2309.16660中提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 通过折叠方法和超对称Schur函数理论，建立了量子仿射超代数特征分解的统一框架，为相关代数结构的研究提供了新的工具，并验证了Bethe ansatz分析中的理论预测。

Abstract: We derive decomposition formulas for supercharacters of quantum affine ortho-symplectic superalgebras and twisted quantum affine superalgebras into supercharacters of their finite-type quantum sub-superalgebras, by employing Cauchy-type identities for supersymmetric Schur functions. These formulas are obtained via a folding (reduction) procedure applied to the supercharacters of the finite-dimensional general linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{gl}(M|N)$. As a special case, our results provide explicit character formulas for a class of Kirillov--Reshetikhin modules of quantum affine algebras (and their Yangian counterparts), thereby proving a previously proposed conjecture derived from Bethe ansatz analysis (arXiv:2309.16660).

</details>
